# The bettor's decisions

The owner's rules for the Algorithm (the play-money bettor in this folder). Read this before any bettor change.

- A change must not undo a rule here without saying so.
- When a change conflicts with a rule, the conflict goes to the owner. The agent doesn't pick.
- Each rule has a pinning test in `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs`. The test's name starts with its id (`D3 ...`), so a failure points back here.
- Update this file and its test in the same commit as the change.

Started 2026-10-18, after about 20 bettor changes in one day. The owner: "keep it organized with good notes so the bot doesn't go retard later."

## Rules

### D1. Gates shrink stakes, never stop a market
- **Date:** 2026-10-17 (1b461dfa); the research gate's version 2026-10-18.
- **Owner:** "why would it stop betting? it should reset everything on an algorithm change, and it needs practice and data; it's play money; don't chase a perfect record via confidence, improve the choices."
- **Rule:** a failing gate puts its market at the practice stake (`PRACTICE_UNITS`, 0.5u, a LIKE at most) and keeps betting it by its normal rules. An edge is still needed. This covers the line CLV gate, the prop CLV gate, the research gates and the MMA round totals.
- **Code:**
  - `desk.mjs`: `gatedPlacement`, `lineGate`, `propGate`, `practiceGates`
  - `research.mjs`: `researchGateOf`, which `practiceGates` turns into a cap
  - `fights-totals.mjs`: the practice stake until `TOTAL_LIVE`
  - `picks.mjs`: `levelOf`, which reads `b.practice` (a LIKE at most)
- **Never:** a gate that sets units to 0, tracks a row "for the gate's sake", or turns a kind of bet off.

### D2. Every slate is played; no sport is reduced to zero bets by its record
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (a454c624).
- **Owner:** "the idea that the bot is going to stop finding picks because of its historical record is a flaw... it should be using history to make BETTER picks, not stop playing the game altogether."
- **Rule:** each slate (a sport's US Eastern day) plays about `PRACTICE_N` picks: NFL 15, NHL 6, MLB 3, NBA 8.
  - Each game gets its share, at least one (`slateQuota`).
  - Every row that clears the vig bets as before.
  - The best of the rest with an edge before the vig (the corrected chance over the no-vig) bet at 0.5u, a LIKE at most, marked `topN`. One per player, and the caps and gates still apply.
  - The record corrects the numbers by segment (D17). It never reduces the volume.
- **Code:**
  - `desk.mjs`: `PRACTICE_N`, `slateQuota`, `practiceTop`
  - `run.mjs`: `practicePicks`, and `betProps` (`topN`)
- **2026-10-18 audit fix:** `practicePicks` returned nothing whenever the correction had no fit, for example when its fit threw. Now the trusted chance's edges rank the picks then, so the slate is still played.
- **MMA (O2 closed 2026-10-18):** `PRACTICE_N.mma` is 3 a card. Every bout clearing the vig bets as before. Of the rest with a DraftKings price and an edge before the vig (the trusted chance over the no-vig, the same fallback as the props without the correction), each main-card bout plays its side, and the best prelims fill the card to 3. Main card: the card's bouts at its last ESPN start time. Each is a 0.5u practice pick, `topN`, tallied apart. Code: `mma-desk.mjs` `mainCardOf`, `mmaPracticePicks`.

### D3. PRACTICE_SPLIT: practice picks are tallied apart
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (a454c624).
- **Owner:** "not set on practice bets counting in the real record."
- **Rule:** while `PRACTICE_SPLIT` is on, a `topN` pick has its own Practice line. It stays out of the bot's record, its recent form and the CLV gates. It is still graded and learned from like any bet: by the correction, the trust and the calibration.
- **Code:**
  - `desk.mjs`: `PRACTICE_SPLIT`, `inPractice`, `record`, `practiceRecord`, and `propGate`/`lineGate` (`!inPractice`)
  - `picks.mjs`: `recentOf`
  - `model-desk.component.ts`: `allPractice`
  - `state.json`: `practiceSplit`
- **Never:** a practice pick in `record()`, the recent form or a gate window. Audited 2026-10-18: no path bypasses it. A re-priced practice pick is picked again from scratch, and `topN` is set only on rows that don't clear the vig.

### D4. The epoch: trust and gates restart on every major change
- **Date:** 2026-10-11.
- **Owner:** "we should be restarting trust every time we make a major change to the model."
- **Rule:** bump `MODEL_EPOCH` (an ISO time, `leagues.mjs`) to the push time on any change that alters projections, pricing or what is bet, and add a line to its History comment.
  - These count only bets since the epoch: the trust fit (`fitTrust`), the CLV gates (`lineGate`, `propGate`), the research gate and the research level.
  - These read every epoch: the calibration and the correction. Since 2026-10-18 they are row-hungry fits, not judges of the model (940fd86a, a454c624).
- **Code:** `leagues.mjs` (`MODEL_EPOCH`), `desk.mjs` (`sinceEpoch`), `run.mjs` (`refitTrust`, `researchCalls`).
- **Not bumped for:** UI-only, data-plumbing or staking-policy changes (1b461dfa was staking policy).

### D5. A research reason goes into the price, never an automatic pass
- **Date:** 2026-10-17 (e91ebd32), 2026-10-18 (19d41785, 63b33dd1).
- **Owner:** "if the book has a real reason, add it to the calculation, don't auto pass."
- **Rule:** a reason the research finds that an input can carry goes into this run's injury report copy: the player's own status, a teammate's, or a QB named to start. The market is projected and priced again.
  - A reason already on the report is "held": priced as it stands.
  - Both are priced at the market's trust with the lean held at half the GAP (`clearedChanceAt`). An edge is bet. With no edge it is passed with its reason, and either way it leaves the CHECKs.
  - Only a real reason that no input carries (a role alone) is passed outright.
- **Code:** `research.mjs` (`researchInputs`, `researchDecision`), `run.mjs` (`researchCalls`, the `betProps` research block).

### D6. Research found nothing: bet the model's side
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (a71c7704, then b811fff6).
- **Owner:** "when you can't find info like Corum, at a certain point we have to just say we like it."
- **Rule:** a real answer that found nothing (explains false, with sources, not hollow) is verdict `bet` at any confidence, low included. It is priced as in D5. Only a low answer that names a real reason it can't size stays a CHECK.
- **Code:** `research.mjs` `verdictOf`.

### D7. A failed search is re-asked, never stored as a finding
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (a71c7704, 73734875).
- **Rule:** a call whose search failed is hollow. That covers an answer's own words ("hit the search limit"), any `web_search_tool_result` error, and no sources with status unknown. A hollow call gets no verdict, is never kept, and is re-asked ahead of every new CHECK.
- **Code:** `research.mjs` (`searchFailed`, `hollow`, `verdictOf`, `researchChecks` ordering), `check-patterns.mjs` (hollow answers are no evidence).

### D8. Soft terms, self-tuning, never all-or-nothing
- **Date:** 2026-10-14 (264738d3, b890dbc0).
- **Owner:** "even if the factors are minor... we should still be able to modify it to like 0.0007 or whatever."
- **Rule:** a small real effect stays in at a small weight, shrunk by its evidence and moved a capped step per refit. On/off exists only as a safety fallback. Related terms are tested jointly, with no double counting against the baseline.
- **Code:**
  - `project.mjs`: the soft late terms
  - `calib.mjs`: `shrinkOf`, `CAL_STEP`
  - `desk.mjs`: `shrunkTrust` (`eff / (eff + TRUST_K)`)
  - `correct.mjs`: ridge `LAMBDAS`
  - `check-learn.mjs`: `loosenings` (weight up to 1, `LOOSEN_STEP` 0.25 a run)
- **2026-10-18 audit fix (the loss review's B12):** the calibration's step was per run, so hourly runs moved it about 0.03 an hour. A spot that lost its gate also dropped to 0 at once. Both now step at most `CAL_STEP` per UTC day, toward the target or toward 0. Labels are fixed as well: a spot shifted only by the pooled rungs says "pooled only".

### D9. The dated AI caps (CAP_WINDOWS) revert on their own on 2026-11-01
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (9d2e4fc6, 5edc4c98).
- **Why:** $233.69 in API credits expires on 2026-11-01. `ai-budget.mjs` `CAP_WINDOWS` is the caps' source of truth.

| Window | Research | News signals | Miss review |
|---|---|---|---|
| 10-18 (UTC day) | $100/day, $100/month | $1/day, $10 for the window (read every run) | $2/day, $40 for the window |
| 10-19 to 10-31 | $8/day, $60/month | $1/day, $10 for the window (read every run) | $2/day, $40 for the window |
| From 11-01 00:00Z (standing) | $4/day, $55/month | $0.15/day, $4/month (once a day) | $1/day, $25/month |

- **Code:** `ai-budget.mjs` (`CAP_WINDOWS`, `scheduledCaps`, `capsFrom`). Repository variables may lower a cap, never raise it.
- **Other caps:** the miss review's backfill has a hard $30 (`MISS_BACKFILL_CAP`). The write-ups are $1.50/day and $25/month, for LOVE/LOCK only (`DAY_CAP`, `MONTH_CAP`, `WRITEUP_LEVELS` lock,high; 2026-10-10/11). Never raise a cap or widen a scope without a measured quote and a yes.

### D10. The miss review explains; it never moves a weight
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (5edc4c98, backfill 36de991f).
- **Rule:** once a UTC day from 12:00, through the Batches API, the review takes each sport's 5 biggest misses plus the 2 worst CLV losers. A broken premise is left out. It tallies their causes.
  - A cause past a shrunk share (25%, 3 reviews or more, variance aside) becomes a recommendation and a refit candidate.
  - A stale-data finding is flagged as a bug.
- **Code:** `miss-review.mjs` (`pickMisses`, `recommendationsOf`), `ai-budget.mjs` `missBudget`.
- **Never:** writes `state.json`, a term or a weight. A recommendation still has to pass the refit's held-out gate, and the champion/challenger gate once it lands.

### D11. The CHECK pattern loop resolves only what research has answered, and keeps research measuring
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (8867ecdd, seeds a07a3885).
- **Rule:** each researched CHECK is grouped by cause and by "feeds saw it". A pattern resolves a new CHECK by rule (cost 0) only at its rate. The rate is `MAX_RATE` 0.9 times the top resolution's share, the support of its graded record and its evidence. So at least about 10% of its CHECKs are still researched, and the share stays measured.
  - A rule's own resolutions are never evidence for the share.
  - A pattern whose record turns falls back to research by itself.
  - A rule-resolved bet goes through the same research gate, research level, prop caps and one-per-player rule as a research bet. It is never a bypass.
- **Code:** `check-patterns.mjs` (`patternBook`, `preResolve`, `MAX_RATE`), `run.mjs` `noteInjuries`.

### D12. One desk run per batch of fixes
- **Date:** 2026-10-18.
- **Owner:** "works really well first, built now second... why are we waiting to fix shit?"
- **Rule:** push each fix as soon as it is green, with no game-time deadlines. Dispatch one desk run after the batch is on master, not one per fix.
- **Where:** process only, in agent briefs (`quality-over-deadline`). There is no code to pin.

### D13. GitHub is the only bettor; never run it locally without --dry
- **Date:** 2026-10-09.
- **Rule:** a local run without `--dry` writes ledgers that collide with GitHub's commits, which happened on 2026-10-09.
- **Code:** since 2026-10-18 `run.mjs` (`liveRunAllowed`) refuses a run that isn't `--dry` unless `GITHUB_ACTIONS` is `true` or `MODEL_DESK_LOCAL=1` is set for a deliberate run (pull first, push right after).
- **Also:** `--dry` spends no Odds API credits unless `--paid`.

### D14. You vs the Bot: same game, same bet
- **Date:** 2026-10-17 (f82f80ee).
- **Rule:** each settled straight bet of yours is set against the bot's own side on that market when you placed it. That side is read from `board-seen.json` rows: the side its board liked better at that price, at its own stake or a forced Kelly stake. "When you placed it" is the row showing then, else the newest within 6 hours, else none.
  - Opposite sides are a duel. The same side is agreed, half a point each.
  - These are never counted: a parlay, a void bet, or a bet with no bot row.
  - Only the settler writes `bot` (`firestore.rules`).
  - The bot's own record stays on the Algorithm tab. The practice split (D3) doesn't change the duel.
- **Code:** `board.mjs` (`botSidesOf`, `botSeenUpdate`), `accounts/settle-lib.mjs` (`botPickAt`, `gradeBot`), `wallet-view.ts` `headsUp`.

### D15. The slot machine is gone for good
- **Date:** 2026-10-17.
- **Rule:** the Book's slot-machine handle and reel spin (pull, pulling, spinning) are deleted. Never bring them back or ask about them again.
- **Code:** `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/`.

### D16. Stale inactives and listings are never read as new
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (f56f86d0, 04c94b45, 27615ed6).
- **Rule:** a game-day inactive list counts only the names new since the team's last game (`freshInactives`). A carried name keeps its injury report status. With no last game read, nothing is taken from the list.
  - A listing dated before the team's last game, plus 4 hours, is taken off the report (`staleReport`). Out and IR listings stay.
  - The posting's size cap is judged on new names (`espn.mjs` `postedMax`).
  - The NBA's starting five gets the same carry-over guard (5106c5fa).
- **Code:** `context.mjs` (`freshInactives`, `staleReport`, `gameRosters`), `espn.mjs` `postedMax`.

### D17. Small counts never read big: line-size trust buckets and stake-Kelly bands
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (940fd86a).
- **Owner:** "make sure small numbers aren't influencing K rating by % or anything crazy."
- **Rule:** a prop's trust is its line bucket's: low (1.5 or under) starts at 0.25, mid (2 to 3.5) at 0.375, high at 0.5. Each rung is pulled toward its sport's and every sport's pooled trust by its own evidence (`TRUST_K` 40), and is held to the stat's own trust.
  - Bands and every Kelly shown use the stake's Kelly (`stakeKelly`, held to -110's scale), never the raw model's.
  - A CHECK is judged by its cleared Kelly, with a bar of +1 (`checkKelly`).
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`lineBucket`, `fitBuckets`, `bucketTrustOf`, `stakeKelly`), `picks.mjs` `kellyOf`, `checks.mjs` `checkKelly`.

### D18. The learned correction: a candidate behind a flag; the props are priced at the trust (the gate's verdict)
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (a454c624; the gate's verdict the same night).
- **Verdict (gate.mjs, D22, 2026-10-18):** the correction loses to the trust pricing it replaced, so `CORR_PRICING` is false. The props are priced at the trust-shrunk chance again (`chanceAt` at the line bucket's trust, D17, with the stake-Kelly bands). Everything else from a454c624 stays: every slate played, the top-N practice picks (ranked by the trusted chance's edge before the vig, D2's fallback), `PRACTICE_SPLIT`. `CORR_ON=1` turns the correction on for one run, as the gate's challenger.
  - a454c624's correction against the trust (the gate agent's run): close log loss worse beyond noise in the NFL, NHL and MLB, results tied.
  - The shrunk correction below, against the trust: NHL close log loss +0.0015 worse (95% +0.0010 to +0.0020) and top picks' CLV -0.024 worse (-0.037 to -0.011); MLB close +0.0004 worse (+0.0002 to +0.0006); NFL tied (close 0.4622 both, results 0.5150 against 0.5157). With the global terms shrunk too (`CORR_GLOBAL_RIDGE=1`): NHL close still +0.0005 worse (+0.0003 to +0.0007), MLB and NFL tied. HOLD both ways.
  - The trust pricing (this version) against origin/master's correction: SHIP. Props close log loss better in the NFL (0.4666 to 0.4622, 95% -0.0086 to -0.0018), NHL (0.6724 to 0.6699, -0.0031 to -0.0018) and MLB (0.6798 to 0.6774, -0.0058 to -0.0002); results log loss tied; MLB top picks' CLV better (+0.013).
  - Out of sample (fit on each sport's earlier 70%, judged on its later 30%, `correctionReport`), log loss of the shrunk correction against a454c624's: NFL 0.4644 against 0.4650 (raw 0.4959, book 0.4651); NHL 0.6683 against 0.6684 (raw 0.6759, book 0.6680); MLB 0.7026 both (raw 0.7110, book 0.6982). It tracks the book, a little better than it in the NFL and no better elsewhere.
- **Rule, while it's on:** one fit covers every graded prop: bets, tracked and practice rows, across epochs and sports. It learns where the model beats the book's no-vig and where it doesn't, by segment (sport, stat, bucket, lean, gap, price, home, timing). It is ridge-shrunk, and its penalty and families are self-tuned on a time split.
  - A segment that loses moves toward the book, or past it.
  - The live calibration sits inside it (it fits the raw chances), never stacked on it. Audited 2026-10-18: `priceProps` passes the raw `pOver` to `correctSides`, and `corrRows` reads `modelRaw`, so there is no double count. Since D33 (2026-10-19) the raw chance is the anchored one (`pA`): the correction, the calibration and the trust all read that one raw.
  - The trust sizes the stake (`trustedUnits`, never under 0.5u). It never decides whether a row plays.
  - Under `CORR_MIN` (120) rows, the old trusted chance is used.
  - **Shrunk by its evidence (the 2026-10-18 audit).** The NFL had tuned to the penalty grid's floor (2). A 4-row segment (rushAtt high under) then kept a third of its own fit, three nested families of the same rows adding up. A dry run priced Christian McCaffrey Under 14.5 carries at ev +0.39. Now:
    - `LAMBDAS` 2000 down to 20, never lower. At 20 a segment keeps about n / (n + 80) of its own fit.
    - A segment under `SEG_MIN` (30) graded rows has no term. It reads its parents' terms.
    - The gap feature is held to `DN_CAP` (2.5 spreads), so it can't extrapolate.
    - The move off the no-vig is capped by the stat's graded rows: `moveCap(n)` = 0.1 + 0.5 · n / (n + 200) logits, about 2.6 points at 4 rows and 11 at 400. In the model's own direction it never goes past the model.
    - The penalty and the family set use the one-standard-error rule toward more shrinkage (`oneSe`): the most shrunk candidate within one SE of the lowest held-out loss, paired row by row.
- **Code:** `correct.mjs` (`LAMBDAS`, `SEG_MIN`, `DN_CAP`, `moveCap`, `capMove`, `oneSe`), `props.mjs` `priceProps`, `run.mjs` `refitCorrection`, `desk.mjs` `trustedUnits`.

### D19. Research earns its level
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (b811fff6).
- **Rule:** research bets, and research-adjusted bets on their CHECK's own market, start at the practice stake (0.5u, a LIKE). They are counted across every sport, since the epoch.
  - The level is earned at 40 graded or more, with the hit rate over its prices' break-even and a mean CLV (v2 `prob`) of 0 or better. Then they stake at their Kelly (`stakeFor`), and LOVE/LOCK are possible.
  - Below that bar they go back to practice, never off.
  - A kind whose own gate is at practice stays at practice.
- **Code:** `research.mjs` (`researchLevelOf`, `researchEarned`, `researchCall`), `run.mjs` (`researchTag`, `betProps`), `picks.mjs` `levelOf`.
- **2026-10-18 audit:** this survived a71c7704 and 19d41785 (both before it), and 940fd86a, a454c624 and 11302242 (after it). Research rows are excluded from `topN`, and the level reads v2 CLV.

### D20. The CHECK rule and the role bets
- **Dates:** 2026-10-12 (7e7dca64), 2026-10-17 (round E), 2026-10-18 (940fd86a, e2d3434a).
- **Owner:** "CHECKs should be for crazy value, not inappropriate math."
- **Rule:**
  - A gap trip is a PASS, kept as a graded shadow.
  - A role or news CHECK must clear +1 on its cleared Kelly.
  - A role cause bets its risky side (0.5u, a LIKE) only once 40 graded CHECKs of that cause pay on the model's side. Those 40 count every graded CHECK, closed ones too (`reopened`). Never under 40 for a cause.
  - check-learn's loosenings apply softly, from 40 decided with the interval's low end 3 points clear of break-even.
- **Code:** `checks.mjs` (`shadowOf`, `CHECK_KELLY`), `props.mjs` (`ROLE_BET`, `ROLE_BET_N`, `roleGateOf`), `check-learn.mjs` (`checkLearning`, `loosenings`, `reopened`).

### D21. Bands, stakes and the book
- **Dates:** 2026-10-09 to 10-13.
- **Rule:**
  - `BOOK = 'draftkings'` only. The owner rejected best-price shopping, and Pinnacle de-vigged is the fair anchor. **Amended by D74 (the owner, 2026-10-20):** DraftKings stays the home book (its line, ESPN's free board, the fair's fallback) and the price bet is the best US book's at that line; Pinnacle stays the fair anchor.
  - LIKE is any edge, LOVE is 2% Kelly, LOCK is 5% plus a 55% chance (9cb65c47). Kept by the owner on 2026-10-19 ("a LOVE at 2.0 is fine"); the caps on them are D36's.
  - Every placed bet is a LIKE or better. No-edge rows are tracked: never staked, never on the record, still learned from (2026-10-13).
  - A flagged pick with an edge bets at the minimum (d9879f0d).
  - Stakes run 0.5 to 3u.
- **Code:** `leagues.mjs` `BOOK`, `picks.mjs` (`BANDS`, `levelOf`), `desk.mjs` (`placement`, `TRACK`).

### D22. Every change faces the champion/challenger gate
- **Date:** 2026-10-18.
- **Owner:** "I fuckin love this."
- **Rule:** a change ships only if it doesn't worsen projection accuracy (recency-weighted, split at the rule eras) and beats or ties the current version against the book. Results go on the dated scorecard.
- **Every bettor change runs it before it ships** (its projections, pricing, stakes or selection): `node libs/ranker/scripts/model/gate.mjs --candidate <ref|worktree|env:NAME=value> [--champion origin/master] [sports] [--refit] [--record]`. A HOLD doesn't ship. A shipped version is recorded with `--record`.
- **Layer a (projections, price-free):** each final's margin and total (normal log score; CRPS reported) and each eligible player game's stat (the version's own count distribution), replayed from the games before it. Weighted by season (halving each season back) and split at the rule eras (`gate-score.mjs` `ERAS`: NFL 2024 kickoff, MLB 2023 pitch clock and shift ban, NBA 2016-17's three-point jump, NHL post-2005 rules). The current season and the two before it inside the era decide. Older seasons are a sanity check at a tenth of their weight: they can flag, never decide.
- **Layer b (every saved price):** the ledgers' graded props and game lines since 2026-10-08, bets and tracked rows, and the game-line backtest. Since D29, also the priced history (`history`): every archived game line since 2020 and prop since 2023, at the open against the close, recency-weighted. Each row is priced again walk-forward by the version: its correction fit only on the slates before the row's day; a version from before `correct.mjs` by the trust's shrink by line size. Measured: log loss on results and against the close's no-vig, each slate's top picks (`PRACTICE_N`) hit rate, CLV (v2) and units at the practice stake, and the edge picks at the stake table.
- **The rule in code:** `gate-score.mjs` `sportVerdict`, `gateVerdict`. SHIP when every sport's deciding layer a isn't worse beyond noise and layer b's log loss, close log loss and top picks' CLV beat or tie. Worse beyond noise means the clustered bootstrap's 95% interval (seeded, by day, slate or game) is wholly worse.
- **Exception (the owner, 2026-10-19, resolving O3):** the close leg alone can't see a bias the Book's close shares: the residual model (D32) corrects the Book's over-shade of about 2 points on overs, which the close carries too, so a measure against the close penalizes the correction by construction, and the trust price it is compared with is itself fit half on the close. When, across the sports, the results log loss and the top picks' CLV each beat beyond noise somewhere and lose nowhere, no projection layer is worse, and the only measure worse beyond noise is the close log loss by at most `CLOSE_LEG_TOL` (0.0005) a row, the owner may ship over the HOLD. It is the owner's call, never the gate's: the verdict stays HOLD and says `OWNER MAY SHIP` (`gate-score.mjs` `ownerException`, `gateVerdict`'s `exception`, `sportVerdict`'s `measures`), and the override is written here with the evidence. Pinned in `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D22 (the tolerance, each condition, and that the exception never turns a HOLD into a SHIP by itself) so it can't widen silently. Used once: D32.
- **Scorecard:** `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/scorecard.json`, an entry per version keyed by `MODEL_EPOCH` and commit, on the frozen benchmark (`gate-frozen.json`, fixed 2026-10-18) and the last 28 days. The Algorithm tab's Scorecard panel draws a point per version (`scorecard.ts`).
- **Leaks:** the weekly self-check's C23 (`leaks.mjs`) replays the priced history by segment (sport, stat, line size, side, team, price, timing; stat with side, stat with line size). A leak loses to the book beyond a 10% false discovery rate (Benjamini-Hochberg), at 40 rows in 8 games or more, with a shrunk loss. Trends are segments worse this week than before. Leaks are ranked by units at risk, carry the miss review's causes, and each is a candidate fix that must pass the gate.
- **Limits:** the props' layer needs the desk's cache (`.cache/model`: players' games and the saved fit; a dry run fills it). Both versions replay under the saved fit's settings unless `--refit`, which a change to the fit's search itself needs (the NFL's is about 27 minutes a version). Live-only code (news, CHECKs, timing, ladder rungs) can't be replayed. The gate says so and reads it as a tie.
- **Code:** `gate.mjs`, `gate-score.mjs`, `leaks.mjs`, `selfcheck.mjs` `c23`, `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/scorecard.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-gate.test.mjs`.

### D23. No sane price has an edge past 12 points or 0.3 SDs over its break-even: such a row is suspect, never bet at size
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (the audit's McCaffrey row, ev +0.39, about 20 points over its break-even); re-expressed 2026-10-19 (the owner: Josh Hokit +205, 5.1 points over the break-even, read LIKE at the practice stake while Amanda Nunes +164, 2.3 points, read LOVE).
- **Rule:** a priced row whose edge over its price's break-even passes `EDGE_SUSPECT` (12 points) or `Z_SUSPECT` (0.3 standard deviations of the outcome at the break-even, sqrt(be(1 - be)): the bar tightens at long prices, about 8.6 points at +1000), either way, is logged loudly (`SUSPECT PRICE`) and marked `suspect`. Its ev is held to `EV_SUSPECT` (0.15) at most, so it can't outrank the rest. It stakes the practice 0.5u and is a LIKE at most. It is still placed if it has an edge (D1: a cap, never an off switch). Read by points and SDs, as D24 reads every gap, never by the return: a long price's return is large at a modest edge. The break-even comes from the chance and the return alone (p / (1 + ev)), so the desk, the board and the page read the same bar.
- **Measured (2026-10-19, every row on the Bot's side with a positive ev in the priced history, the four sports' closes and the props' opens, 16,100):** the rows past 6 points already hit under their chance (48% against 58%), but that is the confidence layer's to shrink (D52), not a broken row; past 12 points or 0.3 SDs there are 6 rows. The old ev bar caught 69 (hit 29.0% against 39.8% priced, -29.7%); the 63 it no longer catches (long prices at 2 to 12 points) went 28.6% against 38.5%, -30.4% flat, about 1.6 standard errors: a long price's chance the Bot overstates, which the bands (D52) and the stakes' blend answer, not the bug bar. Recheck it with the next record.
- **Covers:** props, game lines, MMA moneylines, the board's bands, the replayed record, the page's what-if.
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`EDGE_SUSPECT`, `Z_SUSPECT`, `EV_SUSPECT`, `suspectEdge`, `isSuspect`, `heldEv`, `suspectOf`, `choose`), `props.mjs` `priceProps`, `run.mjs` (`betProps`, the game markets), `mma-desk.mjs` `pickFor`, `picks.mjs` `levelOf`, `board.mjs` `bandOf`, `replay-record.mjs`, `bet-why.ts` (`isSuspect`, `priceAt`), `book-rows.ts` (a file before the cap).

### D24. Model-vs-book gaps are scale-aware, never ratios
- **Date:** 2026-10-18.
- **Owner:** "a guy projected for .6 but the book thinks ~1 is huge %-wise but clearly not as different as projected 8 catches vs 14."
- **Rule:** a gap between the model and the book is read in probability points (the model's chance less the book's no-vig) or in SDs of the count distribution (`lineZ`: the implied mean less the model's, over sqrt(mu + mu²/r)). It is never read as a ratio of the means.
  - The role guard's far line (`ROLE.zHi` 1.15, `zLo` -0.75), the line alarm and C20's bands (`farOf`), and the CHECK diagnosis (`DIAG.MISSING_Z`, `RECENT_Z`, `RECENT_NEAR`, `RATE_FAR`, `NEAR`) are in SDs. The thresholds read as the old ratios did at middle counts (a 4-catch receiver, a 60-yard back).
  - Already scale-aware, left alone: the gap guard and the lean taper (`GAP`, `leanOf`: points), the correction's gap feature (logits and points), the miss review's surprise (`countZ`: SDs of the count), and research's "far" (the CHECKs those guards make).
  - The owner's examples: 0.6 against 1.0 at 0.5 is +0.52 SDs, so it is not a far line (its ratio 1.67 was). It is still past the gap guard at about 18 points, so it is a PASS shadow, not a role CHECK. 8 against 14 at 10.5 is +1.8 SDs at r 20, a far line as before, and about 55 points past the gap.
- **Code:** `props.mjs` (`lineZ`, `ROLE`, `roleChangeOf`, `farOf`, `lineAlarmOf`), `check-learn.mjs` (`DIAG`, `diagnoseProp`, the line loosening), `selfcheck.mjs` `lineBandOf`.

### D25. Research found nothing on a small line: a practice pick unless the corrected chance clears the vig
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (the owner, resolving O1).
- **Rule:** a "found nothing" verdict (D6) on a line of 1.5 or under (the low trust bucket) is priced at its corrected chance where the correction is on, else at the trusted chance (the correction is off since the gate's verdict, D18). Clearing the vig, it is a real research bet. Otherwise it bets the model's side as a 0.5u practice pick (`topN`, a LIKE at most, tallied apart while `PRACTICE_SPLIT`). Larger lines are as D5 and D6.
- **Code:** `research.mjs` `researchDecision` (`low`, `corrected`, as `practice`), `run.mjs` `betProps` (`researchPractice`).

### D26. Users read "the Bot" and "the Book", never "the model" or "DraftKings"
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the owner, twice that day).
- **Owner:** the Book used "model" and "the bot" interchangeably; "the bot" is the standard everywhere users read it. Then: wherever users read "DraftKings", write "the book"; and, to users, the sportsbook IS the Book (they bet with us, we only borrow the lines), so Book and Bot are proper names.
- **Rule:** every word that reaches the page calls the bettor "the Bot" and the sportsbook "the Book": "the Bot's lean", "Bot vs Book", "the Bot's chance", "against the Book's no-vig 50%", "its" where it reads better. Both are capitalized everywhere users read them: "the Bot", "the Book" mid-sentence (the article lowercase), "The Bot", "The Book" at a sentence start. The Book is also the page's name (nav, title, logo chip): one name for both, the page and the sportsbook. That covers the Book, its popovers and About, the Live tab, the Algorithm tab's labels and tooltips, the wallet's You vs Bot, the game view, the text the bettor writes into user-facing fields (picks.json reasons and risks, CHECK reasons and research verdicts, the plain write-ups, the self-check's rows, the board's labels), and the NFL analysis prompts. The Claude write-ups' prompt says so too (`PROMPT_V` 3; older analyses keep their words till rewritten; the plain write-ups all written again, `PLAIN_V` 6), and the write-ups' validator no longer allows "DraftKings" as a name.
  - Kept as names of a technical part, not the Bot itself: "the game model", the fight model, the anytime TD's "plain model" and "red zone's model", the "model epoch", the "Live Model" admin tab, "Model term" (a miss-review cause), "model files", the "raw model" log loss, GitHub's Model Desk workflow, Claude as "an AI model", and nflverse's model. Other sportsbooks stay lowercase common nouns ("a sharp book", "another book's line", "the other books"), and Pinnacle and ESPN BET keep their names (sources, not the Book).
  - Kept as data: the ledger's `book: 'DraftKings'`, `BOOK` in leagues.mjs, the Odds API's bookmaker keys, ESPN's provider name and the NFL dossier's `book` field. Code identifiers, file names, JSON fields (`model`, `modelPct`, data/model) and comments are unchanged.
- **Code:** `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/`, `account/wallet/`, `game-view/`, `writeup.mjs` (`factsOf`: the sheet's book "the Book"; `ALLOW`), `writeups.mjs` `SYSTEM` and `PROMPT_V`, `picks.mjs` `legacyReasonOf`, `mma-desk.mjs`, `checks.mjs`, `desk.mjs` `gapGuard`, `props.mjs`, `research.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `selfcheck.mjs`, `apps/nfl/scripts/analysis/analyze.mjs`; pinned in `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` (every non-comment line of those files read for "DraftKings" outside a data field and for a lowercase "the bot" or "the book").

### D27. Every bet the bot placed is a pick on the Book
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (the owner's bug: WSH @ SF showed one of its four open bets).
- **Rule:** every open bet on a game not started that isn't a tracked row goes in picks.json: a guard's 0.5u cut one (`context.guard`: a moved line, a questionable player, the line gate) and a practice pick (its practice tag) too, and all of them, never cut to `TOP`. `TOP` (40) is only the page's Best Bets count across the sports. Each is shown, then published once locked, so the record by band covers every placed bet.
- **Why:** before 2026-10-13 a guard's cut was an action bet, kept off the Book. Since then a guard's cut with an edge is a real 0.5u bet, but the old filter still hid it, and `TOP` cut a 50-bet NFL Sunday.
- **Code:** `picks.mjs` `buildPicks`, `mma-desk.mjs` `buildFightPicks`; `tests/data.test.mjs` (every open placed bet is in picks.json).
- **Never:** a placed bet left off picks.json, whatever its guard, practice tag or rank.

### D28. Every stat the site has is weighed for the bettor, with no look-ahead
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (the feeds round).
- **Owner:** "the bettor should ABSOLUTELY be knowing about any stats we have including NGS and any analysis I have etc etc etc... bot should be as smart as possible."
- **Rule:** `INPUTS.md` lists every data set the site has against what the bettor reads, each marked in, new, left out (with its held-out gain), in another form, or not useful. A new input:
  - reads only what was published before the bet. A per-game table learns a game after its day. NGS's weekly rows count from their week's last kickoff plus `NGS_PUB_HOURS` (36), and NGS's week-0 season totals are never read. A season-to-date site file is never read for a game in its own season.
  - is a late term on the gate (`project.mjs` `FEEDS`), soft where small (D8), kept on its stat's ctx only with a held-out gain or for the gate to judge again, and runs the replay gate (D22).
  - never comes from free text. An AI analysis's structured claims are tested held out first; the NFL's (2026-10-18: 80 claims, leave-one-out log loss worse) are left out.
- **Code:** `ngs.mjs`, `plays.mjs`, `savant.mjs`, `matchups.mjs` (`shotTable`, `zoneTable`, `contactTable`), `props.mjs` `CTX` and `STATS`, `INPUTS.md`. Tests: `tests/model-ngs.test.mjs`, `tests/model-shots.test.mjs`.

### D29. The priced history: every archived open and close graded in the gate, kept growing by a morning snapshot
- **Date:** 2026-10-18. The owner bought The Odds API's 5M-credit plan for one month to buy the history, then back to the 20K plan.
- **The archive (the docs and probes, 2026-10-18):** game lines (h2h, spreads, totals) from 2020-06-06, snapshots 10 minutes apart, 5 from September 2022; player props from 2023-05-03 (an earlier date is refused). A historical call costs 10 x the markets it returns x the regions; DraftKings and Pinnacle named are one region; an empty answer is free.
- **What was bought** (`odds-history.mjs pull`, local, once): NFL, NBA, NHL, MLB and MMA. Each game's lines at the open (the first daily 10 AM Eastern snapshot that saw it) and the close (a minute before the start). Each game's props, every market the desk prices (`props.mjs` `API_MARKETS`), at the close and at an earlier snapshot (`OPEN_LEAD`: 48 hours for the NFL, 12 for the rest). The API's re-issued event ids are merged by teams and start.
- **Stored:** `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/odds-history/<season>.jsonl.gz`, compact rows (an event matched to its ESPN game with its score; a line; a prop). The unmatched are logged in the pull's cache.
- **The gate (D22) grades it** as layer b's `history`: each version's replayed price (the ratings from the games before each game, the props' replayed projection, the trust fit walk-forward by month) at the open, against the result and the close's no-vig, recency-weighted by `seasonWeight`. It also reports each version against the book at the close (`vsBook`, on the scorecard). The weekly leak hunt (C23) reads the gate's hunt on the same rows (`history-leaks.json`, written with `--record`).
- **The morning snapshot** (the owner, 2026-10-18): `odds-history.mjs morning`, a step of the hourly desk workflow, failing soft. From 10 AM Eastern, once a day a sport: every coming game's lines and the props of the games inside 26 hours (each game's bought once, on its own morning), at DraftKings and Pinnacle. Kind `m` in the same rows, `odds-history/morning/<ET day>.jsonl.gz`, written once a day. Its own budget feature, `snapshot` (`oddsapi.mjs` `SNAPSHOT`: 300 credits a UTC day at most). It is the first cut when credits run low: it stops while 2,000 more than the reserve and history's share is left.
- **Code:** `odds-history.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs`, `gate.mjs` (`scoreVersion`, `compareScores`, `recordScore`), `gate-score.mjs` (row weights), `leaks.mjs` `historyLeakBets`, `selfcheck.mjs` `c23`, `oddsapi.mjs` (`SNAPSHOT`, `spendable`).
- **Limits:** the props are graded where the desk's cache has the players' games (the NFL from 2016; the NBA, NHL and MLB the last two seasons). Older seasons' lines are graded by the ratings alone, with no context. The fitted settings are the version's own (fit over their window), the small look-ahead layer a has too. MMA's history is matched to the fights archive by the fighters' names and the day since 2026-10-19 (`fights-history.mjs`, D36: 98.1% of the bouts priced to the bell).

### D30. Every bet is judged at the price offered the moment it's placed
- **Date:** 2026-10-18.
- **Owner:** "every bet is judged at the price offered at the moment it's placed."
- **Rule:** a bet is placed only at a price quoted in the current run: the live board, or a fresh Odds API call this run.
  - The odds history and the morning snapshot (D29) are for history, learning and the gate only. Nothing that prices or places a bet reads them.
  - A CHECK's stored price is an earlier run's, so it is never bet (`STORED_FRESH` 0, was 6 hours). Its CHECK is still evaluated, kept or closed. A row about to be bet at a stored price owes its game a fresh ask (once per stored price, `PROP_ASKS` at most), and bets on that ask's run at the price then. With no key, a stored price is no fresher.
- **Code:** `checks.mjs` (`STORED_FRESH`, `storedFresh`, `storedBet`), `run.mjs` `betProps` (`stale-owed`).
- **Never:** a bet's `odds` or `placedAt` price from a stored, snapshot or historical price.

### D31. No silent skip: a line move the projection followed is priced, and every skip says why on the board
- **Date:** 2026-10-18 (the owner's Rachaad White question: Under 47.5 Rush Yds, WSH @ SF, K +1.05%, `sk` with no `sw`).
- **What happened:** his line opened at 20.5 with Croskey-Merritt and McNichols healthy and moved to 47.5 with both out on the report. His projection already had them out (47.15). The 23:05Z run was before the depth chart's QB change (8867ecdd), so he was a qb-change role row, and the line-move skip (twice `limit`) stripped the role's kind (the "also" rule): a skip with no kind, no shadow, no reason on the page. On the current code (Kaliakmanis Out by the depth chart: qb1-out, whose safe side is the under) the line-move guard alone skipped it.
- **Rule:**
  - A line move the projection followed is the inputs' own reason, priced (D5). `moveFollowed`: the line now within `MOVE_FOLLOWED` (0.5) of the open's distance from the model's mean, both in SDs of the count (`lineZ`, D24). Such a row is flagged (the guard's half stake), never skipped by the move, and the move no longer strips a role, news or gap CHECK of its kind. A move the projection didn't follow still skips, as kind `moved`.
  - Every skip carries its reason to the board: `sr`, the guard's own words (`skipReason`, at most `SR_MAX` 200 characters; a guard with none says so). A prop's on the row, a gapped game market's on each side. The page shows it on the PASS tag's hover, the price's hover and the K score's popover.
- **Code:** `props.mjs` (`MOVE_FOLLOWED`, `moveFollowed`, `movedSkip` in `priceProps`), `board.mjs` (`skipReason`, `sr`), `lines.service.ts`, `game-lines.component.ts` (`passReason`, `passTag`, `PriceWhy.reason`), `book-page.component.ts` `priceWhy`.

### D32. The residual model: where the Book is wrong, on since the owner's O3 (2026-10-19)
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (built in the morning as a candidate; the owner turned it on the same day).
- **Owner:** "learn where the Book is wrong" (the splash round-2 brief's item 7), with a condition: anything that references the Book must never become a weight that pulls the Bot toward the Book if the Bot gets ahead.
- **What it is:** the deep dive on the priced history (2026-10-19) found only 10 to 25% of the Bot's disagreement with the Book is signal, and that most of what is recoverable at the close is the Book's own over-shade. The residual model (`residual.mjs`) learns which disagreements are real: logit p = logit f + g(x), f the Book's no-vig at the bet's moment, g a ridge logistic regression on the Bot's lean d = logit(raw) − logit(f) and its interactions (the stat, the line's size, the timing, the season's phase, the move to date, the Bot's recent per-stat accuracy) and on the Book's own shade (by stat, home, price band, vig). A boosted-trees stage was measured and left out (NFL close 0.6913 against 0.6879 without: it overfits a signal worth about 0.001 a row).
- **Rule:**
  - It predicts the Book's error, never the Bot's. A Bot whose disagreements have been right shows as a larger learned edge (the lean terms grow, the corrected chance lands past the current price toward the Bot); a Bot whose disagreements are noise reads "no edge" (the corrected chance clears neither of the Book's prices; 74 to 97% of rows by sport). Pinned in `tests/model-residual.test.mjs`.
  - No look-ahead: monthly snapshots (`fitWalk`), each fit only on rows settled (`SETTLE_MS` 12 hours) before its month; a row is priced only by a snapshot whose `to` (the newest moment it learned from) is at or before the bet's moment (`snapshotFor`); the running reads are frozen the same way; the move to date is read only at the close. A planted leak test pins it.
  - Soft (D8): the desk prices the blend of the current price and the corrected chance on the logit scale (`blendChance`); the weight starts at 0 (the current price), moves at most `BLEND_STEP` (0.25) a refit toward the weight with the lowest loss on the newest 12 held-out months (each row priced walk-forward), per sport and kind. Never an on/off switch.
  - The guard (`guardOk`): a refit whose corrected chance is worse than the Book by `GUARD_TOL` (0.0015) a row on its newest 3 months, or worse than the shipped file's on most shared months and in total, is held at the current price for that kind (its live blend 0); the file is refused when every kind is.
  - **On: `RESID_PRICING` true** (since 2026-10-19, the owner's override below); `RESID_OFF=1` prices one run without it (the gate's champion), `RESID_ON=1` one run with it whatever the flag. The hook is `desk.mjs chanceAt`'s fifth argument (the row as ctx), and every pricing path passes it: the live props (`props.mjs priceProps`: the side's fair chance, its raw the anchored chance `pA`, the Book's two prices, the start, the bet's moment now; only where the Book priced the prop, an assumed -110 being no price of the Book's to correct; the trusted chance it blended from kept on the row as `pTrust` with `resid: true`), the live game lines (`desk.mjs choose` and the board's sides, `boardMarkets`, through `sideCtx` from the game's ctx `run.mjs betGames` builds), the gate's history replay (`gate-history.mjs finish`) and its ledger replay (`gate.mjs pricePropsWalk`). A moneyline the spread didn't anchor stays at the Book's chance; a prop the correction (D18, off) prices skips the blend.
  - **The order, and no double count.** A prop's chance is built once, each step reading one input and never its own output: the bot's own number -> the anchor (D33, `pA`: the raw the residual's lean `d` reads, the same raw the history fit it on) -> the live calibration (`calSides`) -> the trust (`chanceAt`'s `fair + trust · lean`: the current price) -> the residual blend (`blendChance` of that price and the corrected chance). The trust fit (`fitTrust`, four-argument `chanceAt`: no ctx) and the calibration (`modelRaw`) never read the blended chance; the weekly refit's collector prices its "current price" with the blend off in its own process (`residual-train.mjs` sets `RESID_OFF`), so the blend's weight is fit against the price it sits on, never on top of itself. The correction and the residual are never both on (`CORR_PRICING` false). Pinned in `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D32: the live chance equals the hook's blend of the four-argument chance, a different raw moves it, a different calibrated chance moves it only through the current price, `RESID_OFF=1` gives the current price back.
  - Retrained by `residual-train.mjs --collect --write` (the weekly self-check's workflow runs it, failing soft, and commits the files), the history replayed as the gate replays it.
- **Measured (walk-forward, every row priced by the snapshot before it; log loss on the results, lower better):** at the close, NFL props 0.6879 (blend) against the current 0.6887 and the Book 0.6887, better in every season 2023 to 2026; NBA props 0.6873 against 0.6876 and 0.6877; NHL props 0.6742 against 0.6742 (tie, blend 0.1); MLB props 0.6688 against 0.6687 and 0.6689 (tie, blend 0). At the open the same order: NFL 0.6893 against 0.6901 and 0.6902; NBA 0.6877 against 0.6885 and 0.6887. Against the Book at the close (`vsBook`, recency-weighted), the gap turns in our favor for the first time: NFL −0.0007 [−0.0012, −0.0002], NBA −0.0009 [−0.0012, −0.0006]; NHL and MLB tie.
- **The owner's override (2026-10-19 morning, resolving O3): on.** "Whatever gets us closer to winning more." The gate's HOLD stood on the close leg alone (NFL +.0005, NBA +.0001, MLB's ledger props +.0001, intervals of no width) while the results log loss beat the Book and the current price (NBA beyond noise, the rest tied or better), the top picks' CLV beat beyond noise (NBA +0.010, NHL +0.0075), and the live top picks' hit went NFL 41.5% to 60.0% and NHL 46.9% to 57.6%. The residual model corrects the Book's about 2-point over-shade on overs, which the close keeps, so the close leg penalizes the correction by construction. The owner took the results, CLV and hit evidence over the close leg; D22 now carries the exception as a rule (the close leg alone, within `CLOSE_LEG_TOL` 0.0005, with results and CLV better beyond noise), pinned so it can't widen. The option taken is (a) of O3 for this change only: D22's measures are unchanged, the exception is documented, and a HOLD still says HOLD. `MODEL_EPOCH` bumped (D4): the trust and the gates restart on the new pricing.
- **The gate on the on-state version (D22, `--candidate worktree --champion origin/master --record`, 2026-10-19 afternoon, the saved fits; the scorecard entries keyed by the epoch 2026-10-19T15:05Z): HOLD, recorded with the override.** Layer a exact ties everywhere. The priced history (every open since 2020 and 2023, recency-weighted): results log loss NFL 0.6885 both (tie), NBA 0.6850 to 0.6844 (−0.0006 [−0.0009, −0.0004], better), NHL +0.0001 tie, MLB 0.0000 tie; the close leg NFL +0.0004 [+0.0003, +0.0004] worse, NBA +0.0001 [+0.0001, +0.0002] worse, NHL and MLB tie; the top picks' CLV NBA +0.0092 [+0.0030, +0.0155] better, NHL +0.0067 [+0.0039, +0.0094] better, NFL −0.0030 tie, **MLB −0.0021 [−0.0043, −0.0001] worse**; the top picks' hit NBA +2.9 points better, MLB +5.0 better [+2.6, +7.6], the units at the practice stake NFL −81.8u to −69.5u, NBA −121.2u to −98.2u, NHL −60.1u to −56.0u, MLB −115.5u to −88.8u. The ledger's own props: the NFL's top picks hit 49.1% to 60.0%, its edge units −8.3u to −5.8u a slate (+2.45 [+0.20, +6.02], better); MLB's 152-row close leg +0.0001 worse. Against the Book at the history's closes (`vsBook`): NFL props 0.6885 to the Book's 0.6894 (−0.0008 [−0.0015, −0.0002], better), NBA 0.6874 to 0.6878 (better), MLB 0.6691 to 0.6692 (better), NHL tie; the lines NFL +0.0009 tie, NHL +0.0010 [+0.0003, +0.0018] worse, NBA and MLB tie. The leak hunt: NFL 0 and MLB 0 leaks as before; **NHL 2 (spreads losing 0.11 nats per 100 rows, dogs 0.04) and NBA 1 (totals, 0.04)**, where master had none: the lines part of the model, whose blend ran 0.25 to 1 on the NHL's and NBA's lines through 2024 and 2025 before falling back toward 0 (NHL lines 0.25, NBA 0 now).
  - **Props only (the owner's O4, 2026-10-19 afternoon; the standing rule "whatever wins more").** The blend prices the props and never a game line: `residual.mjs` `RESID_KINDS` { props: true, lines: false }, read by `hookChance` before anything else, so the moneylines, spreads and totals price as before the model (the trust's chance alone, `desk.mjs chanceAt` with the ctx declined) on the live desk, the board and the gate's replay alike. Why: the lines part put NHL spreads and dogs and NBA totals on the leak list and read +0.0010 worse against the Book at the NHL's closes, and the forum replay showed the Bot's game-line bets losing −11.9% flat at the close. MLB's props stay on (its hit +5.0 points and units +27u better beyond noise); its CLV leg (−0.0021 against the close) is watched on the scorecard: each entry's `historyPicks` (`gate.mjs scorecardOf`) carries the priced history's top picks' CLV, hit and units at the practice stake, the leg the gate decides on, so a record of a later version shows whether MLB's leg tied. The lines part is still fit and reported by `residual-train.mjs` so it can be judged again; it prices nothing until `RESID_KINDS.lines` says so, through the gate. Pinned in `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D32 (a game line's ctx gives the hook null and `chanceAt` the trust's chance, on the shipped NHL file too) and `tests/model-residual.test.mjs`. `MODEL_EPOCH` bumped (D4).
  - **What the exception covers and what it doesn't.** NFL, NBA and NHL are inside D22's exception (the close leg alone, within 0.0005; results and CLV better beyond noise in NBA, CLV in NHL). MLB is not: its history CLV leg is worse beyond noise, by a hair, while its results tie the Book, its hit and units are better beyond noise and its close-leg gap is 0. The owner's instruction was the model on; it is on for every sport, and MLB's CLV leg and the lines' leaks are **O4** below. `ownerException` reads the four sports together as not eligible for that one leg, which is right: the exception stays narrow, the override is the owner's.
- **The gate (D22) on `env:RESID_ON=1` against origin/master, 2026-10-19 morning (the candidate's run): HOLD, on the close leg alone.** Layer a tied everywhere (the projections are untouched). Layer b's history (every open since 2020 and 2023): results log loss NFL −0.0007 [−0.0014, 0.0000] tie, NBA −0.0006 [−0.0009, −0.0004] better, NHL +0.0001 tie, MLB 0.0000 tie; the top picks' CLV NBA +0.0100 [+0.0035, +0.0162] better, NHL +0.0075 [+0.0048, +0.0099] better, NFL and MLB tie; the top picks' hit NBA +2.7 points better, MLB +4.3 better. The ledger's own props (since 10-08): the NFL's top picks hit 41.5% to 60.0% (better) and its edge units +2.0u a slate (better), the NHL's hit 46.9% to 57.6% (better). Against the Book at the close (`vsBook`), the gap turns in our favor for the first time: NFL props −0.0010 [−0.0018, −0.0004] better, NBA −0.0004 [−0.0006, −0.0001] better, MLB −0.0001 better, NHL tie; the NHL's lines +0.0010 worse (its lines' blend reached 0.25 on the open rows' lean; the deep dive's look-ahead caveat on the open replay applies). The one leg that holds it: the "close" log loss, NFL +0.0005 [+0.0004, +0.0005], NBA +0.0001 [+0.0001, +0.0002], MLB's ledger props +0.0001 [+0.0001, +0.0002], intervals of no width: the model corrects the Book's over-shade (about −2 points on overs), which the Book's close carries too, so a measure against the close penalizes the correction by construction, and the current trust price is itself fit half on the close (`fitTrust`). NHL: BEATS. That conflict is **O3** for the owner. Until it's resolved the flag stays off: the code, the files and the tests ship as a candidate (as D18's correction did), recorded on the scorecard as the flag-off version (a tie with master).
- **Loops:** what could undo it, and how each is bounded.
  - The weekly refit (`residual-train.mjs --collect --write`, `model-desk-weekly.yml`): refits the snapshots on the priced history (never the ledger: the live bets' blended prices teach nothing back), prices its current price with the blend off, and ships a file only past the hold-out guard (`guardOk`: a kind worse than the Book by `GUARD_TOL` on its newest 3 months, or worse than the shipped file's on most shared months and in total, is held at the current price; the file is refused when every kind is). The blend's weight moves at most `BLEND_STEP` (0.25) a refit. The desk reads the file and never fits it.
  - The trust fit (`fitTrust`, `fitBuckets`): reads the bets' anchored, calibrated chance against the Book and the result, never the blended price; it sizes the stake and the current price the blend starts from. Since the epoch it restarts (D4), so the first weeks' trust is the start value, and the blend sits on that.
  - The live calibration (`calib.mjs`): fits on `modelRaw` (the anchored chance), never on the blended price; its shift enters the current price, upstream of the blend, once.
  - The anchor (D33): the raw the residual reads. Its pull is refit only by `gate.mjs --record` on the history's raw against the Book, never on the blended price.
  - The learned correction (D18, off): when it prices a row the residual doesn't (the row's `corr` chance stands); never both.
  - The gate (D22): the candidate's history replay prices with the residual, the champion's without, so the next change is judged on the blended price; the residual file itself changes only through the weekly guard, never a gate record.
  - CHECKs, research, the practice picks: read the priced chance `p` as before (the edge before the vig ranks the practice picks); the gap guard reads the anchored model chance, upstream.
- **Code:** `residual.mjs` (the model, `RESID_PRICING`), `residual-train.mjs` (the collector with `RESID_OFF`, the CLI, the report, the guard's file), `desk.mjs` `chanceAt` (the ctx hook), `sideCtx`, `choose`, `board.mjs` `boardMarkets`, `run.mjs` `betGames` (the game's ctx), `props.mjs` `priceProps` (the prop's ctx, `pTrust`), `gate-history.mjs` `finish` and `gate.mjs` `pricePropsWalk` (the ctx), `gate-score.mjs` `ownerException` (D22's exception), `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/residual.json` (the snapshots, the held-out record, the top terms), `.github/workflows/model-desk-weekly.yml` (the retrain). Tests: `tests/model-residual.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D22 and D32.
### D33. The anchored projection: the bot's mean pulled toward the book's implied mean by a weight the evidence sets, 0 the bot's own number
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the sharpen round's diagnosis on the priced history; the owner approved the anchor with one hard condition).
- **Owner:** "I don't want it to become a weight that pulls us TO the book if we ever get ahead."
- **Why:** on every DraftKings close since 2023 (39k NFL, 73k NBA, 49k NHL, 149k MLB prop rows) the bot's own number trails the book by .007 to .018 in log loss, and the gap is broad, not a corner: only 10% to 25% of the bot's disagreement with the book is real (the slope of the result on it), the rest is noise carried at full weight. The spread around the projection is about right, and the bot's average level is as good as the book's (actual over the bot's mean 1.00 to 1.03; the book shades overs). The bot is wrong row by row, where the book's close knows roles, injuries, lineups and leashes the bot reads late or never. Far over the book the bot has been about 0% right, far under about 40%.
- **Rule:** a priced prop's chance comes from the bot's mean pulled toward the mean the board's line and price imply (`anchoredMean`: mu x (im / mu)^pull, in log space), the stat's own shape at that mean, no recalibration. The pull is per stat and per side (pull +- d over and under the book), fit on the priced history by held-out log loss against the close, walk-forward by season, and **shrunk toward 0 by its evidence, never toward the book**: with no evidence the chance is the bot's own, under `ANCHOR_MIN` (300) decided rows it is 0, and a stat where the bot's own number beats the book's implied mean out of sample sits at exactly 0. No floor, no cap short of 1. The trust (D17) prices the anchored chance against the book as before and may rise all the way to 1 (`fitTrust`'s grid reaches it); the live calibration shifts the anchored chance where its rows say so. The bot's own chance stays beside it on every row (`modelPure`, `projection.anchored`), and the scorecard's history table shows all three: the bot's own number, the anchored chance, the book.
  - The pull is fit by the gate (`gate-history.mjs` `propHistoryRows`, each season's rows at the fit on the seasons before it) and written by `gate.mjs --record` to `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/anchor.json`, with each stat's pull on the scorecard entry (`anchor`) and the Scorecard panel. The desk reads the file (`run.mjs` `readAnchor`) and never fits it. `ANCHOR_OFF=1` prices every stat at the bot's own number (the gate's flag candidate).
  - Measured 2026-10-19 (walk-forward by season, the first season's pull 0, recency-weighted, log loss at the close against the book's): NFL props, the bot's own number +.0168, the current pricing -.0001, the anchored chance alone -.0005 (+.021 in the unanchored first season, -.0009 and -.0016 in the two newest), anchored then trusted -.0011; NBA has one season of props, so nothing is anchored yet; NHL and MLB ties. The shrink's `ANCHOR_K` is 300 rows' worth: at 1500 a stat's first season kept under half its fit and the priced chance gave back a third of the gain (-.0007); from 0 to 500 it is -.0011 either way. The pull runs about 0.7 to 0.9 on the NFL's yards and catches, 0.4 to 0.6 on its attempts and completions, about 0.9 on NHL points and MLB hits, 0.7 to 0.8 on NHL shots and MLB strikeouts, outs and total bases.
  - **The gate (D22, `--refit` for the NFL, 2026-10-19): NFL SHIP and BEATS** (the priced chance against the Book at the history's closes .6886 to the Book's .6892, 95% -.0010 to -.0003, the first version under the Book; projections exact ties), **NHL and NBA ties, MLB HOLD** (its open rows +0.0002 a row worse beyond noise over 149k, the close a tie). So the anchor prices the NFL, the NBA and the NHL (`ANCHOR_SPORTS`, `anchorOn`) and not MLB, whose pull is fit and recorded for the readout only. MLB's anchor turns on only through a gate's SHIP.
- **This is a pricing change, not the bot's own number getting sharper.** The owner's north star stays the raw column: the anchor closes the gap by borrowing the book's information; the pull by stat is the honest measure of how far the bot's own numbers still have to come, and it falls on its own as they do.
- **Code:** `anchor.mjs` (`anchoredMean`, `anchoredChances`, `pullOf`, `fitStatAnchor`, `fitAnchor`, `readAnchor`, `ANCHOR_K`, `ANCHOR_MIN`, `PULL_GRID`), `gate-history.mjs` (`propHistoryRows`, `finish`, `vsBook`'s `anch`), `gate.mjs` (`scoreVersion`'s `anchor`, `recordScore`, `scorecardOf`), `props.mjs` `priceProps`, `run.mjs` `refitCalib` (reads it), `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/scorecard.ts` `anchorText`. Tests: `tests/model-anchor.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** what could undo it, and how each is bounded.
  - The trust fit (`fitTrust`, `fitBuckets`): reads the anchored chance as the model's; it can only move toward the book (shrink) or toward 1 by evidence since the epoch. It cannot raise the pull. With the anchor doing the shrinking its fit climbs toward 1 (the history's walk-forward trust reaches 0.9 to 1 after the first season).
  - The live calibration (`calib.mjs`): fits a logit shift per spot on `modelRaw`, now the anchored chance; gated on held-out gain, capped at `CAL_CAP`, stepped `CAL_STEP` a day. It can't stack a bias the anchor removed: a shift wins its gate only where the anchored rows still lean. Same raw either side, so nothing is counted twice.
  - The learned correction (`correct.mjs`, off): would fit on the anchored chance too.
  - The refits (`fitStat`): fit the bot's own projection and never read the pull; the recalibration `cal` stays on the bot's own chance (the pages), the anchored chance reads none.
  - The pull itself: refit only by `gate.mjs --record` on the whole priced history (tens of thousands of rows, walk-forward), never by the desk's hourly loops on the ledger's few hundred rows (`run.mjs` has no `fitAnchor`).
  - CHECKs and research: the gap guard reads the anchored chance, so far fewer gap CHECKs trip (the market is already read); role and news CHECKs are unchanged.
- **Never:** a prior or floor that assumes the book is right; the desk fitting the pull; a bet priced from the history's stored line (D30: the anchor reads the current run's price alone).
- **Measured and held the same round (the season-start vacancy):** the deep dive found week-1 backs at the vacated cap (Najee Harris projected 89 on a 23.5 line): the NFL's vacated work counts every last-season regular not in a game's logs as missing, departures included. The fix (a carried regular missing only on the report or the reserve list, `matchups.mjs`) tied the book at the close (props -0.0003, inside the noise) but made the price-free projections worse beyond noise with `--refit`: +0.0064 a row over 31,776 deciding player games (95% +0.0031 to +0.0095). A departed regular's work is real signal for the role players the book never prices; the week-1 overshoot is the cap and the newcomer the present pool doesn't hold till he plays, not the departure. HOLD, nothing changed. Done right as D35.

### D34. The game model's schedule terms are soft: in at their own ridge, never gated one by one, from the schedule alone
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the misses round: the owner's "work backwards from the Book's known misses on game results").
- **What was done:** every game with a close in the odds history (2020 on: NFL 1,737, NBA 7,765, NHL 8,074, MLB 15,137) joined to every pre-game fact the schedule, the lines and the facts cache give (`scratchpad/misses/situations.mjs`), each tagged with when it would have been known; the close's residual by feature level and pairs, shrunk (DerSimonian-Laird with a floor), BH q-values, recency weights and sign stability by season; the Book's biggest misses and what is over-represented among them (`analyze.mjs`); an AI pass (Opus 5.5, the Batches API) as the quant (`FINDINGS.md`).
- **What the close gets wrong (the real part):** little. The close is within a point of calibrated everywhere. The patterns that held across seasons: the NHL's back-to-back side wins about 2.8 points less than the close says (8 of 8 seasons; 4.4 when the other side is rested), a rested NHL side (2 games or fewer in 7 nights) about 3 points more; late home starts in the NHL (21:00 ET on) the home side about 3 points less; the NBA's totals run about a point over at altitude and a point under when a side comes off a blowout win. Everything else at q under 0.2 was a referee, an umpire, a one-season level or a market-structure effect (the totals' skew by line level, the favorite-longshot bias).
- **Rule:** a schedule situation enters the game model as a **soft** context term (`context.mjs` `SCHEDULE_TERMS`, `soft: true`): the time zones a side sits from its home clock and has crossed since its last game (`tzHome`, `tzMove`), the start by its own clock (`clockLate`, `clockEarly`), the last result (`letdown`, `bounce`, `letdownT`), the week's density (`density7`), the long road trip (`tripLong`), the home stand's opener (`firstHome`), altitude on the total (`altitudeT`) and the lopsided matchup on the total (`lopsidedT`, the NBA and NFL: a dynamic term, the model's own expected margin either way over the usual gap, set as the game is predicted (`ratings.mjs` `setDynamic`); the NBA's lopsided games ran a point over the close and its coin flips a point under, 7 of 7 seasons).
  - **Never gated one by one.** `ratings.mjs` `fitContext` keeps every soft term out of `selectTerms`. The set comes in together at one ridge of its own, `lambdaSoft`, the fit's own lambda times 1 to 1024, the multiple whose held-out games were best; so a term's size is `n / (n + lambdaSoft)` of its least-squares fit, and a nothing sits near 0. The main lambda is fit with the soft set out, so it can't tilt it.
  - **The only switch is the safety fallback (D8):** the whole set stays out when every ridge is worse than none by more than 1 nat over the held-out games (`lambdaSoft` null). Measured 2026-10-19: no sport fell back; the NHL and MLB took 16x the fit's lambda (sizes under 0.02 goals or runs), the NBA and NFL 1x.
  - **From the schedule alone.** Every soft term reads the games list, the places and their clocks (`zoneOf`, `clockAt`): no box score, report or line, so a coming game has them exactly as a final does. A term that needs a day-of fact goes in as a gated term, as before.
  - **Bounded against the loops.** The refit fits them every run from the history (the Refits loop); the trust sizes the stake on the game lines they move; the calibration, the correction and check-learn read props only; the miss review never writes a weight (D10); the self-check reports. None of them can turn a soft term off or move its size: only the ridge does, and the gate (D22) judges the whole set.
- **The gate (D22, `--refit`, against origin/master 6e96c007):** see the scorecard entry of 2026-10-19 and FINDINGS.md.
- **Code:** `context.mjs` (`SCHEDULE_TERMS`, `BLOWOUT`, `zoneOf`, `daylightAt`, `clockAt`, `featurize`), `ratings.mjs` (`fitContext` `softGates`, `lambdaSoft`; `online`), `run.mjs` `contextState` (`soft`, `lambdaSoft`). Tests: `tests/model-context.test.mjs` (the clocks, the terms from a made-up schedule, the fit's soft handling), `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D34.
- **Held, not built (the analysis's other reads):** the totals' skew by line level (the NHL's high totals run 0.2 goals under the market's own mean, its low ones over: a shape for `margins.mjs`, not a situation); the NHL's favorite-longshot bias (favorites of .75 or more +5 points: a pricing read, the trust's); DraftKings against Pinnacle at the close (look-ahead at bet time); the referees and umpires (one or two seasons each, n under 100).

### D35. The season-start vacancy: a departed regular's work vacates net of the work brought in, the newcomer in the pool
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the sharpen round, continuing D33's note).
- **What was wrong:** the NFL's vacated work (`matchups.mjs` `vacated`) counted every last-season regular not in a game's logs as missing, at half his work, and shared it among the players the team's own work table held. A newcomer (a back signed from another team, a rookie) isn't in that table till he plays, so in week 1 a departed starter's work went whole to the returning role players, at the cap (3: Najee Harris 89 on a 23.5 line; week-1 backs 2 to 4x the line). Dropping departures outright (D33's note) threw away the role players' signal: +0.0064 a row worse.
- **Rule:** a regular gone since last season (`cur` false) still vacates his work, **netted against the work the team brought in** at his position group: each arrival's incoming work (`incoming`: his recent work on his last team, at half across a season; a rookie's or an unknown's `NEW_MIN` 0.5), faded game by game as the departed's is (`arr`, `WORK_RATE`), and a newcomer present tonight with no work for this team yet in the pool at that work (`present`: the game's players in the history; at bet time the roster by position where he's played somewhere within a season or has a prop on the game's board, `props.mjs` `extra.present`, `extra.board`). So a team that lost two backs and signed one vacates the difference, shared in proportion among the ones here, the newcomer among them; one that lost a back and signed no one vacates his work in full, as before; one that signed a like-for-like replacement vacates nothing. This season's absences are untouched. No roster read live: as before (the `noRoster` guard already flags it).
- **Gate (D22, `--refit` for the NFL, 2026-10-19): SHIP, ties everywhere, nothing worse.** The price-free projections 3.6245 to 3.6232 a row over the 31,776 deciding player games (-0.0013, 95% -0.0032 to +0.0003: inside the noise, where D33's plain fix was +0.0064 worse beyond it), the older seasons -0.0001; the ledger's top picks hit 45.5% to 49.1% and CLV +0.066 to +0.069 (inside the noise); the priced history ties to the fourth place (33,184 rows); against the Book at the history's closes .6886 to the Book's .6892 as before (95% -.0010 to -.0003). The fit's pull toward the Book rose on carries (0.68 to 0.73) and rush+rec yards (0.69 to 0.73), the stats the vacancy moves most: the netted work changes the Bot's number where the Book already knew the role, and the anchor reads that; the rest unchanged. The games' layer exact ties (the change is the props').
- **Code:** `matchups.mjs` (`NEW_MIN`, `lastWork`, `incoming`, `vacated`'s `present`, `arr` on the work's entries), `props.mjs` `priceProps` (`present`, `board`). Test: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D35.
- **Loops:** the refit (`fitStat`, `vc` with `vs`) reads the vacs as given and fits their strength: it can shrink `vc` where the netted work is noise, never widen the pool. The learned split (`absorbOf`, off: `TRY_VL`) learns fresh absences only (`cur`), so departures and arrivals never enter its table. The CHECK learning's `vacated-fade` diagnosis reads the same vacs. `lastWork` is the history's own, rebuilt each run from the logs: nothing is stored or stepped between runs.
- **Never:** a departed regular vacating nothing (the role players' signal); an arrival counted twice (his incoming work nets once, as `arr` on his entry or as a newcomer in the pool, never both: an entry exists only once he's played for the team).

### D36. MMA's priced history: matched, in the gate, and the trust fit from it (the practice cap lifts only where the history earned it)
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the MMA history round).
- **Owner:** "Do we not have historical bet data on MMA? That's arguably more valuable than other sports."
- **What was wrong:** the archive (`apps/mma/scripts/model/odds-history`, D29: about 600 bouts a year since 2020-06, the moneyline and the round total at DraftKings and Pinnacle, the open and the close) was bought and never read: it keeps a bout by its fighters' names with no ESPN id, `gate.mjs` `SPORTS` left MMA out, so MMA had no scorecard, no leak hunt, no gate, and the live desk treated it as a brand-new market (the untested start of 0.5, a unit at most, the round totals tracked till 40 graded) with six seasons of priced bouts sitting there.
- **The match (`fights-history.mjs`):** each event joined to the fights archive by both fighters' names and the day (accents, case, spacing, punctuation and a Jr./Junior suffix dropped; one exact and the other at least by surname and first initial; a reordered, joined, longer or married name; a surname a letter apart; a looser pair only where it is the day's sole candidate; two listings of one bout go to the one priced to the bell), each match carrying `crossed` where the API's first side is ESPN's second fighter. **3,121 of 3,935 events (79.3%); 2,080 of the 2,120 priced to the bell (a close inside 6 hours: the bouts that happened), 98.1%.** The rest are listings for bouts moved, pulled or never made (the biggest days 2020-11-29 with 14, 2021-02-20 with 13, 2021-01-17 with 11) and about 40 the API named wrong (King Green as "Amanda Bobby Cooper", Carlos Felipe as "Antonio Carlos Junior", Semelsberger as "Matthew Lopez").
- **The gate (`gate-mma.mjs`, `gate.mjs` `scoreVersion`'s MMA branch):** layer a, price-free, every bout's moneyline and its round totals at every line its rounds carry, each from a fit on the bouts before its half-year (the rating as of the bout's date already), 2018 on, weighted by year (the current year and the two before decide); layer b, the priced history's opens and closes framed on ESPN's first fighter and Over, Pinnacle's pair the fair chance where it posts the line, the trust per market walk-forward by month (`gate-history.mjs` `finish`); the desk's own graded bets at the version's chance and the history's trust as of their month. Pinned in `tests/model-mma-history.test.mjs` (no look-ahead by a planted flip of later results; the rows' framing straight and crossed; the closed listing). MMA is in the weekly self-check's sports (C23 reads `history-leaks.json`) and the frozen benchmark.
- **What the history says (the first entry, 2026-10-19, the same model code either side: ties, SHIP):** at the close the Bot's own number trails the Book far: moneylines 0.6214 to the Book's 0.5804 on 1,968 bouts, round totals 0.6827 to 0.6585 on 916; the slope of the result on the Bot's lean against the close is 0.010 (nothing). At the open (a week or more out) the Bot's own number beats the opening moneyline (0.6362 to 0.6478) and trails on totals (0.6689 to 0.6527), so what the model knows the Book has by the bell. The Bot's chance is calibrated by decile at the close (the 0.1 to 0.2 bucket lands 0.159, the 0.7 to 0.8 bucket 0.748); the Book shades the longshots (0.171 and 0.238 where 0.000 and 0.159 land), the favorite-longshot bias the taper keeps the desk from betting into. The top picks at the open (3 a card by edge) hit 42.8%, CLV -0.049, -38.9u at the practice stake: the market moves against the Bot's leans. The history's trust, fit on every open and close row recency-weighted: **moneylines 0.05, round totals 0** (on the opens alone 0.15, on the closes alone 0).
- **Rule:**
  - The live desk's trust (`mma-desk.mjs` `fitMmaTrust`) is fit on the live bets since the epoch **pooled with the history's rows** (`history-rows.json`: every open and close the fight model priced walk-forward, as the trust fit reads a bet), the history's rows recency-weighted by season and capped at `PRIOR_ROWS` (400) rows' worth, the live rows whole. So the trust is fitted from the first run (no "40 graded" wait where the history has them), the practice cap on an untested market lifts only where the history says the model earned it (a trust over 0: an edge at the trusted chance), and stays where it hasn't (trust 0: the Book's chance, no edge bet; the card's practice picks alone, at the practice stake, by the Bot's own edge before the vig). Free to rise: the fit's grid reaches 1 and the live rows weigh whole against a capped prior, so a season of live bets can move it. The round totals' "graded with a close" count toward `TOTAL_LIVE` reads the history's too; its rungs by the line's size fit on the history's rows. Gates shrink stakes, never stop a market (D1): a trust of 0 tracks and practices, it never turns MMA off.
  - A tracked row with no return on either side is the model's side (`pickFor`), never the cheaper vig's (the loss review's B10), so the trust and CLV learn from the Bot's own leans.
  - The files are the gate's (`gate.mjs --record` writes `trust-prior.json` and `history-rows.json`); the desk reads them and never writes them; without them the fit is the live bets' alone, as before. Pinned in `tests/model-mma-trust.test.mjs` (fitted from the first run; a noise history keeps the trust at 0 and the pick tracked; an informative one lifts it and the pick is an edge bet; the live rows move it; rows before the epoch never count; the desk writes neither file).
  - `MMA_START` bumped to the push (the league's own epoch; `MODEL_EPOCH` is the team sports' and the line pool's, untouched): the price moved from the untested start to the history's fit, so the league's bets restart on it (D4).
- **The fight model through the gate (the same round; the brief's order: the first-listed bias, the rating's decay, the totals' base by weight class, the raw hunt's finds). Every candidate held, with numbers** (each a flag in `fights-model.mjs` / `fights-totals.mjs`, off; the gate `--candidate env:<flag>` against origin/master, MMA):
  - The first-listed bias (REVIEW H11, `FIGHT_CORNER=1`: a term of 1 on every row): there is none in the walk-forward chances. 2018 on, 6,078 fit rows: ESPN's first fighter wins 0.4666, the model's mean for him 0.4691; where he's the model's dog 0.3562 against 0.3592, the favorite 0.6195 against 0.6212; on the history's 1,968 closes 0.450 against 0.455. The term fit at nothing, the gate a tie (+0.0003). The review's +3.9 points was a full fit's read on its own sample. Also audited: the archive's first fighter is ESPN's competitors[0] while the live desk sorts by ESPN's `order` (8 of 13 recent bouts the same, 5 swapped), so a first-listed term could never carry from the archive to the live card anyway.
  - Recency in the fit (REVIEW H21, the owner's rule; `FIGHT_HL`, `TOTAL_HL` in years): the rows weighted by age starve the soft rule (each term's weight is shrunk by its two half-fits' z, and the weights cut the halves' effective rows, zeroing terms). Half-life 2 years: the bouts' log loss +0.0277 a row worse (0.6241 to 0.6518), the totals' +0.0285; 4 years +0.0052 (a tie) and +0.0065 (worse); 1 year the coin (0.6931). HOLD. The rating alone's walk-forward log loss by half-year 2018 to 2026 runs 0.63 to 0.70 with no trend (2024H1 0.633, 2026H1 0.656; the review's .634 to .675 was its six halves' ends), so the rating's decay isn't the leak.
  - The rating's deviation terms (H21, `FIGHT_RD=1`: the gap in how sure the rating is of each, the rating by how unsure it is of both): the deciding years +0.0030 (a tie), the older +0.0053 worse beyond noise, the closes' raw 0.6231 against 0.6214. Nothing to ship.
  - The totals' base by weight class (REVIEW H10, `TOTAL_CLASS=1`: each class's ridge-shrunk offset on the line's base, `CLASS_K` 30 bouts): the totals' log loss -0.0016 a row in the deciding years (95% -0.0056 to +0.0026, a tie), the history's top picks' CLV better beyond noise (+0.053), the closes' raw 0.6811 against 0.6827; but the desk's own 4 graded round totals worse (+0.001 beyond their tiny noise): a HOLD by the rule, to run again once the ledger has a few cards of totals. With a 2-year half-life beside it +0.0204 worse.
