  - The raw hunt (the gate's priced hunt can't see the raw model: at a trust of 0 the priced chance is the Book's; `scratchpad/mma-gate/raw-hunt.mjs`): the Bot's number is calibrated by its own decile at the close (0.1-0.2 lands 0.159, 0.4-0.5 lands 0.466, 0.7-0.8 lands 0.748) and loses to the Book everywhere by the Book's information, not a corner: by the Book's decile the Bot is compressed (the Book's 0.1-0.2 lands 0.098 where the Bot says 0.274; its 0.7-0.8 lands 0.803 where the Bot says 0.629), the slope of the result on the Bot's lean against the close 0.010. The worst segments: a lean of 0.15 or more (+0.101 nats a row, n 511: the taper keeps the desk out of them), the Book's favorites of 0.75 or more (+0.081, n 398: the Bot under-rates big favorites), catchweights (+0.169, n 40), a big-promotion debut on the second side (+0.103, n 98: the Bot's debut prior is too kind), the other fighter at home (+0.065, n 237), women's bouts (+0.042); nothing the Bot beats the Book on beyond noise (main events -0.007, five-rounders -0.001, a rating gap over 1 -0.004). Round totals: the 1.5 line (+0.027, n 303: the Bot says 0.596 go past it where 0.498 do; the Book posts 1.5 where it expects a quick finish), 2026 (+0.038: the overs ran 0.537 where the Bot said 0.590), featherweights (+0.051). The Bot's own number beats the opening moneyline (0.6362 to 0.6478): the model's information is real a week out and priced in by the bell.
- **Live effect (2026-10-19):** the moneyline trust 0 at 400 rows' worth (0.05 on the whole history), the round totals 0: every MMA side is priced at the Book's chance, no edge bet clears, the card's three practice picks (and every main-card bout with a lean) play at 0.5u, a LIKE. The practice cap stays until the live bets, pooled with the history, say otherwise.
- **Code:** `fights-history.mjs`, `gate-mma.mjs`, `gate.mjs` (`SPORTS`, `scoreVersion`, `projRows`, `compareScores`, `tableOf`, `recordScore`, `scorecardOf`), `gate-history.mjs` `finish` (exported), `selfcheck.mjs` (the sports), `gate-frozen.json` (mma), `mma-desk.mjs` (`PRIOR_ROWS`, `readHistoryRows`, `fitMmaTrust`, `pickFor`, `mmaPracticePicks`, `stateOf`'s `historyTrust`), `apps/mma/src/StaticData/model/{scorecard,trust-prior,history-leaks}.json`, `apps/mma/scripts/model/history-rows.json`. Tests: `tests/model-mma-history.test.mjs`, `tests/model-mma-trust.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** what could undo it, and how each is bounded.
  - The trust fit (`fitMmaTrust`, every run): reads the history's rows as given and the live bets since the epoch; the history's weight is capped, the live rows' whole, the grid reaches 1 either way. It can't refit the history's chances (the gate's record does, from the archive and a version of the model, D22).
  - The gate's record (`--record`, a shipped version): rewrites `history-rows.json` with that version's walk-forward chances, so the desk's prior is always the shipped model's own history. Never the ledger's rows: the live bets stay the live evidence.
  - The MMA model's refit (every run, `fights-model.mjs` soft terms): changes the live chance, never the history's rows; a change big enough to move the price goes through the gate and a record.
  - The practice picks (`mmaPracticePicks`): read the Bot's own edge before the vig, not the trusted chance, so a trust of 0 never empties a card (D2); they're tallied apart (`PRACTICE_SPLIT`).
  - The weekly self-check (C23): reports the history's leaks, never changes the bettor.
- **Never:** the desk fitting or writing the history's rows or the prior; a history row priced by a fit that saw it; the history as a price the desk bets at (D30).

### D37. MMA bets early: the trust by lead band, the early line untapered, a four-week window
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the open round, the coordinator's follow-up on D36: the owner's rule, the Bot picks its own time to bet).
- **What the history says (D36's instrument by the row's lead, `gate-mma.mjs` `leadBand`):** the archive's opens are the API's first daily sighting of a bout, a day to a month out. On 3,052 open moneylines: **two weeks or more out** the Bot's own number beats the opening line, 0.6389 to the Book's 0.6797, the trust fit 0.25 tapered and 0.75 untapered, the top picks' CLV +0.080 tapered and +0.288 untapered with 58.6% beating the close; the lean's size runs with the open-to-close move (correlation 0.62); of the 1,250 early rows 406 sit past the gap guard (0.15), where the Bot's side wins 55.7% and its CLV is +0.455. **2 to 14 days out** the Bot ties or trails the line (trust 0, the top picks' CLV -0.10 to -0.13, 0 to 18% beating the close). **Inside 48 hours** the Book is far ahead (0.6715 to 0.5856), the close sharper still (D36). Round totals: no edge at any lead (early 0.6671 to 0.654, the trust 0 tapered and 0.2 untapered, a tie).
- **Rule:**
  - Every priced row and every live bet has a lead band: `early` two weeks or more before the start (`LEAD_BANDS.early` 336 hours), `mid` 48 hours to two weeks, `late` inside 48 hours (the close among them). The trust is fit a market **a band** (`trustPrior` and `finishByLead` walk-forward by month on the band's rows before; the desk's `fitMmaTrust` pools the history's band rows with the live bets of the same band, each live bet banded by its own lead, start less `placedAt`), and a bout is priced at the trust of its lead at the moment of the ask (`trustFor`, `fittedFor`), the board's sides the same.
  - **The early band is priced untapered and never gapped:** its pricing key is the market's name with `Open` (`priceKey`: `mlOpen`, `totalOpen`), a key `desk.mjs` `GAP` has no limit for, so `chanceAt` takes the lean whole, `gapGuard` skips nothing and `fitTrust` fits on the whole lean. The mid and late bands keep the market's own key (the taper to half the gap, the skip past it). D23's sanity guard stays: an early line with a return past `EV_SUSPECT` is flagged and staked at the practice stake.
  - **The window is four weeks** (`leagues.mjs` `TIMING.mma` lines 672 hours, was 72) and the desk reads ESPN's cards four weeks ahead (`mma-desk.mjs` `AHEAD_DAYS` 28, was 5), so the first ask on a bout lands in the early band where the books' opening line is soft. A bout ESPN lists that the API doesn't carry yet, two weeks or more out, rests a day between asks (`FAR_H`, `REST_FAR`), not three hours: the ask is for the whole board, and the credits stay about a card's worth a week.
  - `MMA_START` bumped to the push: a pricing change, the league's bets restart on it (D4).
- **Gate (D22, `--candidate worktree --champion env:MMA_LEAD_BANDS=0` (the harness's old pricing: one trust a market fit on the closes), MMA, 2026-10-19): SHIP, BEATS.** The priced history (every open since 2020, recency-weighted): the results log loss 0.6591 to 0.6498 (-0.0093, 95% -0.0140 to -0.0050, better), against the close 0.6619 to 0.6578 (-0.0041, better), the top picks' CLV -0.0825 to +0.1788 (+0.261, 95% +0.143 to +0.416, better), their hit 42.8% to 47.3%, their units at the practice stake -38.9u to +72.7u; layer a exact ties (the model is unchanged). Against the Book by band at the open: moneylines early raw 0.6452 / priced 0.6481 / Book 0.708 (-0.060, 95% -0.088 to -0.027, better), mid tie at the Book (0.6384), late tie at the Book (0.5799); round totals ties in every band. The scorecard carries the by-band table and the beat-close readout (`beatClose`).
- **What the live desk does now:** a bout two weeks or more out is priced at the early trust (0.75 on the moneyline, untapered: the Bot's own number near whole) and bet where it clears the vig, at the Kelly stake (D23's guard at a return past 0.15); the same bout inside two weeks is priced at the mid trust (0, the Book's chance: no edge bet, the card's practice picks alone) and inside 48 hours at the late (0). A bet placed early keeps its price; the close is read for its CLV as before. The card of 10-24 is five days out as this lands (mid: no edge bets); the cards of 10-31 and 11-07 are in the early band.
- **Superseded measure (2026-10-20, D56):** the early band's edge above was mostly the archive's mirrored opens (one open in seven on the other fighter). On the repaired archive the Bot's own number trails the Book's at every lead (two weeks or more out raw 0.6296 against 0.6042) and the early moneyline's trust fits 0.1, not 0.85. The rule (the bands, the early key, the window) stands; the fit decides.
- **Caveat the owner should know:** the archive's "open" is DraftKings' first daily sighting; the books' earliest MMA lines are soft and their limits small, so the paper edge here is largest where real money is hardest to get down. The desk is play money (D13, D27); the live early bets' CLV, pooled with the history's by band, will say whether the lines the desk actually asks for at four weeks out carry the same edge.
- **Code:** `gate-mma.mjs` (`LEAD_BANDS`, `BANDS`, `leadBand`, `priceKey`, `finishByLead`, `trustBets` and `packRows` with the lead, `trustPrior` by band, `beatClose`, `MMA_LEAD_BANDS=0` the harness's champion), `gate.mjs` (the by-band table and beat-close in the report and the scorecard), `mma-desk.mjs` (`fitMmaTrust` by band, `trustFor`, `fittedFor`, `pickFor`'s and the board's `key`, `AHEAD_DAYS`, `FAR_H`, `REST_FAR`, `MMA_START`), `fights-totals.mjs` `totalPickFor`'s `key`, `leagues.mjs` `TIMING.mma`. Tests: `tests/model-mma-history.test.mjs` (the bands, the key, the lead packed), `tests/model-mma-trust.test.mjs` (a fit a band; the early key untapered and never skipped, the market's own skipped; live early bets move the early band alone), `tests/model-mma.test.mjs` (the window).
- **Loops:** as D36's, by band: the desk's fit reads each band's history rows and the live bets of that band, never writes the rows; the gate's record rewrites the rows with the shipped model's walk-forward chances and leads; the practice picks still read the Bot's own edge; the self-check reports. The far rest bounds the asks. Nothing moves a band's trust but its own evidence.
- **Never:** the early band's untapered pricing applied inside two weeks (the key is the band's, not the market's); a history row without its lead read as early (an unknown lead is late); the window widened further without the gate.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-19: the anchor on MLB's starting pitchers alone (strikeouts and outs)
- **The ask:** MLB's pitcher strikeouts and outs are a broken corner (the deep dive: 11% of MLB's prop rows and half its gap to the Book, the slope of the result on the Bot's lean about 0: the Book knows the leash). MLB's anchor was held sport-wide (D33), its pull on `k` and `outs` fit at 0.96; the candidate priced those two stats at the anchored chance and left the batters at the Bot's own number (`anchorOn(sport, stat)`).
- **Gate (D22, MLB, the saved fit): SHIP as a tie in every layer, and a no-op in the price.** On the history's 7,754 strikeout and 6,471 outs rows the priced chance is the same to the fourth place either way (strikeouts .6875 / .6875, the Book .6875; outs .6848 / .6848, the Book .6848): the walk-forward trust already prices both at the Book, because the anchored chance still trails it. Anchored at the Book's own mean with a pull of 0.96, outs score .6946 against the Book's .6848 and strikeouts .6954 against .6875 (the Bot's own .7267 and .7028). Nothing shipped: a pricing change that moves no price isn't one.
- **Why the anchor can't fix pitchers: the shape, not the mean.** On outs the Bot's over chance averages .439 where overs land .504 (the Book .506); at the Book's mean it only climbs to .472. The rows the Bot reads lowest (over chance .15 to .34) go over 49% to 53% of the time. The negative binomial (r 150, rk 1) is right-skewed, an outs count is left-skewed (a hook ends the start early: the median sits above the mean), so the shape's chance over the line runs 3 to 7 points low at any mean. Strikeouts the same, smaller (.476 against .489). Hits and total bases are calibrated within a point.
- **Next (the deep dive's J, then E):** a left-skewed outs shape (a short-hook mixture with the hook's chance from the knockout rate, the opponent, the park and September), strikeouts through the batters faced it implies, the recalibration fit at priced lines; then the anchor on both again, and the outs line as the strikeouts' leash. Each through the gate with `--refit` for MLB.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-19: a history-fitted trust prior for the game lines and totals
- **The ask:** fit the lines' and totals' trust prior on the priced history, walk-forward, letting a totals trust sit at 0 where the history says so, free to rise where the Bot is ahead.
- **Measured** (the gate's own instrument, `gate-history.mjs` `lineHistoryRows`: each market's trust fit monthly on the closes before, from 0.5; log loss at the close, lower better; the slope of the result on the Bot's lean in logits, under 0 anti-informative):

| Sport | Market | Walk-forward trust (newest seasons) | Priced / Book, newest season | Slope by season |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | spread | 0 since 2022 | .6882 / .6882 (2026) | -.05 to +.09 |
| NFL | total | .19 to .25 | .6953 / .6945 (2026, 92 rows); .6935 / .6937 (2025) | -.29 (2026), +.11 (2025), -.11 to +.02 before |
| NFL | ml | 0 since 2021 | .6641 / .6641 | -.10 to +.13 |
| NBA | spread, total, ml | 0 to .08 | ties to the fourth place | -.07 to +.03 |
| NHL | spread, ml | 0 since 2022 | ties | -.17 to +.13 |
| NHL | total | .29 to .40 | .6890 / .6882 (2026), .6960 / .6927 (2027, 134 rows) | +.05 to +.09 (2021 to 2025), -.05 (2026), -.54 (2027) |
| MLB | spread, ml | .05 to .17 | ties | -.03 to +.05 |
| MLB | total | 0 since 2022 | .6926 / .6926 | -.03 to +.02 |

- **Verdict: HOLD, nothing to ship.** The live trust already sits where the history puts it: every sport's `state.trust` has spreads, totals and moneylines at 0 to 0.15 (fit on the backtest's Pinnacle closes and the live bets since the epoch, D17's grid reaching 1 either way), and the priced chance ties the Book at the close in every market but NHL totals, where the walk-forward trust of 0.3 is worth +0.0001 in the seasons it was earned and costs 0.0008 to 0.0033 in 2026-27 as the lean turned against it. A history prior would move the first months of 2020-21 (the sanity set) and nothing the desk prices now. The totals lean is noise at the close, as the deep dive said; the fit already reads it so.

### D46. A suggestion never shows a band the Bot couldn't give the row; the bands stay
- **Date:** 2026-10-19.
- **Owner:** on a Book suggestion, Alexander Volkov ML at −120 (the Bot 55.8%, break-even 54.5%) shown as a LOVE: "Why is this a LOVE? The edge seems super small." Then: "a LOVE at 2.0 is fine."
- **Rule:**
  - The bands stay as D21 has them (`BANDS`: LOVE at a 2% Kelly score, LOCK at 5% with a 55% chance, LIKE any edge). No floors on the edge in points.
  - A side on the board (board.json) carries the desk's cap: `cap: 'medium'` (a LIKE at most, whatever its price) and `cr` its why, wherever a bet on it would be capped by `levelOf`: the lines' CLV gate or a stat's at the practice stake, a role bet's side, MMA's round totals under `TOTAL_LIVE`, an MMA side whose lean is under the league's edge (a card's practice pick at most). `picks.mjs capOf` is `levelOf`'s cap: for every edge bet with an edge, `levelOf` is `capBand(edgeLevel, capOf)`.
  - A price the desk wouldn't bet at all (ESPN's line or ESPN BET's where the desk bets only at the Book's own price: tracked only) is a PASS on the board, with its reason (`sr`, D31). So it is never a suggestion.
  - The board's own band `b` is held to the cap and to a suspect price's LIKE (D23). The page holds every band it works again to the same: the Book's suggestions (`suggestions.ts repriced`, a LIKE at the practice stake at most), the game view's tiles at a live price (`game-lines.component.ts call`) and the K popover's what-if (`bet-why.ts whatIfOf`: `most`, a ceiling, a PASS still at a price with no edge).
  - The K popover says the edge in points plainly at its price (`bet-why.ts edgeLine`): "Edge +1.3 pts: the Bot 55.8% vs 54.5% to break even at −120 · Kelly 2.6% of bankroll". The Kelly score's -110 scale makes a 1.3-point edge a 2.6% Kelly, which clears LOVE at 2%.
- **Volkov's row (dry run, 2026-10-19):** the fight model 59.9% (the rating alone 67.0%; Volkov 27-11, Kuniev 4-1 on ESPN), the Book's no-vig 52.2% (ESPN BET's −120 / +100), the lean 7.7 points (just past half the ml GAP, tapered to 7.3), the trust 0.5 (untested: no graded MMA moneylines since the start), blended 55.8%, break-even 54.5%, the edge 1.3 points, Kelly 2.64%. The chance is sane: half the model's lean, as the untested trust says. It was a LOVE by the bands; it is a PASS now, since its price is ESPN BET's, which the desk only tracks.
- **Code:** `picks.mjs` (`capOf`, `capBand`), `board.mjs` (`bandOf`, `sideCap`, `boardMarkets` gate and off, `boardProp` gate), `run.mjs` (the board's gate and off), `mma-desk.mjs` (`boardSidesOf`, `boardTotalOf`, `buildMmaBoard` edge and totalsLive), `suggestions.ts`, `bet-why.ts` (`whatIfOf`, `priceAt`, `edgeLine`), `game-lines.component.ts`, `book-page.component.ts`.
- **Never:** a suggestion, a tile or a what-if above the band the desk would give that side at that price.
### D41. Usage first, rate second: the work a player is expected to get is a first-class number on every row
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the usage round; the owner's "what's the next step to improving accuracy?").
- **Why:** the Bot's own number trails the Book at the close (NFL props .7064 against .6892, NBA .7061 / .6878, NHL .6819 / .6734, MLB .6759 / .6692), and 75 to 90% of its disagreement is noise concentrated where the close knows roles, injuries, lineups and leashes (D33). The projection already projects usage before the rate in most places (the NFL's snap share and targets, the NBA's minutes, the NHL's time on ice, MLB's lineup slot and leash), each fit held out and walk-forward inside `project.mjs fitStat`, but as parts scattered over the projection, the matchups and the row, never one number that can be read against the posted lineup or the depth chart.
- **Rule:** every priced prop carries one usage object (`usage.mjs usageOf`): `kind` (snaps, targets, starts, minutes, toi, pa, outs; games where the base isn't the usage one), `unit`, `expected` (the usage the projection reads: the NFL's snap share and targets, the NBA's minutes, the NHL's minutes on ice and power-play time, MLB's plate appearances at his lineup slot, a starter's expected outs from the outs model, on his strikeouts row too), `typical`, `recent`, `last`, and the sport's own parts; `source` says which base made it. It rides on the projection (`projection.usage`), the ledger's bet and picks.json (`usage`), so the popover can show it. It is read off the fitted base, never fit or stepped on its own: the price and the readout can't disagree, and nothing is priced off it that wasn't priced already.
  - **Unified, not duplicated.** The volume base, the vacated work, the absorb table and the netted vacancy (D35) stay where they are and feed the usage; the usage object reads their results. A new usage input (a posted lineup's minutes, a depth chart's order) enters through the base it belongs to, held out and gated (D22, D28), and shows up here by itself.
  - **The expected outs behind strikeouts** (`props.mjs pitcherOuts`): the outs model's mean on the same row, a readout only. Pricing strikeouts through the outs (K per out times expected outs: the deep dive's E) is a projection change for its own gate.
- **Gate (D22):** a readout; the projections, prices and selection are untouched, so it ties by construction. Recorded on the scorecard as its own version.
- **Code:** `usage.mjs` (`usageOf`, `usageText`, `UNITS`), `props.mjs` (`pitcherOuts`, `usage` on the projection), `picks.mjs` (`usage` on a prop's pick). Tests: `tests/model-usage.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** none can move it: the usage is a function of the fitted base's parts each run, stored nowhere, stepped by nothing. The refits (`fitStat`) that fit the bases (sn, sr, vol, the lineup terms) move it only as they move the projection.

### D42. The Bot picks its own time to bet: windows a day ahead, the lineup risk, a fresh quote only to bet
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the owner's decision that morning, folded into the usage round).
- **Owner:** windows open the day before for every sport but MMA, every market priced and published as a suggestion from then on, every hourly run; inside the window the Bot decides when to place each bet for its own record, judged by a fitted lineup-risk term; its recorded price is the price at that moment; the placement-time choice self-tuning on history, per sport and stat, soft; inside the Odds API budget.
- **What this is about:** having the Book's information, not racing the line. The Bot's disagreement with the Book is 75 to 90% noise where the close knows roles, injuries, lineups and leashes (D33). An early bet faces the news the close will know: the usage behind the stat changes between the early price and the lock, and the Book moves with it. So the Bot bets early only where its edge survives that risk, and otherwise waits for the lineups, inactives, goalies or probables, or for the last chance.
- **Rule:**
  - **The windows** (`leagues.mjs TIMING`): NFL, NBA, NHL and MLB lines and props at 26 hours; MMA stays at 72. The Book's preview window follows (`preview-props.ts PROPS_WINDOW_HOURS`). Every priced market goes on board.json as a suggestion (`sug`: the side the Bot sees an edge on at the latest known price, its edge, the hours to the start, the survival its lean was held to, the price's source), every run from the window's open.
  - **The lineup risk** (`lineup-risk.mjs`): per sport and stat, `kept` is the share of the Bot's edge on its own side at the open (the priced history's, OPEN_LEAD before the start) still there at the close on the same side at the close's line and price, on the rows it would have bet (an edge of `EDGE_MIN` 0.01 or more); with it `moved`, how often the line or the price (5 points) changed by the close, and `lead`. Fit by `gate.mjs --record` on the priced history (`gate-history.mjs propHistoryRows`, both ends at the trusted chance) to `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/lineup-risk.json`; the desk reads it and never fits it. Under `RISK_MIN` (300) rows a stat reads the sport's pooled survival; with none, 0: with no evidence the edge survives, the Bot waits (the owner's "only when the edge survives").
  - **The survival at h hours** (`survivalAt`): `kept` at the open's lead or further out, 1 inside the last chance, a straight line between; 1 once the news the stat waits for is in (the NHL's goalies, MLB's starters and lineups: `timing.mjs newsMissing`; the NFL and NBA have no such news, their curve runs to the last chance).
  - **Self-tuning** (`ledgerRisk`, `riskAt`): the Bot's own prop bets since the epoch with a close, by stat and the hours before the start they were placed (`HOUR_BUCKETS` 2, 4, 8, 14, 27), the same ratio from each bet's edge at placement against the close's chance at its own price; blended into the history's survival at the bucket by its rows' worth (`n / (n + RISK_K)`, 40). Soft, never a switch (D8); a stat whose early bets keep their CLV earns a higher survival at those hours by itself.
  - **The bet** (`run.mjs betProps riskOf`): before the news is in, the row's chance is `fair + s x (p - fair)` (`riskChance`) and it is bet now only if that clears the price, at that chance and return, the bet carrying `early` (its hours, the survival, the full lean) for the record to judge; with no surviving edge it is held for a later run, neither bet nor tracked (a tracked row would block the bet later; the no-edge rows are tracked at the last chance as ever, D21). At the last chance everything is bet with what's known, as before. Practice picks (D2) come from the rows that pass.
  - **A fresh quote only to bet** (`run.mjs defer`, `askNow`): where the Book's free board carries prices (ESPN's NBA, NHL and MLB boards) or the morning snapshot has the game (the NFL's, `suggest.mjs`), the game's first ask of The Odds API is deferred: the props are priced off the free price first, and the quote is bought (and the game priced again on it) only where a row's surviving lean clears that price; else the run shows the suggestions and spends nothing. The owed ask, the near-start ask and the last chance ask as before; `PROP_ASKS` a game still caps every ask. Where there is no free price at all (the NFL with no snapshot) the first ask comes when due, as before.
  - **The morning snapshot prices a suggestion, never a bet** (`suggest.mjs`, the one reader outside the gate): a row priced from it is marked `snapshot` and never bet, never tracked, never a CHECK's stored price. D30 stands: a bet's price is the price offered the moment it is placed.
- **Credits** (the 20K plan, oddsapi.mjs): opening the windows earlier adds no asks: a game's asks are capped at `PROP_ASKS` (6) and the first one now comes later, not earlier (when the Bot is about to bet, or at the last chance), so the early hours cost nothing. Measured on the ledger (2026-10-08 to 10-18): the NFL's props asks about 63 a week at about 10 credits (about 90 a day), the NBA's 5 a game, the NHL's 3, MLB's 4, each about two asks a game (the first and the near one): about 80 + 48 + 10 a day in season, with the lines' calls about 230 a day, about 7,000 a month against the daily allowances (`budget`: 70% of what's left past the reserve and history's share for props, 30% for lines, 2 to 4 days' burst). The morning snapshot keeps its own 300 a day. The projection falls where the deferred first ask finds no edge at the free price.
- **Gate (D22):** live-only timing code the replay can't see: a tie by construction; recorded on the scorecard. The lineup risk's fit is written by the same record (D33's pattern).
- **Measured (the priced history at the open against the close, 2026-10-19):** how often the line moved or the price moved 5 points by the close: NFL passing and rushing yards 74 to 90%, rush+rec 82%, carries 36%, attempts 41%, receptions 22%, touchdowns and interceptions under 7%; NBA points 49%, PRA 57%, rebounds 33%, assists 33%, threes 18%; NHL points and shots under 4%; MLB hits 6%, total bases 7%, strikeouts 29%, outs 27%. The ledger's own early bets (10-08 to 10-18): the close moved against the NFL's 14-to-27-hour bets by 0.7 points on average, the NHL's by 0 to 0.2, MLB's 27-hour-plus by 1.7: the survival by stat is what the fit reads, never a flat discount.
- **Code:** `leagues.mjs` (`TIMING`), `lineup-risk.mjs` (`fitLineupRisk`, `survivalAt`, `ledgerRisk`, `riskAt`, `riskChance`, `readLineupRisk`), `suggest.mjs` (`morningPrices`, `withSnapshot`), `run.mjs` (`riskOf`, `defer`, `askNow`, `priceNow`, `early`, `sug`), `board.mjs` (`sug`), `gate-history.mjs` `propHistoryRows` (`risk`), `gate.mjs` (`recordScore` writes lineup-risk.json), `preview-props.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-lineup-risk.test.mjs`, `tests/model-timing.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** the history's fit moves only with a gate record (never a desk run); the ledger's blend reads bets since the epoch, so the epoch resets it (D4); the trust fit (`fitTrust`) reads an early bet at its own `tw` as before (a bet placed before the window at half) and never the survival; the calibration and the correction read `modelRaw`, untouched by the hold; the CLV capture, the CHECKs, the research and the practice picks are unchanged in kind, fed by fewer early rows.
- **Never:** a bet, a tracked row or a stored CHECK price from the snapshot; a first ask bought with no edge at the free price before the last chance; a survival above 1 or a prior toward betting early with no evidence.

### D43. MLB pitcher shape: a starter's outs are a reflected count, left-skewed; strikeouts keep their shape
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the usage round, the deep dive's J and the hold of the same day: "the shape, not the mean").
- **Why:** on outs the Bot's over chance averaged .439 where overs landed .504 (the Book .506), and the rows it read lowest went over half the time: the plain negative binomial is right-skewed, an outs count is left-skewed (a hook ends a start early, so the long tail runs toward 0 and the mass sits near a cap, the median above the mean). Anchoring the mean to the Book couldn't fix it (the hold above: outs .6946 anchored against the Book's .6848).
- **Rule:** a stat marked with caps (`props.mjs STATS flip`: outs [21, 24, 27]) is shaped as M - k at mean M - mu, a negative binomial on the outs still to get (`project.mjs flipOf, flipLog, flipOver`, inside `chanceLog` and `chanceOver`, so every chance, push, implied mean, anchor and gate reads one shape). The fit tries each cap at its own best dispersion and growth on the fit window (`fitStat`'s shape search beside rk) and keeps one only where the held-out games are better with it, as rk is kept (`gains.flip`); the recalibration is fit on the shape that stands. A mean at or past the cap, or a line the cap can't clear (a complete game's 27 at a cap of 24), reads the plain shape, so nothing is priced impossible. Strikeouts stay plain: their count's shape is right (measured below), their gap is the mean.
- **Measured (2026-10-19, the worktree's replay under the saved fit, the last 30% held out, 2,641 starts; the counts' log loss, lower better):** outs plain 2.8009 (r 300), reflected at a cap of 21 2.6748 (r 3), 24 2.7334 (r 16), 27 2.7425 (r 60); at the median line (the fit's check) 0.6375 plain against 0.6307 to 0.6324 reflected after the recalibration; at the Book's closing lines (2,382 rows) the raw over chance said .433 against .512 landed plain, .483 to .529 reflected, the raw log loss .7256 to .7116-.7165 (the Book .6812: the mean's noise is the rest). Strikeouts: plain 2.1869 against 2.28 to 2.49 reflected at any cap; its over chance .468 against .487 landed, a level, not a shape.
- **Gate (D22, MLB with --refit):** see the scorecard entry of this commit.
- **Code:** `project.mjs` (`flipOf`, `flipLog`, `flipOver`, `logAt`, `overAt`, `chanceLog`, `chanceOver`, `impliedMean`'s cap, `fitStat`'s shape search and gate), `props.mjs` STATS outs `flip`. Tests: `tests/model-shape.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** the refit alone sets the cap (once a day, or on a code change), held out like rk; the calibration, the correction, the trust and the anchor read the chances the shape gives and move nothing in it; the saved fit carries `flip` as any parameter (fitcache), a fit from before it reads 0 (the plain shape) till the refit.
- **Never:** a cap under the line priced (the plain shape stands in); a cap kept without its held-out gain; the shape on a stat not marked.

### D44. Totals: the Bot's trust in its own total held at 0 by rule; a total is bet only where the named terms earn it
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the usage round; the deep dive's I and the hold of the history-fitted trust prior the same day).
- **Why:** the Bot's own totals lean is anti-informative at the close in every sport (the deep dive: at the close the slope of the result on the lines' deviation is about 0 or negative, NFL -0.08, NBA -0.03, NHL -0.03, MLB +0.02; the walk-forward trust on the priced history sat at 0 since 2022 for MLB and NBA totals and the NHL's 0.3 cost 0.0008 to 0.0033 in 2026-27). What the close does get wrong on totals is named: the NBA's altitude and lopsided games, the sides off a blowout win (D34's soft schedule terms, each in at its own ridge).
- **Rule:**
  - **The trust at 0** (`desk.mjs TRUST_START`, `trustStart`): a total's trust starts at 0 and the fit (`run.mjs refitTrust`) reads only the Bot's own graded totals since the epoch, never the backtest's closes; under 40 of them it stays at 0, and with them it climbs only where that held-out record earns it (the fit's 2 nats a unit of trust as ever). The other markets are as they were (0.5, the backtest and the live record). The gate (`gate.mjs`) and the priced history (`gate-history.mjs lineHistoryRows`) fit a total's trust from 0 the same way.
  - **The named terms price a total** (`margins.mjs totalSplit`, `ratings.mjs namedIdx`, `online.part`): the Bot's total is split into its base (everything but the soft schedule terms) and the named terms' shift (`exp.namedT`, the replay's and the live expectation's). A total's side carries `named`, the shift as a chance at the Book's line (the over's, the under's its negative), and `chanceAt` adds it to the Book's fair at full weight (their ridge already sizes them, D34) with the base's lean at the trust on top: at trust 0 a total's chance is the Book's fair plus what the named terms say, so it is bet only where they move it past the price. The bet, the backtest's rows, the board and the trust fit all carry the shift, so a total is judged and refit on the chance it was bet at.
  - **The Book's over-shade** (the deep dive's A, D32's residual model) is a props read and isn't a totals term yet; a fitted over-shade on totals would enter here the same way, as a named term with its own held-out gate.
- **Gate (D22):** see the scorecard entry of this commit. A tie at the close is the expected read: the Bot's lean was already trusted near 0 there; the rule pins it and lets the named terms through.
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`TRUST_START`, `trustStart`, `chanceAt`'s `ctx.named`, `price`, `choose`, `fitTrust`), `margins.mjs` `totalSplit`, `ratings.mjs` (`online.part`, `namedIdx`, `replay`'s `namedT`), `run.mjs` (`expectFor`, `refitTrust`, the bet's `named`), `backtest.mjs` `repriced`, `board.mjs` `boardMarkets`, `gate.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs`. Tests: `tests/model-totals.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** the trust fit can raise a total's trust only on the Bot's own graded totals since the epoch (D4 resets it); the named terms' sizes move only in the context refit's ridge (D34), never here; the calibration, the correction and check-learn read props only; the board shows the chance the desk bets with.
- **Never:** a total's trust fit on the backtest's closes or on rows from before the epoch; a named term that isn't a soft context term; a totals bet with no named shift and no earned trust.

### D45. The play chance reads the practice days and the board's missing props
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the forum round's ideas 1 and 4, queued into the usage round).
- **Why:** the thread's one transferable edge is timing on injury news: its pickers beat the DraftKings close 73% of the time against the Bot's 55% on its real bets since 10-08, mostly by reading Wednesday-to-Friday practice and the props the Book withholds before the designation is official. Both signals were already in reach: the play table measured the practice level at +0.0203 held out (2023-2025), the largest of its levels, but never read it live (`PLAY_UNREAD`); and the priced history shows a regular whose props are missing at the open while teammates' are posted was out, or under a quarter of the snaps, 44.8% of the time against 9.1% listed (1,310 against 8,504 cases): with no report status 28.6% against 8.4%, Questionable 47.1% against 26.0%.
- **Rule:**
  - **The practice level is read live** (`playchance.mjs PLAY_LEVELS` qb, gone, practice; `nflPractice`, `practiceFrom`): the week's practice status by ESPN id from nflverse's injuries file for the current season (read again every 12 hours; the game's week from nflverse's schedule), on the game's live facts (`live.practice`, run.mjs), into the questionable regular's chance (`props.mjs qMiss`). The table is fit on the final practice of the week, which is what's posted once the window opens (26 hours before the kickoff, `PLAY_FINAL`), so live and fit read the same thing. No look-ahead: the file holds what nflverse has posted at the run's moment.
  - **The missing-prop signal** (`board-miss.mjs nflBoardMiss`, `playchance.mjs boardMissChance`, `props.mjs missOf`): a regular at the position (`matchups.mjs regulars`) with no prop on this game's board while `PLAY_MISSING_MIN` (2) teammates at his position group have one misses the game at the rate the history gives his report status group: not on the report (`none`), or Questionable (`q`), each pulled toward its group's pooled rate by `BOARD_K` (25) cases. It replaces 0 (not on the report) or the table's chance (Questionable) in the vacated work his teammates inherit and in the guards; a guard's fixed view (every questionable one in, or out) stays fixed. The rates are fit each run from the history's opens against the snap counts (2023 on, about two seconds) and logged; nothing in it is a price (D30: the history is training data for the play chance here, as nflverse's past reports are for the table).
  - **Both are NFL inputs**; the other sports' lineups and goalies already come through `newsMissing` and the posted lists.
  - **The table still decides the practice level each run** (`choosePlayTable`: its held-out gain and no group's bias past `PLAY_TOL`): on 2026-10-19's live fit it gained +0.0203 held out and was left out on the tight ends' bias, so the read is wired and takes effect the run it clears; the missing-prop rates read from the first run (28% and 45%).
- **Gate (D22):** the replay can't see either (the history's vacancy reads the final report alone), so the gate reads a tie; the measure is the play table's held-out check in every run's log (the practice level kept on its gain) and the history's rates above. Recorded on the scorecard.
- **Code:** `playchance.mjs` (`PLAY_LEVELS`, `PLAY_UNREAD`, `practiceFrom`, `nflPractice`, `boardMissChance`), `board-miss.mjs` (`nflBoardMiss`, `BOARD_K`, `BOARD_OUT_PCT`, `PLAY_MISSING_MIN`, `boardMissText`), `props.mjs` (`boardTeam`, `listedMates`, `boardMissing`, `missOf`, `qMiss`), `run.mjs` (`practiceFor`, `r.boardMiss`, the live facts). Tests: `tests/model-play-chance.test.mjs` (D45).
- **Loops:** the table is re-fit each run from past seasons' reports as before (the practice level kept only on its held-out gain, `choosePlayTable`); the rates are re-fit each run from the history, never stepped; the re-price on news (`repriceOpen`) reads statuses, not practice or the board, so an early bet stands till its status changes or the last chance.
- **Next (the forum's 2 and 4, held with numbers):** the last-game overreaction as a residual feature (not run this round); the return ramp is already a searched late term (`project.mjs ij`): rushing yards +0.0012 held out, carries +0.0006, receptions +0.0002, receiving yards 0, rush+rec -0.0003, kept on none (its subgroup bias), so it stays measured each refit.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-19: opening-week priors for the NBA, NHL and MLB
- **The ask:** priors from the prior season (and the Bot's own prior-season projections) so no sport starts blind (the deep dive's C: NBA games 0-2 at 11.16 nats a hundred rows against the Book, b* 0.001; the NHL's first 25 games 65% of its gap; MLB's 2025 rows with no last season).
- **What the code already does:** a player's prior is his last season at weight 1 and older seasons at 0.5 (`project.mjs makeModel`, DECISIONS.md "Recency in the fits"), the NBA's minutes pulled toward last season's a game early on (`MIN_RECENT`), the NHL's and MLB's the same way through their rates; a player with no run of his own reads the eligible players' pool at his position (`pool`). So the live desk's openers this week read last season: the NBA's 2026-27 rows have 2025-26 in the cache, the NHL's 2026-27 have 2025-26.
- **Where the blindness is:** the desk's history for the NBA, NHL and MLB is two seasons (`run.mjs loadHistory`, `playerlogs.mjs`), so the first season in the cache has no prior at all, and that season is where the deep dive's opening numbers come from (NBA 2025-26's openers, 100% with no last season; NHL 2025-26, 89.5%). It is the gate's and the fit's blind spot, not tonight's: a fit whose window opens on a blind season learns its K and kd from it. The fix is depth, not a prior: a third season in the cache (ESPN's box scores for the NBA's and NHL's 1,300 to 1,400 games a season, StatsAPI's logs for MLB), which is a data decision (the Actions cache's size and the first run's fetch, the fits' time on a longer window) for the owner, and a replay the gate can't judge cleanly (both versions read the same history, so the deepened one scores on different rows).
- **The position-aware pool:** the NBA's positions (`props.mjs nbaPos`, `project.mjs ps`) were tried on the pool and the opponent's table on 2026-10-13 and kept on no stat (`TRY_PS` false: the pace kept instead); the NHL's pool is already by position (D against F); MLB's starters read the league's K prior (`baseball.mjs lgK`, 120 outs' worth) and its batters their slot. The "Bot's own prior-season projection" as a prior is the same information as last season's rows at weight 1, which the prior reads already.
- **Verdict: HOLD, nothing to ship this round.** The live openers aren't blind; the history's first season is, by the cache's depth. Next: deepen the three caches by a season (the deep dive's C, +0.02 to +0.04 nats a hundred rows for the NBA by its estimate), then re-fit K and kd with an early-season held-out slice and re-measure the position prior (`TRY_PS`) on the longer window.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-19: the last-result overreaction (the forum's idea; D45's "Next")
- **The ask:** the Book's price overreacts to a team's or player's last result (a blowout, an upset, a prop player's outlier game); fade the move. Soft, self-tuning, tested jointly, no double count.
- **What already reads it:** D34's soft schedule terms `letdown`, `bounce`, `letdownT` (a side off a blowout win or loss), the projections' last-5 form weight (0 to 0.4), and the residual's lean `d` (D32), which carries the Bot's own fade of a hot game (the NFL's raw leans −0.07 logits a standard deviation of last-game surprise against the Book).
- **The candidate (worktree only, not shipped):** two residual features (`residual.mjs features`: `last`, `last*late`, capped at 3), the last result's surprise in the row's frame, known by its start plus 4 hours before the row's snapshot (D48), inside 30 days: a prop, the player's last game on the stat against the Bot's projection for it in its standard deviations; a moneyline or spread, the home side's last margin against its rating's expectation (in sigmas) less the away side's; a total, both sides' last totals against expectation. The rows were collected the way `residual-train.mjs --collect` replays the priced history; both versions fit walk-forward (`fitWalk`) on the same rows, the blend weight as fit, flat 1u on every row whose priced chance clears its price.
- **The raw signal (the Book's error, result less the no-vig, a standard deviation of surprise):** only the NFL's props lean the hypothesis's way (close −0.78 points ±0.51, open −0.81 ±0.63; −0.72 against the current price); the NBA's props run the other way (+0.43 ±0.36: after a hot game the over does better than the Book says), NHL and MLB props +0.1 to +0.3 inside the noise, every sport's lines inside ±1.3 points at 95% widths of 0.5 to 2.6.
- **The increment (walk-forward log loss on the results, recency-weighted, with against without, by game):** props NFL +0.00002 (tie), NBA close −0.00003 (tie), open +0.00003 (worse beyond noise), NHL +0.00001 (tie), MLB 0.00000 (tie); the lines' part (priced at the trust alone today, O4) NBA +0.00007 worse beyond noise, NHL, MLB and NFL ties (the NFL's close −0.0007, 95% −0.0031 to +0.0001, on 1,593 games). The ridge kept the term near 0 everywhere (largest standardized weight 0.014, NBA props, positive: follow, not fade). Flat units moved inside the noise (NFL props +12.9u to +36.3u on about 4,200 bets with the accuracy tied; NBA props +84.2u to +74.4u).
- **Verdict: HOLD, nothing shipped.** The Book doesn't overreact to the last result at any size the history can see once the Bot's own lean and the residual are in; where the raw fade shows (NFL props) the residual's lean already carries it. No gate run: the candidate moved no price by more than 0.00003 a row. Re-measure when a season's more props are in the history (the NFL's raw −0.78 is the one to watch).

### D47. The Bot's history is the replayed record: today's algorithm over the priced history, plus its real bets since the epoch
- **Date:** 2026-10-19.
- **Owner:** "Since the Bot is agnostic, update the Algorithm history (record, last 5, etc.) with the bets and confidence bands it WOULD have made against the history. With full history we can change the algo and keep an honest history, since it's a bot and not a human." And: the replayed record isn't a side panel; it is the Bot's history everywhere the site shows one; the band headers read "This year" (a calendar year, a season not being defined across five sports).
- **Rule:**
  - **The Bot's history = the replay of the shipped algorithm over the priced history before the current `MODEL_EPOCH` + the live ledger's real bets on the games from the epoch on** (the same algorithm, so they are the replay going forward). The split is by the game's start, so a game is in exactly one part. On each epoch bump `gate.mjs --record` rebuilds the "before" part.
  - **The replay** (`replay-record.mjs replayRecord`, run inside the gate's scoring on the rows `gate-history.mjs` and `gate-mma.mjs` price): every key (a game's market, a player's prop) once at most, walk-forward with no look-ahead (the rows' chances are the version's own replay: the ratings and projections from the games before, the trust by the months before, the anchor by the seasons before, the residual by the snapshots before; the lineup risk the replay reads is fit on the seasons before the row's, `walkRisk`), at the Bot's own placement (D42: the clean open, seen after both sides' previous game and inside the sport's window, where its edge held to the surviving share still clears the price; else the close, bet if it clears the vig; MMA's open as the archive has it, priced by its lead band, D37, inside the live window since D56), at the Bot's own bands (`picks.mjs edgeLevel` on the Kelly at the chance it was bet at, a LIKE at the least, a suspect return a LIKE at most) and stakes (`desk.mjs stakeFor`, a suspect return at the practice stake), the slate's practice picks on their own line (`slateQuota`, `practiceTop`, `PRACTICE_SPLIT`). The price is DraftKings' snapshot nearest the placement, never shopped. The live desk's CLV gates, CHECKs, research and news can't be replayed and aren't.
  - **The file:** `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/replay-record.json`, small (the summaries, the last 20 bets, the last 14 days; no row list): overall, practice, the rows passed (at a flat unit: context, never the record), by band, by market, by season (with its context flag: `full` where the desk's cache had the props, `ratings` where the lines were priced by the ratings alone), by calendar year, the days, the streak, the honesty flags (open against close, context by season, the price convention, the split).
  - **The pages read the merge** (`picks.mjs recordBlocks`: `mergeRecord`, `mergeRecent` into picks.json's `record` and `recent`, so the Book needs no new fetch): the band headers' "This year" (`record.year.byLevel`, the caveat on hover), the Recent form card's Bot side (the replay's days, last bets and streak joined to the live block's), the Algorithm tab's "The Bot's history" panel (`replay-history.ts botHistoryOf`: the record, by band, by sport, by market, each sport's seasons, the last 20 in the Recent form's style, the one-line caveat). The live ledger's own figures stay on the Algorithm tab as the secondary "live since this version" line and the tiles (what the Bot actually did); the wallet's You-vs-Bot duel stays the live forced side (the user's bets are real).
  - **Regenerated only by `gate --record`** (a shipped version), never by the hourly desk (the Loops rule).
- **Measured (the record of 2026-10-19's master, `--score-only worktree --record`, the whole priced history):** NFL 1750-1769-10, −39.0u (ROI −1.1%) on 3,529 bets, all at the close (the archive's NFL opens are 48 hours out, outside the 26-hour window), LOCK 47-32 +19.5u, LOVE 457-470-3 −25.7u, LIKE 1246-1267-7 −32.8u; NHL 813-995-29, −45.0u (−3.1%), 796 at a clean open; NBA 2236-2579-2, +24.0u (+0.5%), LOCK 171-95 +22.4u; MLB 701-810-9, +28.6u (+2.3%), LOVE 118-103-1 +47.7u; MMA 784-865-27, +176.1u on 1,676 bets, 1,531 at the archive's first sighting (D37's early band: a soft opening line, the paper edge largest where real money is hardest to get down; the caveat says so). Full context (props and lines) NFL 2023–2026, NBA, NHL and MLB the last two seasons; the older seasons' lines by the ratings alone.
- **Code:** `replay-record.mjs` (`replayRecord`, `walkRisk`, `cleanOpenOf`, `summarize`, `caveatOf`, `mergeRecord`, `mergeRecent`, `readReplay`), `gate.mjs` (`scoreVersion`'s `replay`, `recordScore` writes the file), `gate-mma.mjs` (the same), `gate-history.mjs` (the prop rows' names), `picks.mjs` (`recordBlocks`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`buildFightPicks` reads it), `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/replay-history.ts`, `model-desk.component.*` (the panel), `book-page.component.*` ("This year", the caveat), `book-rows.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-replay-record.test.mjs` (no look-ahead by timestamp, the risk walk by season, the clean open, the bands summing, the file's shape, the merge), `tests/book-replay-history.test.mjs` (the panel's numbers, the shipped files' shape).
- **Loops:** the replay reads the same walk-forward rows the gate scores and writes nothing the desk fits on; the desk reads the file and never writes it; the merge reads the ledger and writes nothing back. A new epoch starts the live part over and the next record rebuilds the replay with the new algorithm. The record never depends on whose cache was on disk (2026-10-19, after 57f80332 wrote the NFL at -63.5u from a worktree's incomplete `.cache/model` where the desk's gives -32.95u, ddefa4e1): every score carries an input fingerprint (gate.mjs `inputsOf`: the props fit's code, day and file hash against the code each version expects; the context facts' games before the epoch by season and source, and whether the context fit; the NFL's injury listings by season; the ratings state, the Pinnacle backtest, the residual model's file; the replay's rows and keys, the frozen benchmark's rows and mode; the units), written into replay-record.json (`inputs`) and each scorecard entry (its hash and counts). Before writing any sport, `--record` checks them all (`recordGuard`) and exits 3 writing nothing when one is incomplete (no fit or one neither version expects, no context facts or fit, no ratings state or backtest, the champion and the candidate with the same code replaying differently) or held: anything shrank since the last record, or, with the model's code unchanged since it (the pricing parts, replay-record.mjs and picks.mjs, the gate's own files, residual.json), any input count differs or the units moved more than `UNITS_TOL` (3u): "inputs changed, not the model". A held record is written only with `--accept-inputs "<why>"`, the reason kept on it. `gate.mjs --rebuild-cache [sports]` builds the full cache first (the context facts, the players' games, the injury listings, the props' fit where none this code can use; free sources, no bets), then records with `--record`. Tests: tests/model-record-guard.test.mjs.
- **Never:** the replay regenerated by the desk; a replayed bet priced from a snapshot after its placement; a game counted in both parts; the practice picks in the record; the replay's numbers in the wallet's duel or the trust fit.
### D48. No look-ahead by timestamp: every replayed bet reads only facts dated before its placement
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the coordinator's round on D47: the Algorithm tab showed the NBA beating the close 91%, its LOCKs 100%, the NHL 82%, MLB's LOVEs 93%; the forum audit's caveat put it on the day-of context at the clean opens).
- **What it was:** mostly not the day-of context (the NBA's 2020–2025 lines are priced by the ratings alone and beat the close 99% in 2021–22). The archive's open is each book's own first sighting, so Pinnacle's open, the fair the replay prices against, came after DraftKings' in 10 to 28% of the daily sports' games (a median of hours later; the NFL's two thirds, MMA's 18%): the "fair" carried the market's later price, and a moneyline at trust 0 bet wherever DraftKings' open sat off it, beating the close by construction. Beside it, smaller: the day-of terms (the NBA's missing production from the box, the NHL's starting goalie, MLB's starter, lineups, weather and crew) read at an open, the ratings' state learned from other games finished after the snapshot, the trust by the game's month, not the snapshot's; MMA's early opens read Pinnacle's later price too, and its fit's period could start after the open.
- **Rule:**
  - **The Book's prices:** Pinnacle's price is the fair only where it was seen by DraftKings' own snapshot (`gate-history.mjs historyLineBets` `seenBy`, `gate-mma.mjs historyRows` the same), else DraftKings' own no-vig; a moneyline reads the spread's frame only where the spread was posted by its snapshot, else it isn't priced there. Each MMA row's time, lead and band are its own market's.
  - **The game as of the snapshot** (`ratings.mjs replay` `asOf`, `featsAt`): every archived line is priced from the ratings after the finals over by its snapshot (a final's result known its start plus 4 hours) and the terms as of it, read aside so the replay's own run is untouched. The day-of terms (`context.mjs DAY_OF_TERMS`) are known `DAY_OF_LEAD` before the start (inactives and goalies 30 minutes, a starter or lineup card an hour, the NFL's starters 90 minutes, the weather 30 minutes, the crew 6 hours); before that a term reads what was known (`featurize` `dayOf`): the NBA's regulars who missed the team's last game, the NHL's no-probable rule (the last starter, his backup on a back-to-back's second night), none (0) for the rest. A prop's projection reads its game's context whole, so a prop row is stamped the start less `dayOfMinLead`: its opens (12 hours out) are never bet.
  - **The trust as of the snapshot** (`walkTrust` `at`): an open row reads the trust fit on the months before its own snapshot's.
  - **MMA** (`gate-mma.mjs walkForward` `prev`): an open seen before its half-year's fit began reads the fit standing then; each row is stamped with the fit's last bout (`FIT_KNOWN_H`) and both fighters' own last bouts before this one (`BOUT_KNOWN_H`). A fighter's rating moves only on his own bouts (rating.mjs), so it is as of the open already: no early open read a later fact (`openHeld` 0).
  - **The stamp and the check** (`replay-record.mjs knownAt`, `lateFacts`): every row carries `factsAt`, the latest fact its price read. An open with a later one falls to the close; a close with one isn't bet or picked; every bet keeps its `factsAt`, and the file counts the bets past their placement (`flags.facts`: `stamped`, `late` 0, `openHeld`, `closeHeld`). `tests/model-no-lookahead.test.mjs` fails on any.
- **Measured (`--score-only worktree --record`, every sport, the whole priced history; before → after):** NFL 1750-1769-10 −39.0u → 1611-1620-10 −37.9u (all at the close either way); NBA 2236-2579-2 +24.0u, beating the close 91% (LOCK 100%) → 1603-2003-2 −51.6u, 63% (363 at a clean open, was 1,348); NHL 813-995-29 −45.0u, 82% → 789-987-26 −48.4u, 81%; MLB 701-810-9 +28.6u, 77% (LOVE 93%) → 586-699-9 +7.7u, 64%; MMA 784-865-27 +176.1u, 65% → 688-778-25 +179.4u, 62%. The NHL's 81% is honest by timestamp: its open bets are puck lines and moneylines at trust 0 where DraftKings' price sat past Pinnacle's no-vig at the same snapshot (a price both books showed at that moment), which DraftKings then moved to; small edges, where real limits are smallest.
- **Gate (D22):** a version from before the asOf replay prices its rows at the game's own expectation, stamped with the game's facts (its replay never bets those opens); the history layer against such a champion favors the champion at the open until both carry it.
- **Never:** a fair from a book's price seen after the row's snapshot; a day-of term at its box-score value before its fact was posted; a replayed bet whose `factsAt` is after its `at`.

### D49. The news-speed input: Sleeper's statuses and the reporters' posts, ahead of ESPN's report, into every input that reads it
- **Date:** 2026-10-19.
- **Owner:** the forum audit showed the edge is timing: the sharp posters beat the close 73% of the time by knowing injury and role news before the line moves, the Bot 55%. "Get the Bot news faster, free sources only."
- **Rule:**
  - **Sleeper** (`fastnews.mjs sleeperPlayers`): the public players dump (`api.sleeper.app/v1/players/<nfl|nba|nhl>`, no key) pulled at most once a UTC day, as Sleeper asks (the first run from `SLEEPER_HOUR` 20 UTC, or any run once the kept one is `SLEEPER_MAX_AGE_H` 30 hours old), kept compact in `.cache/model/sleeper-<sport>.json`: each player's status class (`sleeperStatus`: Out, IR, PUP, Sus out; Doubtful; Questionable, DTD questionable), body part, practice (`sleeperPractice`), `news_updated`, depth-chart order. The lighter intraday endpoints (trending adds and drops) carry no status and aren't read.
  - **Bluesky** (`news-handles.json`, 15 accounts: 7 NFL, 3 NBA, 5 NHL; each checked to exist, to have posted in the last week and to post injury or lineup news): their posts read each desk run through the public AppView (`app.bsky.feed.getAuthorFeed`, no auth), the ones since each account's cursor that name an injury, a status or a role (`POST_RELEVANT`) given to the news signals' classifier (`news-signals.mjs postSignals`: claude-haiku-5-5 at low effort, 25 posts a call, under the news signals' caps and the run's call count, `NEWS_CAPS` / `CAP_WINDOWS`), which returns structured claims only (player, team, status out/doubtful/questionable/limited/active/none, kind, confidence), checked against the call's posts (`claimsOf`). Kept in `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/news-fast.json` with the post's time (`at`) and the run that read it (`seen`); the bettor reads them the next run. A validation call (2026-10-19) cost $0.001 to $0.0015 for 13 to 25 posts.
  - **Into the report** (`fastNotes`, run.mjs `fastNews`, before the context reads the reports): a status for a coming game's side posted inside `LOOK_H` hours of its start (NFL 54, NBA and NHL 30: an older one is the last game's) and before the run (no look-ahead), with confidence `MIN_CLAIM_CONF` (0.6) or more for a claim, the player matched to an ESPN id (the dump's `espn_id`, else his name on the side's ESPN roster; none, not applied), goes into that game's report copy (from `sleeper` or `bluesky`, dated its own time) only where it is worse than ESPN's own entry and newer than it, or ESPN doesn't list him: the research's rule (`researchNote`). An owner's note or the research's entry on him wins; an "active" claim is logged, never applied (a return is ESPN's or the posted roster's to say). So every input that reads the report reads it: the statuses, the questionable regular's play chance (D45), the vacated work and the usage (D41), the missing-prop signal's status group (D45), the game lines' context, and the inputs (`timing.mjs inputsOf`), so an open bet on the game is priced again the next run (`repriceOpen`). The NFL's practice level (D45) takes Sleeper's practice where nflverse's file has none for him (`withSleeperPractice`, inside the week, before now).
  - **The Bot's own time to bet (D42)** (`run.mjs riskOf`, `fastFor`): an NFL or NBA row whose side has fast news posted `FRESH_H` (12) hours ago at most is past its news: survival 1, the full lean, bet now if it clears the price, the bet carrying `early.fast` (the player, status, source and time). Those bets go at the practice stake (a LIKE at most) until `fastEarned`: `FAST_EARN_N` (30) graded since the epoch with their mean CLV (the close's chance at the bet's price less its no-vig) above 0; till then the board carries the row's cap (`cap: 'medium'`, D46), so its suggestion shows no band the bet couldn't get. The NHL waits on its goalies as before.
  - **The speed** (`speedUpdate`, `news-speed.mjs`): each run (not `--dry`) logs each coming game's listed players by source, the run that first saw each status and the source's own time (`apps/<sport>/scripts/model/news-speed.json`); `news-speed.mjs` sets them against the Book's move in board-seen.json (his own props' lines pulled or moved, the first run after the earliest source) and gives each source's median lead. `--retro` does the same for the week from what the sources show now.
- **Measured (2026-10-19, `news-speed.mjs --retro`, the week's games; each source's own time, counted only before the start):** NFL 157 status changes: ESPN had 36 before the start (the rest updated after the games), Sleeper 124 (first on 76), Bluesky 58 (first on 54). Sleeper against ESPN where both had it: −0.1 hours (n 31, Sleeper mirrors the report minutes after it: its worth is the players ESPN's report leaves off and the practice days, not speed); Bluesky +0.8 hours ahead of ESPN (n 11). NHL 36: Sleeper −0.1 hours (n 25), Bluesky −3 hours (n 3). The Book's move could be timed on 2 NFL events only (Bluesky +5.4 hours ahead of it): DraftKings posts no props for a player already listed out, so a status's move is mostly in his teammates' lines, which move for many reasons; the live log measures it from now on (the median lead by source, NFL week 8 and the NBA's opening week).
- **Gate (D22):** live-only inputs the replay can't see (the priced history's vacancy reads the final report alone; no Sleeper or Bluesky history exists): a tie by construction, as D42 and D45; `gate.mjs nfl nba nhl` against origin/master (2026-10-19): ties everywhere, SHIP. The early fast bets are at the practice stake till their CLV earns more.
- **Code:** `fastnews.mjs` (`sleeperPlayers`, `sleeperDue`, `compactSleeper`, `sleeperStatus`, `sleeperPractice`, `espnAbbr`, `ALIAS`, `authorPosts`, `POST_RELEVANT`, `readClaims`, `candidatesFor`, `fastNotes`, `fastFor`, `fastEarned`, `withSleeperPractice`, `speedUpdate`, `LOOK_H`, `FRESH_H`, `FAST_TIMING`, `FAST_EARN_N`), `news-handles.json`, `news-signals.mjs` (`postSignals`, `claimsOf`, `postsPrompt`, `readFastLog`), `run.mjs` (`fastNews`, `riskOf`'s fast, the early fast bet's practice stake, `practiceFor`), `news-speed.mjs` (`bookMove`, `leads`, `liveEvents`, `retroEvents`). Tests: `tests/model-fastnews.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** none fits on it. The claims' log grows by the news step (`FAST_KEEP_DAYS` 21), the speed log by the desk (`SPEED_KEEP_DAYS` 10); the stake's earn reads the fast bets since the epoch, so the epoch resets it (D4). The lineup risk's ledger blend (`ledgerRisk`) reads the fast bets with the rest, by the hours they were placed.
- **Never:** a source's status from after the run, or from outside the game's window; a softer status over ESPN's, or one older than ESPN's own entry; a claim's free text in a price; a return applied off a post; an early fast bet above the practice stake before it's earned; the dump pulled more than once a UTC day.

### D50. The NBA's season start: a team's first games priced at the level the lean was fit at, and no role till five are ranked
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the night before the 2026-27 opener; the flag from the dry run of a3199887: BOS @ DET's 56 props 91% under the line's even chance, 75% projected under the line, e.g. Derrick White points 13.8 on a 15.5 line in 34.2 minutes, Paul George 14.9 on 15.5).
- **What was measured:** the NBA's history holds two seasons, so its first season's openers are blind (the hold above, "opening-week priors") and the gate never saw an opener with a prior. For the measure alone the worktree's history was deepened by 2024-25 (ESPN's 1,315 finals and box scores, not committed) and the props replayed under the saved fit, so 2025-26's openers read 2024-25 as tonight's read 2025-26. On the 2025-26 rows matched to the DraftKings closes (the eligible players): the season's games 0, 1-2, 3-9 and 10 on, the actual over the projection 1.044, 1.095, 1.025 and 1.004 (points 1.093, 1.146, 1.029, 1.007); the Bot's chance under even on 68%, 72%, 67% and 64%; its log loss .7271, .7252, .7066, .7014 (the Book's .6872, .6923, .6878, .6878). Overs at the opener hit 47.3% against the Book's 49.7%, so part of an opener's under lean is real; the rest was three things the season start reads differently from the rest of the season, and one on the board:
  - **The allocation unread** (`project.mjs` `squadF`, tr): a team's S and T aren't read till it has played TR_MIN games, so its factor is 1; elsewhere it lifts the NBA's rows about 3% on average (1.024 to 1.035 by stat), and the lean (`scale`, 0.971 to 0.985) was fit with that lift in. An opener sat about 3% under the level the lean was fit at.
  - **The work missing read as none** (vacF): no regular of this season is known till the team has played, so the work missing tonight is 0 against last season's in the games his rate came from (vacF 0.962 to 0.979 on the openers, 1.003 to 1.005 the rest of the season).
  - **Every player a bench player in a team's games 1-2** (`matchups.mjs nbaMatchups roleOf`): no one has three games yet, so no one is in the top five and every player read B; points (the one stat with a role table, roleK 32) priced its stars against the bench's table (0.937 against 0.989 the rest of the season).
  - **And tonight's board: 8 of its 11 assists rows were ESPN's "Total Assists and Rebounds"** (`props.mjs STATS ast`: `/^Total Assists/` read both, and the ladder's main rung took the combination's line: Jalen Duren 12.5, Cade Cunningham 14.5, Jayson Tatum 14.5, Derrick White 8.5, each priced against his assists alone, under at 93% to 99%, 4 of them PASSes past the gap). The live board's alone: the history's props come from The Odds API's `player_assists`, and no NBA prop had been bet yet.
  - Not it: the minutes (last season's share, as designed: the openers' actual 23.2 against 24.9 expected, if anything high), the per-minute rate, the anchor and the calibration (both read the chance after the projection; the calibration has no graded NBA rows yet). Tried and dropped: the pace's denominator at last season's average in his games in place of 1 (its own team's pace counted once at the opener): the openers' log loss .7271 to .7288, worse.
- **Rule** (NBA stats marked `opener`, `props.mjs NBA_TEAM`):
  - **An unread allocation at its running mean** (`OPEN_TR`, `trOpenOf`): where S or T can't be read (`squadRead`), the factor is the geometric mean of the read ones over the last TR_NORM rows before the day (`trSeen`, walk-forward; none read yet, 1); live, the history's latest (`params.trOpen`, written by `fitStat`, or by one replay under a reused fit's settings, `trOpenFit`). A mean-preserving allocation (trn) stays at 1, its mean is 1.
  - **The work missing's factor 1 till the team has played this season** (`OPEN_VAC`, `teamNew`: none of its games this season learned).
  - **No role till five of the team's players have three games this season** (`roleOf` returns null, so the position's table prices them), the known role as before.
  - **The board's assists and rebounds are those markets alone** (`/^Total Assists( (|$)/`, `/^Total Rebounds( (|$)/`, as the site's `preview-props.ts` reads them).
- **Measured with the rule (the same deepened replay, the saved fit):** games 0 actual over projection 1.044 to 0.990, under even 68% to 57%, log loss .7271 to .7251 (points .7285 to .7213, pts+reb+ast .7547 to .7494, rebounds .7154 to .7124; assists .7423 to .7477 and threes .7001 to .7028 worse, 155 and 173 rows, inside one night's noise); games 1-2 1.095 to 1.068, 72% to 68%, .7252 to .7208 (every stat better); games 3-9 .7066 to .7067; games 10 on .7014, unchanged. BOS @ DET tonight (the fresh dry run, the refit under this code): its props under the line's even chance 91% to 82% (75% to 60% projected under the line; the second game on the board 66% and 44% with it), Derrick White's points 13.8 to 14.8, Paul George's 14.9 to 15.9, Duncan Robinson's 10.5 to 11.2; the assists rows on their own lines (Cade Cunningham's chance over 0.05 to 0.49). What's left of the lean is this game's: both defenses' tables 0.92 (the league's 0.97 to 0.99), the slowest pace pair (0.968), and the Book's opener overs (47.3% landed against its 49.7% in 2025-26).
- **Gate (D22, `--refit`, NBA):** SHIP, ties everywhere, on both histories. The committed one (against origin/master 0ea5ddc6): props layer a 2.4746 to 2.4745 over 102,020 player games (95% -0.0001 to 0.0000), the priced history's closes .6847 both, its top picks' CLV -0.0419 to -0.0416, against the Book at the closes -0.0004 better as before; its first season's openers are blind, so it sees the roles' fix alone. The deepened one (2024-25 added for the measure, against a3199887, both refit on three seasons): props layer a 2.4837 both over 207,140 (+0.0001, 0.0000 to +0.0001: the openers are 1% of the rows), the closes .686 both, the anchored chance against the Book -0.0003 (95% -0.0005 to -0.0002) as before.
- **Code:** `project.mjs` (`OPEN_TR`, `OPEN_VAC`, `squadRead`, `trOpenOf`, `trOpenFit`, `squadUp`'s `trSeen`, `teamNew`, `fitStat`'s `trOpen`), `props.mjs` (`NBA_TEAM` `opener`, `priceProps`'s `sqF`, `fitProps`' reused `trOpen`), `matchups.mjs` (`nbaMatchups roleOf`), `props.mjs STATS` (ast, reb). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D50, `tests/model-back-to-back.test.mjs` (the known role's test: no role in a team's first three games).
- **Loops:** the refit (`fitStat`) fits the lean with the openers at their new level and writes `trOpen` from its final replay; nothing steps it between runs; the calibration, the anchor, the trust and the correction read the chance after it as before. The NFL, NHL and MLB are untouched (no stat marked).
- **Next:** the deeper history itself (a third season in the NBA's cache: the hold above), which would let the gate judge the openers directly and the fit learn K and kd from openers with a prior.

### D51. The game lines' history bar: a line's real stake is earned by its segment (market, side, timing) or a named reason on the priced history, at its minimum edge, else the practice stake
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the lines round, after the forum replay's finding: the Bot's NFL line bets with a positive edge lose far past the vig, −13.4% flat at the close on 873, while its props sit near it).
- **Owner:** the Bot keeps recommending and betting game lines in every sport, but stops losing on them; work backwards from the winners; gates shrink, never stop; everything self-tuning on held-out evidence.
- **What was found (the gate's replay of the priced history, every DraftKings close since 2020 and every clean open, priced walk-forward by master with the context, each row's better side where its return at its own price is over 0, flat 1u; `scratchpad/lines2`):**

| Sport | Close picks | Flat ROI | ML | Spread | Total | Clean-open picks | Flat ROI | Beat the close |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | 874 | −11.6% | −15.0% (441) | −4.6% (253) | −13.1% (180) | 199 | −9.0% | 54% |
| NHL | 1,904 | −6.3% | −6.5% (656) | −8.3% (648) | −4.1% (600) | 2,145 | +1.2% | 36% |
| NBA | 949 | −0.1% | +1.4% (827) | −16.1% (60) | −5.4% (62) | 1,860 | +9.8% | 81% |
| MLB | 1,525 | −4.0% | −10.3% (694) | +1.9% (516) | +0.3% (315) | 1,472 | +3.4% | 44% |

  - **The term that makes the losing edges: the two books' disagreement at the close.** The lines' trust sits at 0 to 0.15 in every sport, so the trusted chance is the fair chance, Pinnacle's no-vig at the same line (`oddsapi.mjs` `linesFromEvent`, `fair: pin ?? own`), and a row's return at DraftKings' price is the gap between the books, no lean of the Bot's. Every side whose DraftKings price clears Pinnacle's no-vig, bet flat with no Bot chance at all: at the close NFL −15.0% on 401 (ML gaps of 4% or more 8-68, −47%), MLB −4.2% on 534, NHL −2.3% on 583, NBA +2.0% on 756 (its ML gaps of 4% or more −23%); the same snapshot time for both books (no stale-book artifact: 99.8% of NFL closes within 10 minutes). At the clean open the same rule wins: NBA +12.2% on 1,678 (beat the close 85%; 7 of 7 seasons positive on the moneyline), NHL +5.5% on 1,023 (5 of 6), MLB +5.9% on 724 (3 of 6), NFL −0.4% on 99; inside the live window (the 8-to-14-hour opens) NBA +11.7% on 1,254, MLB +5.3% on 321, NHL −2.5% on 585. These read no Bot chance, so the open's look-ahead (the replay's day-of context) isn't in them: the Book's opening line is soft where Pinnacle's is sharper, and by the close DraftKings has the news Pinnacle's snapshot hasn't. The "far over the Book" side is the late gap.
  - **Its shrink, measured and held as a price:** the fair as a logit blend of Pinnacle's no-vig (weight w) and DraftKings' own, w by held-out log loss: the best w flips season to season in every sport and market (NFL close ML 0 in 2022, 2024, 2025 and 1 in the rest), the losses differ by 0.0003 at most, and a smaller w keeps the biggest gaps, which lose most at the close. So the gap is shrunk where it loses by its stake, not its price: the timing segments below.
  - **No threshold alone makes a market net positive out of sample** (the minimum Kelly refit monthly: NFL ML −29.4% on 61, NHL spreads −10.1% on 383); the units by Kelly band aren't monotone (NFL [3,5) +8.7%, [5+) −13.6%).
- **The winners, worked backwards (the owner's framing):** every line's two sides at the close (labeled by the result against the close's no-vig) and at the clean opens (by the close against the open's no-vig), joined to the misses round's situations table (D34: rest, back-to-backs, games in 7 nights, time zones and body clock, road trips and home stands, off a blowout or a one-score game, streaks, divisional, start time, season phase, favorite and dog size, total size, line move, the books' gap, NFL wind; starter changes aren't in the table on player ids, so not tested), per season, the residual shrunk by 400 rows' worth, Benjamini-Hochberg q within each sport, timing and market, kept at q under 0.2 with the sign in 75% or more of 4+ seasons and positive flat units. What survived, ranked by units at the close:

| Pattern (the side) | Where | n | Residual | Flat ROI | Seasons |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| The books' gap: DraftKings over Pinnacle's no-vig | NBA ML, clean open | 1,655 | | +12.1% | 2020 +48%, 2021 +4, 2022 +13, 2023 +4, 2024 +43, 2025 +10, 2026 +21 (7/7) |
| The same | NHL all lines, clean open | 1,023 | | +5.5% | 2021 +7, 2022 +1, 2023 −5, 2024 +12, 2025 +15, 2026 +13 (5/6) |
| Rested against a back-to-back | NHL ML, close | 1,297 | +4.0 pts (q 0.09, 7/7) | +4.1% | 2021 +26, 2022 +9, 2023 +4, 2024 −1, 2025 +7, 2026 −1, 2027 +3 (5/7) |
| The same | NHL ML, clean open | 1,218 | +0.7 pts (q <0.001, 6/6) | +4.9% | 2022 +13, 2023 +5, 2024 0, 2025 +10, 2026 +1, 2027 +6 (5/6) |
| Two or more days more rest | NHL spread, open | 919 | | +7.6% | 2021 +6, 2022 +16, 2023 +14, 2024 +7, 2025 +12, 2026 −4 (5/6) |
| The same | NBA spread, close | 682 | +5.2 pts (q 0.19, 5/6) | +5.3% | 2021 −22, 2022 +13, 2023 +9, 2024 +8, 2025 +4, 2026 +6 (5/6) |
| 2+ zones east of its home clock | NBA ML, open | 1,076 | +0.3 pts (q 0.17, 6/7) | +4.3% | 5/7 |

  Everything else at q under 0.2 was the vig (sides whose residual is real but smaller than the price), a population-sized level, or a single season; MLB and the NFL have no pattern with positive units in most seasons (MLB's back-to-back side loses: every series is one).
- **Rule:** a game-line pick's real stake is earned by its **keys** on the priced history pooled with the live bets: its **segment** (`desk.mjs` `segmentOf`: the market, the side class the Book's own chance puts it in (the favored side or the dog, a total's over or under) and the **timing** (`timingOf`: `EARLY_HOURS` 6 or more before the start early, else late; the history's clean opens and closes by their own hours, a live bet by its placement)), and each **named reason** its side carries (`LINE_REASONS`, from the schedule alone, `scheduleRest`: the NHL's `restedVsB2b` (the other side played yesterday, this one didn't) on moneylines and spreads and `rest2` (two or more days more rest) on spreads, the NBA's `rest2` on both). A key is on at the smallest **minimum edge** of `EDGE_GRID` (0, 1, 2, 3, 5%) whose picks at that return or more are net positive past `BAR_Z` 1 standard error over `BAR_MIN` 40 picks' worth (`keyVerdict` on `edgeStats`). A pick bets at full stakes when its segment is on at its return, or a reason it carries is (`barGate`); any other pick, and one whose keys have no history, bets by its normal rules at the practice stake (`PRACTICE_UNITS`, a LIKE at most, `gatedPlacement` with the key's reason, "under the segment's minimum edge" where it is that; D1), never turned off: an edge is still needed, the pick is placed, graded, on the Book and on the Practice line (D3).
  - **The evidence:** the priced history's closes and clean opens as the shipped version prices them, each positive-edge pick flat at its own price, recency-weighted by season, summed by key and minimum edge (`gate.mjs` `lineBarOf`) and written by `gate.mjs --record` to `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/line-bar.json`; **pooled with the sport's live line bets placed since the epoch**, whole (`lineBar`: graded, not void, never a tracked row; the practice bets among them at their flat result; each by its own segment and the `reasons` it carried). The desk reads the file (`readLineBar`) and never writes it. Free to rise: a season of live bets weighs as much as the history.
  - **Soft and gated, never a weight on the chance:** a reason or a segment is only a key the record judges; one whose record isn't net positive beyond noise does nothing but leave the pick at the practice stake. The schedule's terms that move the chance stay D34's, at their ridge.
  - **With the CLV gate (`lineGate`):** both on a row (`bothGates`), either failing the practice stake with each failing one's reason. `state.json`'s `lineBar` shows every key's verdict and minimum edge, its record on the history and the live bets.
- **Measured walk-forward (each month's verdicts from the picks before it, `scratchpad/lines2/evalbar.mjs`, the Bot's own picks; the reasons not in this table):** the real bets after the rule against every positive-edge pick before it, flat 1u: NFL 0 real bets (every line the practice stake; before: −11.6% close, −9.0% open); NHL 529 (close 144 −0.1%, open 385 +2.8%; before −6.3% and +1.2%); NBA 960 (open ML 869 +10.0%, beat the close 86%; close ML 91 −31.8%: the late ML segment turned on in two months and lost; before −0.1% and +9.8%); MLB 300 (open 282 +4.0%, beat 56%; close 18 +13.9%; before −4.0% and +3.4%). The minimum edge adds +0.4 to +2.0 points over the same segments' practice rows. The Bot's picks carrying a reason: NHL `restedVsB2b` ML +24.8% on 144 at the open, +14.7% on 80 at the close; spreads +13.1% on 133 at the open; NBA `rest2` ML +4.3% on 100 at the open.
- **Measured and held the same round (each with numbers; none shipped):**
  - *The anchor's de-vig.* A power de-vig on Pinnacle's pair cuts the close's loss in the NFL (−14.1% to −9.3%), the NHL (−6.7% to −3.7%) and MLB (−4.2% to −3.8%) but turns the NBA's +3.6% on ML dogs into −1.0%, and every sport still loses at the close. `gate-history.mjs`'s `devig` is the gate's own yardstick, shared by both versions, so it can't be A/B'd there as it stands.
  - *The fair's blend of the two books* (above): no stable weight; the timing segments carry it.
  - *The NBA's body clock (2+ zones east, +4.3% at the open, 5/7)*: needs the time zones live on the lines path; next round, as a reason.
