  - *A sport-wide favorite or dog rule:* the side asymmetry isn't one-signed across sports (NFL ML dogs lose, NBA ML dogs win at the open; NFL spread dogs win, NHL spread dogs lose), so it is the segment's side class.
- **Re-measured on the look-ahead fix (D48, 2026-10-19 evening; the owner's follow-up):** the open keys above were measured on rows from before D48, where a Pinnacle open first seen after DraftKings' snapshot priced the fair. On the gate's rows now (Pinnacle's fair only where DraftKings' snapshot saw it; a row whose price read a later fact not bet there), the clean opens' positive-edge picks, flat 1u, before and after:

| Open key | Before D48 | After D48 | After, by season |
|---|---|---|---|
| NBA, the Bot's picks | 1,860 +9.8% (beat the close 85%) | 530 +4.7% (57%) | 2020 +2%, 2021 −49, 2022 −12, 2023 +8, 2024 +62, 2025 +16, 2026 −2 |
| NBA ML dogs | 1,004 +14.5% | 414 +7.5% | 2021 −44, 2022 −25, 2023 +7, 2024 +75, 2025 +16, 2026 +9 |
| NBA ML favorites | 753 +4.0% | 55 −6.0% | |
| NBA, the books' gap alone | 1,678 +12.2% (88%) | 419 +8.3% (59%) | |
| NHL, the Bot's picks | 2,145 +1.2% | 1,039 +4.0% (64%) | 2021 +6, 2022 −1, 2023 −2, 2024 +7, 2025 +12, 2026 +23 |
| NHL ML dogs / favorites | 370 +6.5% / 402 +1.3% | 135 −0.8% / 62 −2.3% | |
| NHL, the books' gap alone | 1,023 +5.5% (79%) | 725 +3.5% (73%) | |
| MLB, the Bot's picks | 1,472 +3.4% (65%) | 670 +1.4% (50%) | 2021 +10, 2022 +6, 2023 +12, 2024 +5, 2025 +16, 2026 −12 |
| MLB ML dogs / favorites | 431 +2.6% / 254 +1.9% | 344 −4.8% / 62 −13.4% | |
| MLB, the books' gap alone | 724 +5.9% (82%) | 284 +1.5% (57%) | |

  Most of the "soft open" was the leak: the share that beat the close fell from 79-88% to 57-73%, and two-thirds of the open picks are gone (their Pinnacle price came after DraftKings'). What's left is small and unstable by season; on the record (recency-weighted, walk-forward by key and minimum edge) **no open key earns full stakes in any sport**; the one key on today is the NFL's late overs. The timing split stays: it keeps the late keys' losses (the close's books' gap) from deciding an early bet's stake, and the live bets since the epoch earn the early keys their own record.
- **The bar's own place (`replay-record.mjs` `barPlace`, the follow-up for MLB):** under the bar MLB's replayed lines went from +2.5u on 873u staked to −13.7u. The cause: MLB's Kelly stakes win where its flat picks lose (its late ML dogs −7.8% flat, but the bigger edges won: the bar's practice stake on them gave up +11.7u). The minimum edge, the timing split and the season weights weren't it (the bar without each: −13.7u, −14.0u, −14.7u; judged on units at the Kelly stake −17.7u; "full stakes unless losing beyond noise" −6.9u). So the bar has to earn its own place, walk-forward by season: it stakes a season's lines only where, on every season before (unweighted), the lines as it staked them earned at least what they would have at full stakes; the first season, with nothing to judge on, is the bar's. Where full stakes out-earned it, that season's lines are at their full stakes and bands, and the record writes the verdict for what comes next into `line-bar.json` (`applies`, `place`), where the desk reads it (`barAside`; `barGate`'s `aside`: every line at its own stake, the reason on the bet). Lines only, the replayed units on the post-D48 rows (the champion / the bar / the bar with its place): MLB +2.5u / −13.7u / −5.9u (2021 the bar's, 2022 on full stakes: the first season is the whole remaining gap), NBA −21.4u / −5.6u / −5.6u (the bar every season), NHL −48.1u / −33.7u / −33.7u (the bar every season). A month-by-month version cost the NHL 15u and the NBA 7u (its first months judged on too little) and was held.
- **The gate (D22, `--candidate worktree --champion origin/master --refit --record`, the four team sports, 2026-10-19):** a staking policy, not a pricing change (every chance and projection the same): see the scorecard entries. `MODEL_EPOCH` not bumped (D4: staking policy).
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`BAR_Z`, `BAR_MIN`, `SIDE_CLASSES`, `EARLY_HOURS`, `TIMINGS`, `timingOf`, `segmentOf`, `EDGE_GRID`, `flatProfit`, `barStats`, `addStats`, `edgeStats`, `addEdgeStats`, `barOf`, `keyVerdict`, `LINE_REASONS`, `REST_CAP`, `scheduleRest`, `reasonsOf`, `reasonLabel`, `lineBar`, `barGate`, `bothGates`, `walkBar`, `readLineBar`, `barAside`), `gate.mjs` (`lineBarOf` with the clean opens and the reasons, the place in line-bar.json, `scoreVersion`'s `lineBar`, `recordScore`'s file), `replay-record.mjs` (`replayRecord`'s `barAt`: a replayed line bet whose keys hadn't earned their stake at its return by its own day, judged on the line picks of the months before it, at the practice stake, a LIKE, counted in the file's `timing.barred`; `barPlace`, the bar's own place by season, `flags.bar`), `run.mjs` `betGames` (`r.lineBar`, `scheduleRest(r.history)`, each pick's gate at its hours, return and reasons; the bet's `reasons`) and the state's `lineBar`, `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/line-bar.json`. Tests: `tests/model-line-bar.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D51.
- **Loops:** what could undo it, and how each is bounded.
  - The record (`gate.mjs --record`, a shipped version): rewrites the file from the whole priced history as that version prices it; never the ledger's rows. The live bets stay the live evidence, pooled at the desk every run.
  - The trust fit, the calibration, the residual and the anchor: price the chance; the bar reads the priced pick's flat result and moves no chance. A pricing change that moves the picks goes through the gate and a record, which refits the bar on the new picks.
  - The context fit (D34): sizes the schedule's soft terms in the chance; the reasons read the same schedule but only as keys of the bar, so a reason never counts twice in a price, and the bar never moves a term.
  - The CLV gate (`lineGate`): the sport's, beside it; neither can lift the other's practice stake.
  - The practice picks (D2) are the props'; a line at the practice stake is the bar's, tagged with its reason, in the record at 0.5u as a gate's cut is.
  - The bar's own place (`barPlace`): set only by a record from the replay's lines, the bar's stakes against full stakes on every season; the desk reads `applies` and never sets it, and the live bets don't move it (a record does). It can only hand a sport's lines back their own stakes, never raise one past them.
  - The replayed record (D47): each line bet staked by the bar walk-forward on the line picks before its month (`walkBar`), never by the bar of today, so the history is honest and a record rebuilds it with the shipped version.
- **Never:** a key at 0 units or tracked for the bar's sake; the desk writing the file; the bar reading a tracked row, a void, a prop or a bet from before the epoch; a reason as a weight on the chance; a bar that only shrinks (the live bets can lift it).

### D52. The confidence layer: bands and stakes earned on the record, LIKE until a tier earns it
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the confidence round: the owner's "the confidence algo is arguably as important as the pick algo, and merging them is the beauty of our app and where our edge will be"; and "do the history audit working backwards once again, to diagnose ways to improve our CONFIDENCE algo so that LOCKS > LOVES > LIKES > PASS").
- **What the history says (scratchpad confidence, BANDS.md and the audit; the forum round's bands table agrees):** over the whole priced history at the close, today's bands do not order by return. Pooled: LIKE −3.8% (8,540 rows), LOVE −2.2% (2,646), LOCK −6.9% (329); by sport at the close LOVE/LOCK against LIKE: NFL −2.7/−4.8 vs −3.9, NBA +0.8/−11.6 vs −1.7, NHL −8.1/−5.2 vs −5.0, MLB +3.4/−7.3 vs −7.2; on the LOVE and LOCK rows the Book's log loss beats the Bot's in every sport (LOCK pooled .6849 to the Bot's .6962). The bands rank the Bot's confidence in itself, not value: the biggest edges are the tails of its noise. The backwards audit (every fact known at placement, per sport, pass and kind, shrunk and at a 10% false discovery rate over 40 rows or more) finds almost nothing that separates the confident winners from the losers: in the NFL's close lines a price of +160 or longer loses (17% hit, −29%, q .08) and a market that has moved against the side loses (−49%, q .01); everything else is noise once the looks are counted.
- **The quality model (`confidence.mjs`):** logit P(the pick wins) = logit(the priced chance) + a core (the intercept and a slope on the priced lean, lightly penalized: how much of the priced edge the picks keep) + a ridge logistic on the facts at placement (the edge in points, Kelly and its square, the lean's sources (the Bot's own number, the anchor, the trust, the residual), the price band, favorite or dog, the vig, the side, the hours before the start, the trust, home, a prop's edge in standard deviations and its gap to the implied mean, the recent per-stat accuracy and the Book's recent shade, the stat or market, the line bucket, each with its own slope on the edge), fit walk-forward in snapshots every `EVERY_MONTHS` on the bets (`FIT_ON`) settled before the cutoff, recency-weighted by season, the penalty by the one-SE rule on the newest months with "nothing on top" as a candidate. A pick is scored by the newest snapshot whose newest moment is at or before the bet's moment: no look-ahead (pinned by a planted leak and a timestamp test). **Measured 2026-10-19:** the ridge stage is dropped by the one-SE rule in every sport (nothing beats nothing on top), and q (the stake's Kelly at the calibrated chance) has no ordering power on the bets: by fifths of q the hit rates and returns sit in no order in any sport or pass. Held out on the bets the calibrated chance about ties the priced one (NFL .6828 to .6830, NBA .6884 both, NHL .6556 to .6567, MLB .6667 both) and trails the Book where the Book is ahead of the price.
- **Rule:**
  - **LIKE is any positive edge at the priced chance, as before (D21): the Bot bets from any edge.** LOVE and LOCK are earned: a tier opens only where, held out over the priced history, the picks at or over its bar on q (with the owner's Kelly floors: LOVE at 2%, LOCK at 5% with a 55% chance, D21, never a LOVE under them) made a better flat return than the picks under it beyond noise (a clustered bootstrap by game at 99%, the bar above it clearing too, `TIER_MIN` 200 picks) and beat the close at least as often, or (the closing-line route, the AI pass's: a return gap takes thousands of picks a tier to show, the close converges in a few hundred) on `CLV_MIN` (300) opens or more beat the close `CLV_LEAD` (5 points) more often than the rest beyond noise with a better mean closing-line value and a return not under the rest's (`fitTiers`). **No tier is earned on 2026-10-19 in any sport: a two-band world, LIKE and the practice picks, until the record earns one.** The cap rides on the bet (`conf.cap`: 'medium' a LIKE at most, 'high' a LOVE at most) and on every board side (`board.json` `cap`, with `q`), the same rule as D46's caps: `picks.mjs levelOf` is `capBand(the rule's band, conf.cap)`, `capOf` the lower of the rule's cap and the layer's; the page holds a pick, a suggestion, a tile and the what-if to it at every price (`book-rows.ts`, `suggestions.ts`, `bet-why.ts` `most`), and the Algorithm tab's By Confidence reads the band as placed.
  - **The stake is Kelly on the calibrated chance** (`unitsOf`: `stakeFor` at the calibrated chance's return, the practice stake where it clears no edge), **blended into the priced chance by a weight the record sets** (`blendFit`, by evidence alone, the coordinator 2026-10-19: 0 to start; it moves only on `BLEND_NEW` (100) or more graded bets since the last record's `through`, one `BLEND_STEP` (0.25) to the neighbor, down or up, with the lower log loss on those new bets, or holds; never over 0 where the calibrated chance doesn't beat the priced one on them; `through` moves only when the weight is judged, so recording the same data again changes nothing, pinned by tests/model-confidence.test.mjs; a file from before the rule reads as no evidence, BLEND_START; the newest `BLEND_MONTHS` grid stays a readout, D8). Never over the stake the rules set, never under the practice stake; a bet a rule already holds (a guard, a gate, a suspect price, a practice pick, a role or research bet) keeps its stake. On the first record the weight is 0.25 in the NFL, the NBA and the NHL (the calibration gives back part of the overconfident tail on their newest year: held out on the bets the calibrated chance .6828 to the priced .6830 in the NFL, .6556 to .6567 in the NHL, a tie in the NBA), 0 in MLB.
  - **The file** (`apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/confidence.json`) is written by `gate.mjs --record` from the priced history as the version priced it (the closes and the props' opens; the lines' opens left out as a look-ahead), never by the desk, which reads it (`run.mjs` `withConfidence`). A sport without one (MMA) keeps the Kelly bands and stakes.
  - **Not an epoch change (D4):** the priced chance, the selection and the gates are untouched; the bands and stakes are policy on top of them.
- **The AI pass (Opus 5.5 on the audit packs, scratchpad confidence/review-*.md and review-synth.md, $1.35):** every sport's verdict is the same: no ordering holds out of sample, LOCK selects the residual's largest overstatements (the tails of noise: on the confident rows the trust kept is under 0.05 and the edge is the residual's alone), collapse to LIKE plus the practice picks, and earn a tier back on closing-line value from the clean prop opens rather than on returns (which need thousands of picks a tier). Its risk-reducing PASS rules on the lines (a price of +160 or longer, a market that moved against the side at the close, a residual that says no edge) and its 'give the residual a fitted trust' go to the lines and residual agents.
- **Gate (D22, `--refit`, against origin/master):** see the scorecard entry of 2026-10-19 (the pricing is unchanged, so every leg ties by construction; the layer's evidence is its own held-out record above).
- **Code:** `confidence.mjs` (`pickOf`, `features`, `fitWalk`, `snapshotFor`, `qualityOf`, `capOf`, `unitsOf`, `blendFit`, `fitTiers`, `fitConfidence`, `loadModel`), `gate.mjs` (the fit in `scoreVersion`, the file in `recordScore`), `gate-history.mjs` (`pCur`, `mu`, `sd`, `im` on the rows), `run.mjs` (`withConfidence`), `picks.mjs` (`levelOf`, `capOf`, the row's `cap` and `conf`), `board.mjs` (`sideQuality`, `q` and `cap` on every side), `tiers.ts` (`heldTo`), `book-rows.ts`, `suggestions.ts`, `bet-why.ts`, `model-desk.component.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-confidence.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D52.
- **Loops:** fit only by the gate's record on the history, never on the ledger (the live bets' stakes teach nothing back); it reads the priced chance and never moves it (the trust, the calibration, the residual and the anchor are upstream and read nothing from it); the practice picks, CHECKs and research read p as before; the blend's weight and the tiers move only by held-out evidence, a step a record; a cap or a stake here never turns a kind of bet off (D1, D2).
- **Never:** a LOVE or a LOCK under the Kelly floors; a tier opened on one look at the grid or on the ledger's few rows; the desk fitting or writing the file; a stake from the calibrated chance over what the rules allow or under the practice stake.

### D53. A bet placed under a band or stake rule changed since is the Bot's to price again before the start
- **Date:** 2026-10-19.
- **Owner:** "you can change your bet to fix it." The Bot placed Josh Hokit +205 (UFC 334) at 0.5u, a LIKE, while D23's old bar by the return (ev past 0.15) held it; D23 by the edge in points (27a9d205) no longer does, and the board reads that side higher. It's play money and the Bot's own bet.
- **Rule:**
  - **Every bet keeps the rules it was placed under** (`rules`, `desk.mjs rulesOf(BANDS)`): `CAP_RULES` (the cap rules' version, bumped by hand whenever the code of D23's suspect bar, D46's caps or D52's layer changes how a band or a stake is held: 1 the ev bar, 2 the edge in points), D23's numbers and the bands (D21). A bet from before the rules were kept reads as placed under today's, except one the old ev bar held (its `suspect` with no edge in points: `cap1`, `rulesAt`).
  - **Each run compares them** (`rulesChanged`): an open bet whose rules differ from today's, its game more than `REPRICE_BY` from the start and not published, is taken back and priced again like a bet whose inputs changed (`run.mjs repriceOpen`, the reason `rule changed (placed under ..., now ...)`; `mma-desk.mjs rulesBack`), at most `MAX_REPRICES` (6) times across runs, the same count as a re-price on news.
  - **At a fresh quote only (D30):** the bet is withdrawn only in a run holding a fresh price for it (MMA: this run's Odds API board; the other sports: the existing re-price path, which bets only at a live price); without one it stands as placed. Its new side, band and stake come from today's rules at that price; at a moved price it is placed at the new one; with no edge now it is tracked, and with a skip (the gap guard) it is not placed again.
  - **Both kept honestly (D47):** the new bet carries `repriced` (the count, the reason, when, and what it was: side, line, price, stake, intent, band, chance, placement time, rules); the run logs both prices and writes a changelog entry (`Open bet priced again: rule changed`). The wallet's You-vs-Bot duel reads the Bot's side as of the user's placement, unchanged.
- **Applied:** Josh Hokit +205 0.5u (`cap1`) is the one open bet in any sport placed under the old suspect rule (2026-10-19: NFL, NBA, NHL and MLB none); it is priced again on the first desk run with an Odds API price for the bout.
- **Gate (D22):** live-only placement code the replay can't see (the replay places every row once at today's rules): a tie by construction.
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`CAP_RULES`, `MAX_REPRICES`, `rulesOf`, `rulesAt`, `rulesChanged`), `run.mjs` (`RULES_V`, `repriceOpen`, `stampOf`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`RULES_V`, `rulesBack`, `withdrawnOf`, `runMma`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D53.
- **Never:** a re-placement at a stored or snapshot price; a bet withdrawn without a fresh quote in hand; a locked or published bet moved; the withdrawal dropped from the new bet's record.

### D54. The news log and the price log: what the desk knew, when, and what the Book's price was then; logging only
- **Date:** 2026-10-19.
- **Owner:** keep every news item the Bot sees once, with when it first saw it, so a replay can set "when did we know" against "when did the line move"; and every DraftKings (and Pinnacle) price the desk already fetches, each game's whole curve open to close, to find later the best time to bet by sport, market and side (bet now or wait). No new paid calls, no change to the betting.
- **Rule:**
  - **The news log** (`newslog.mjs`, `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/news-log/<YYYY-MM>.jsonl`, committed by the desk's commit step, append-only): every item the run read for a coming game, written the first run that saw it (`t`, UTC) and again only when what it says changes (`from` -> `to`), so an item seen run after run keeps its first time. Items: ESPN's injury report entries (and the ones the owner's note, the research or the game-day roster put in, by their own `src`), an entry cleared off ESPN's report (`to` null; not when the side's whole list is gone, a partial report), Sleeper's statuses inside the game's window (raw word, body part, practice day, depth), the Sleeper and Bluesky statuses put into the report ahead of ESPN's (`applied`, D49), every Bluesky claim the classifier made (any status and confidence, its post's link), the starters and lineups the run priced with (`timing.mjs inputsOf`: the NFL's quarterback, the NHL's goalie, MLB's starter and batting order, the NBA's unusual five, the posted inactives), the teams' news flags (headline), and MMA's bouts' state (on, cancelled, postponed, replaced). Each with the source's own time (`own`), sport, event, team, player (ESPN id and name where known), a short text, and `px`: the Book's prices the run had for the game at that moment (its lines, the player's props, Pinnacle's no-vig chance where the run had it; else the last board's lines with their time). Dedupe is against the month's file and the one before's (no state file).
  - **The price log** (`apps/<sport>/scripts/model/price-log/<YYYY-MM>.jsonl.gz`, a gzip member a run appended): every price the run read (the line log's rows, `linelog.mjs`: ESPN's board `e` and The Odds API's DraftKings `o` game lines, the props, the closes `c`; Pinnacle's no-vig chance at the Book's line as `pin:<market>`; MMA's moneyline and round total, `e` there ESPN BET's), a row only when its price changed since the key's last row, a header a game a month (its start, for the hours before it) and a heartbeat a game a run (`['h', t, game, n]`: the run saw n of its prices; the unwritten are as last written). The line log in the cache (`linelog.mjs`) is unchanged.
  - **Logging only:** run.mjs's last step (`logTheNews`, after the bets, picks, board and state), MMA's after its picks and board are built; it reads the run and changes nothing in it, asks no source, and never fails the run (warned and skipped). `--dry` counts and writes nothing.
- **Measured (2026-10-19):** a dry run (no Odds API price, late night, few props up) read 484 news items: NFL 172 (ESPN 47, Bluesky claims 122, the quarterbacks 2, a flag), NBA 126 (ESPN 99, Sleeper 10), NHL 136 (ESPN 92, the goalies 22, Sleeper 5, DailyFaceoff 2, a Bluesky status applied), MLB 0 (no games), MMA 50 bouts; and 290 prices (1.6 KB gzipped for the NFL). The board-seen files (every price the boards showed, by game) put the changes at about 260 rows a day for the NFL, 320 the NBA, 730 the NHL, 90 MLB, 30 MMA, about 9 in 10 of them props; with ESPN's and the Odds API's rows both, a heartbeat a game a run and gzip at about 8 bytes a row, the price log is about 1 to 3 MB a month for every sport together, and the news log (plain text, about 350 bytes an item) 1 to 3 MB. Props are kept whole (changes only), well inside the budget.
- **Gate (D22):** no bet, stake, price or input reads either log: a tie by construction, and the pinning test checks the run's facts are the same after both logs as before.
- **Code:** `newslog.mjs` (`newsItems`, `newItems`, `logNews`, `logNewsItems`, `mmaNewsItems`, `snapshotOf`, `pxFor`, `priceRows`, `newPriceRows`, `logPrices`, `readPriceLog`), `run.mjs` (`logTheNews`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`mmaPriceRows`, the logs after its board). Tests: `tests/model-newslog.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D54.
- **Never:** a bet, stake or input that reads either log on a live run (a fit may, by its own decision and gate); an API call made for a log's sake; an item's first time overwritten; a committed log rewritten rather than appended to.

### D55. Fast and paced: free game lines from ESPN, a watcher that kicks the desk on a change, and the $30 plan's credits paced to about 95% of the month, never over
- **Date:** 2026-10-20.
- **Owner:** the Bot should surface good bets as constantly and quickly as possible, as cheaply as possible. The Odds API stays on the $30 plan (20,000 credits a month, `oddsapi.mjs MONTHLY`) and must never go over; use as close to all of it as possible, with a safety reserve. And (2026-10-20, via the coordinator): "we have lots of game lines but no props for a lot of them, and I never get props till last minute."
- **Measured first (2026-10-20, 35 credits in all: 18 to compare game lines, 17 to probe the props):**
  - *ESPN's free scoreboard against The Odds API's DraftKings, the same minute, 41 games (NFL 14, NBA 14, NHL 11, MLB 1):* every spread and total line the same on 40 (the one apart, CIN @ BAL, ESPN's -3 against the API's -2.5, a stale side); every spread and total price the same on all but 5, each 1 to 2 cents off; the moneylines 1 to 3 cents off on most, every one the API's decimal price rounded (it sends 2 decimals; -180 comes back -179). So ESPN carries the Book's own lines, the American prices exact. ESPN has no Pinnacle, and the lines' fair chance is Pinnacle's no-vig (D51: the lines' trust sits near 0, so a line's edge is the books' gap), so a line is still bet only at a paid quote (D30, D51 unchanged); ESPN decides when that quote is bought.
  - *When the Book posts props (an empty answer is free: one market a game):* NFL Thursday's game posted 67 hours out and the London game 128, the Sunday slate not yet at 132 hours; NBA posted 42 to 46 hours out (none at 66); NHL none yet at 18 to 21 hours; the World Series' strikeouts 91 hours out. On the Book (board-seen.json, the games of the last week): NFL props first shown 25.9 hours before the start (the window), NBA 25.8 (the window), NHL 8.5, MLB 5.5; every game with lines had props in the NFL and NHL, 9 of 10 in MLB. The window held the NFL's and NBA's back a day or more.
  - *The months' spending (state.json's odds summary, 2026-10-09 to 10-19, the $30 plan):* lines 424, props 1,829 in ten days, about 225 a day, about 6,800 a month of the 20,000. The live budget held back 2,000 for the reserve and 6,000 for history no desk run buys (history is bought only by a local pull, whose usage file is that machine's), so the live features could never have used more than 60%. On the 5M plan's month (from 2026-10-19) the API's balance reads 2.67 million, and the old budget's allowance read 51,522 lines credits a day: nothing held the desk to 20,000, and the anytime touchdown's projection read the 5M plan's used count (2.3 million) and turned itself off.
  - *Actions minutes:* the repo is public (`gh api repos/soggust/SeasonRanker .private` false), so its Actions minutes are free; the desk's runs (the last 53) took 24 minutes on average, 10 to 40.
- **Rule:**
  - **Never over the $30 plan** (`oddsapi.mjs budgetOf`): what's left is the lower of the API's balance and `MONTHLY` less the desk's own tally of the cycle (`recordOn`: the cycle's and the UTC day's spending by feature, kept in the usage file, not the 3,000 calls it keeps). The tally is mirrored in the repo (`apps/nfl/scripts/model/odds-spend.json`, `SPEND_FILE`, written on GitHub's runs only and committed with the desk), so a lost cache reads it back (`withMirror`). `RESERVE` is 1,000 (5%: `TARGET` 95% of the month used); history keeps its own 6,000 cap but is no longer held back from the live features.
  - **Paced** (`budgetOf`, `spendable`): each day's lines and props allowance is what's left past the reserve over the days left, by share (lines 30%, props 70%), a busy day up to its burst (2 and 4 days' worth), and the cycle on a straight line: by any moment it may have spent at most its share of `MONTHLY - RESERVE` for the time gone plus `AHEAD_DAYS` (3), so a greedy week can't spend the month's end and the slow days bank credits for the NFL's Sundays. The cuts' order stays: the snapshot first (its own 300 a day, stopped while less than 2,000 over the reserve is left), then history, then props, then lines.
  - **The game lines bought on a change** (`run.mjs liveLines`, `oddsapi.mjs linesWant`, `.cache/model/lines-quoted.json`): the sport's paid lines call (3 credits, every game) is bought only when a due game with a market to bet wants it: never quoted; the Book's line on ESPN's board moved since its quote (half a point of line, or half a point of a side's no-vig chance; the API's rounding is a tenth to three tenths of a point, not a move); into its last chance since; or the quote stale (an hour while the month projects under its target, `refreshOn`, else 3 hours: Pinnacle moves without DraftKings). Between paid calls the board shows ESPN's line, as before.
  - **The props' window opens when the Book posts them** (`leagues.mjs TIMING` props: NFL 96 hours, NBA 48, MLB 48, NHL 30; the lines' window stays 26; the Book's preview window follows, `preview-props.ts`). An ask that finds none of the Book's props posted costs nothing, isn't counted toward `PROP_ASKS`, and is tried again `EMPTY_RETRY` (2 hours) on. D42's lineup risk still holds an early lean to the share that survives to the close, and D42's deferred first ask stands where the board has its own prices (NBA, NHL, MLB: the free board prices the suggestions). The never-asked games are asked first, every one the allowance can pay for, not only the soonest.
  - **The props' asks by value** (`timing.mjs propAsk`, `oddsapi.mjs TIERS`): each ask spends only its tier's share of the day's props allowance, so with credits short the higher go first: `first` (1, props soon after they're posted), `news` (0.9: an out on either side's report since the game's last ask, where the board has no prices: the teammates' lines move with it) and `owed` (0.9), `late` (0.8: once, inside the last chance, where the last ask held rows for the lineup risk: their stat's line moves by the close, D42) and `near` (0.8), `refresh` (0.6: mid-window, the last ask `REFRESH_H` 6 hours old, only while the month projects under its target). `PROP_ASKS` (6) still caps a game.
  - **The watcher** (`scripts/desk-watch.mjs`, the task "SeasonRanker desk watch", every 5 minutes while the owner is logged on, `scripts/desk-watch.ps1 -Register`): polls the free sources only (ESPN's scoreboards and injury lists, the D49 reporters' Bluesky posts; Sleeper stays once a UTC day, D49) and dispatches `model-desk.yml` (the owner's gh sign-in, the reason in the dispatch) when something is pending: a line moved half a point or a side's no-vig chance 2.5 points inside the lines' window, a game crossing into its window or last chance, an NFL kickoff 85 to 40 minutes out (the inactives), a new Out or Doubtful on ESPN's list for a team playing inside the window, or a reporter's post naming an out, a scratch or a goalie's start. An item is settled by any desk run that starts after it was seen. The rate (`dispatchDecision`): none while a run is queued or running, none within `COOLDOWN_MIN` (20) of the last run's start, at most `MAX_PER_DAY` (40) a UTC day. It never runs the bettor (D13) and never spends a credit. "SeasonRanker desk kick" (hourly) stays the backstop.
  - **Logged:** each run says why it bought the lines or not, the props' asks by why and the empty ones, and the month's projected credits against `MONTHLY`; the state's odds summary carries `projected`, `target` and `refresh`.
- **Projected (the $30 plan, in season):** lines about 4,000 a month (each sport's call when ESPN moves or hourly while due), MMA about 500, props about 7,500 (the NFL's every game asked when posted and refreshed to `PROP_ASKS`, about 4,500; the NBA's and NHL's asked to bet off their free boards, about 3,000), the morning snapshot about 4,500: about 16,500 against the 19,000 the pace allows; the straight line and the reserve hold any month under 19,000 whatever the demand (a greedy month simulated: 18,976).
- **Gate (D22):** the budget, the asks' timing and the watcher are live-only (a tie by construction, as D42); the props' window is read by the replay (`replay-record.mjs`, D47) at its next `gate.mjs --record`, which puts the history's NFL opens (48 hours, `OPEN_LEAD`) inside the window. `MODEL_EPOCH` bumped (the owner's rule: the bets are placed at other times).
- **Code:** `oddsapi.mjs` (`MONTHLY`, `RESERVE`, `TARGET`, `AHEAD_DAYS`, `TIERS`, `SPEND_FILE`, `mirrorOf`, `withMirror`, `budgetOf`, `recordOn`, `spendable`'s tier and straight line, `projectedMonth` on the tally, `refreshOn`, `linesSig`, `linesWant`, `LINES_FRESH`, `LINES_MOVED`, `usageSummary`'s projection), `run.mjs` (`liveLines`, `LINES_QUOTED`, `EMPTY_RETRY`, `outsKey`, the props' asks), `timing.mjs` (`propAsk`, `REFRESH_H`) (`apiProps` given its ask's tier), `leagues.mjs` `TIMING`, `preview-props.ts`, `scripts/desk-watch.mjs`, `scripts/desk-watch.ps1`. Tests: `tests/model-odds-pace.test.mjs`, `tests/model-desk-watch.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D55.
- **Never:** a month past `MONTHLY` by the desk's tally or the API's balance; the reserve spent; the API's used count read as the month's (a bigger plan's); a line bet at ESPN's price alone; the watcher running the bettor, spending a credit, or dispatching while a run is queued or running or inside the cooldown; an empty props answer counted as an ask.
### D56. A price is its competitor's: the archive's mirrored MMA opens repaired, the trust refit on them, the replay inside the live window, and one "beat the fair close"
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the coordinator's audit of the MMA record: Shevchenko +700 at the open where she closed -2000, Nunes +750 where she closed -909).
- **What was wrong:**
  - **The prices on the wrong fighter.** `odds-history.mjs` built the archive with each snapshot's moneyline and spread by that snapshot's home and away and no names, and the event kept one home and away (its `eventsSeen` key was the ordered pair). The Odds API lists an MMA bout's fighters either way round from one snapshot to the next, so an open seen in the other order sat on the other fighter. Measured on the committed rows: 702 of 7,106 MMA open-close pairs (9.9%, each book) swung more than 20 points across even, nearly every one a mirror image; of the bouts where both readings are clear (each side 65/35 or more), 338 of 2,200 snapshots (15.4%) flipped. Two books in one snapshot agree (5,634 to 6), so the flip is the API's listing, not a book's. This was most of D37's "soft early line": two weeks or more out the Book's open read 0.6797 (worse than a coin on lopsided bouts) because one open in seven was the other fighter's price.
  - **The replay bet MMA at any lead** (`replay-record.mjs` `inWindow`: MMA always in), where the live desk only asks four weeks out (`TIMING.mma` 672 hours, `mma-desk.mjs` `AHEAD_DAYS` 28): 1,343 of 1,491 replayed MMA bets were at the archive's first sighting, some five to eight weeks out, prices the live desk never sees.
  - **"Beat the close" was two measures blended.** The replay's (`beatOf`) compared DraftKings' raw closing price with the bet's, the vig in both, any improvement a beat, a moved line read by its points with the price ignored, ties dropped; the live bets' (`clv.mjs`) the no-vig CLV; the Algorithm tab's panel (`replay-history.ts` `addReplay`, `addLive`) averaged the two, and showed a share on any count (the NFL's 0% on 2 live bets).
  - **MMA's close window** was the team sports' 6 hours (`gate-history.mjs` `CLOSE_MAX`): the archive's close snapshot is a minute before a cluster's first start, a card's bouts start hours apart, so 35% of the replayed MMA open bets had no close to check.
- **Rule:**
  - **Every price on its competitor's name.** `build()` frames each snapshot's prices by the names (`framed`: a snapshot that listed the event's away side first has its moneyline swapped and its spread's point negated; totals have no side; names that aren't the event's pair are dropped, never guessed onto a side), and `eventsSeen` keys an event by its unordered pair and takes its last sighting's frame. `readHistory` puts each file's lines on the kept event's frame by that file's own event rows (a morning snapshot's names are its own).
  - **The bought archive repaired once** (`odds-history.mjs repair`, `repairFrames`, `framePrior`, `frameVerdict`; its record `apps/mma/scripts/model/odds-history/frames.json`, and it never runs twice): the raw snapshots aren't on disk, so the names are gone from those rows and the prices decide. Each event's snapshots are read from the latest back (the latest is the event's frame: its names are the last sighting's), each against the nearest later one framed; the two readings (the same fighter favored, or the other) need a move of ||x|-|y|| or |x|+|y| in the no-vig's log odds; the moves are double-exponential at the sport's own scale (`scale`: the mean move or its 99th percentile / ln 100, the larger: MMA 0.152), the flip share the sport's own (MMA 0.154), and a snapshot is put in the reading whose posterior odds pass `FRAME_ODDS` (99 to 1); one that passes neither is dropped (its totals kept), **never the nearer guess**. MMA: of 9,125 snapshots on 3,901 events, 429 mirrored back (571 rows: 560 opens, 11 closes), 1,399 dropped (1,943 rows: 1,912 opens, 31 closes, mostly near-even bouts where either reading is a plausible move). After it, no MMA open swings more than 20 points across even (1 of 5,199, a real move).
  - **Every sport audited** (the same test, open against close at the same book): NHL 0 and MLB 2 of 46,000 pairs; the NFL's 79 and the NBA's 32 are real moves (a quarterback out, a September look-ahead line, a re-used event id) except the 2020 bubble's (Utah-Lakers 2020-08-04, an exact mirror). A team's game is framed by its venue's home (every matched event's API home is ESPN's home by name), so only neutral sites can flip (`neutralOids`: games.json's neutral, the 2020 bubbles and MLB's 2020 neutral postseason, `NEUTRAL_WINDOWS`). **Measured and left:** read the same way, the four sports' neutral sites hold one mirrored NBA snapshot (4 rows) and about 150 near-even opens the rule would drop; not repaired (a four-sport re-record for one game), `build()` and the reader frame them by name from now on.
  - **MMA's close: the last DraftKings price within 12 hours of the bout** (`gate-mma.mjs` `MMA_CLOSE_MAX`). Measured on the repaired archive, the Book's no-vig log loss at its last price by its lead: inside 1 hour 0.5929 (1,918 bouts), 1 to 6 hours 0.5646 (101), 6 to 12 hours 0.5956 (653, as sharp as the bell), 12 to 24 hours 0.6540 (305, not a close). The team sports' 6 hours stand.
  - **The trust refit on the cleaned history** (D36's record, `gate.mjs --record`): the moneyline's early band 0.85 to 0.1, its late band 0.1 to 0, the mid 0 as before; the round totals unchanged (early 0.5 on 15 rows, mid and late 0). At the open two weeks or more out the Bot's own number now trails the Book's (raw 0.6296 against 0.6042 on 877 rows; mid 0.5899 against 0.5501): **D37's premise (the soft early line) doesn't hold on the cleaned history.** D37's machinery stays (the band's trust, the early key untapered, the four-week window): the fit decides, and at 0.1 the early lean is a tenth of the Bot's. No market turned off (D1): an MMA side still bets where its priced chance clears the vig, the card's practice picks as D2.
  - **The replay bets MMA only inside the live window** (`inWindow` for every sport: the open's lead at most `TIMING[sport].lines`): an open first seen further out waits for its close, as the live desk would.
  - **Beat the fair close: one definition** (`replay-record.mjs` `fairBeatOf`, `replay-history.ts` `fairCloseOf`): a bet placed before the close beat it when its own price has a positive expected return at the close's no-vig fair chance of its side (Pinnacle's where DraftKings' snapshot saw it, else DraftKings' own, D48; a live bet's `clv.mjs` `ev`), a close at the bet's own line only (a moved line has none; never a carried guess); no ties dropped (an expected return of 0 or less is no beat). The file carries each tally's count (`beatN`) and mean expected return (`clv`), the day buckets' `clv` column; a file from before it (no `clv`) isn't joined. The panel shows the share from `BEAT_MIN` (30) such bets, "—" under, the count and the mean return on its hover (`beatText`, `beatTip`). The live tiles (`desk-math.ts` `tally`, clv.mjs's no-vig CLV) are unchanged.
  - `MMA_START` bumped to the push (D4: the trust moved, so the league's price moved). `MODEL_EPOCH` not bumped: the team sports' pricing is unchanged (their beat-the-close readout only).
- **Measured (the replayed records, `gate.mjs --record`; before → after):**

| Sport | Bets | Record | Units | ROI | Beat the close (before: raw price) | Beat the fair close (after) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MMA | 1,491 → 594 | 688-778-25 → 184-400-10 | +290.7u → -95.2u | +11.9% → -15.0% | 62% of 791 | 31.6% of 282, average return at the fair close -5.4% |
| NBA | 3,597 (same) | 1601-1994-2 | -40.5u | -1.5% | 63% of 321 | 49.6% of 345, +1.4% |
| MLB | 1,278 (same) | 572-697-9 | -8.4u | -1.0% | 64% of 384 | 35.0% of 432, -1.4% |

  The NFL (its replay all at the close: no open bet to check) and the NHL keep their files of c933c3d2 till their next record (a record here needs --refit, about half an hour): their beat-the-close share reads "—" on the panel meanwhile (a file from before D56 isn't joined).

- **Gate (D22, `--candidate worktree --champion origin/master mma --record`):** SHIP, ties everywhere (both versions read the repaired archive and the 12-hour close: the harness's; the versions differ in the replay's window and beat and nothing priced). On the same cleaned rows the champion's replay (no window) was 209-434-11, -74.3u on 654 bets; the candidate's 184-400-10, -95.2u on 594. The record was HELD (replay keys 4,592 to 4,497: the dropped opens) and written with `--accept-inputs` naming this repair. NBA and MLB re-recorded with `--score-only worktree --record` for the new readout (their bets and units unchanged; only the replay files committed).
- **Live (2026-10-20, a dry run):** MMA's trust by band ml early 0.1 / mid 0 / late 0 (was 0.85 / 0 / 0.05), totals early 0.5 / mid 0 / late 0. A bout four weeks out is priced at a tenth of the Bot's lean: Amanda Nunes +164 (the Bot 41.4%, the Book 36.7%) has no edge at it; Josh Hokit +205 about +2% expected return on a lean under a point, under MMA's 3-point bar: a practice pick at most. Their open bets (1.5u and 3u LOVE, placed at the old trust) stand as placed: D53 reprices a changed band or stake rule, not a refit trust; the owner's call.
- **Coverage (the owner's complaint: lines on Nunes and Hokit four weeks out, none on a card two weeks out):** not ours. The Odds API's own list (the morning snapshot of 2026-10-19, kept, and the desk's run of 2026-10-20: "34 due bouts not on The Odds API's board by name") carries UFC 333 (10-24, its three early prelims at Pinnacle alone, so not on the Book) and UFC 334's two main events (Nunes, Hokit), and nothing of the 10-31 and 11-07 Fight Nights or UFC 334's other ten bouts. ESPN lists them all (the desk reads 28 days ahead) and the desk asks for the whole board every run (a far bout the API lacks rests a day). They come onto the Book the run the API lists them. No paid call was made to check.
- **Code:** `odds-history.mjs` (`eventsSeen`, `framed`, `build`, `readHistory`, `FRAME_ODDS`, `FRAME_CLEAR`, `logOddsOf`, `framePrior`, `frameVerdict`, `repairFrames`, `NEUTRAL_WINDOWS`, `neutralOids`, `framesFile`, `repairArchive`, the `repair` command), `apps/mma/scripts/model/odds-history/*.jsonl.gz` and `frames.json`, `gate-mma.mjs` (`MMA_CLOSE_MAX`), `replay-record.mjs` (`inWindow`, `fairBeatOf`, the tallies' `clv`, `DAILY_COLS`), `replay-history.ts` (`BEAT_MIN`, `fairCloseOf`, `beatText`, `beatTip`, `addReplay`, `addLive`, `dailyTally`), `model-desk.component.*` (the cell), `desk-help.ts` (`historyBeat`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`MMA_START`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D56 (a mirrored snapshot keyed by name and the repair's three verdicts; the replay's window; the definition and the dash under 30), `tests/book-replay-history.test.mjs`, `tests/model-replay-record.test.mjs`.
- **Never:** a price put on a side by its position alone where the names say otherwise; an archived snapshot framed by the nearer guess where the prices don't decide it; the repair run twice; a replayed bet at a price the live desk's window never asks for; a beat read off the raw price with the vig in, or a share shown on fewer than 30 bets.

### D57. The slate's practice picks are tracked, not staked
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner: "is 'for practice' fucking us?... make them tracked only").
- **What the history says:** the replayed slate practice picks (D2/D25's best of the rest, an edge before the vig, 0.5u) lost about the vig in every sport: NFL 2,475 −71.0u (−5.7%), NBA 2,799 −58.1u (−4.2%), NHL 5,493 −85.1u (−3.1%), MLB 9,497 −192.0u (−4.0%), MMA 264 −0.2u. They were already out of the record (PRACTICE_SPLIT), so the record by sport doesn't move; the stake they cost does. Their reason, data to learn from, is kept: a tracked row is graded and learned from like a bet (D1's TRACK), and the price and news logs (D54) now collect the rest for free.
- **Rule:** `desk.mjs PRACTICE_STAKED` false: no slate pick is forced into a bet (run.mjs's props, mma-desk.mjs's moneylines), so the best of the rest stay tracked rows at no stake like every other row short of the vig; the replay (replay-record.mjs) places none, they stay among its passes. PRACTICE_N, practiceTop and the Practice line stay for a later turn back on. The other "practice stake" (D51's bar, D24's gates: a real edge held to 0.5u until its market earns more) is unchanged: gates shrink, never stop.
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`PRACTICE_STAKED`), `run.mjs` (the props' `forced`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`forced`), `replay-record.mjs` (the practice loop). Tests: tests/model-replay-record.test.mjs, tests/model-decisions.test.mjs D57.

### D58. Reason-based confidence tiers: a LOVE needs a proven reason, a LOCK two; the Kelly size is one candidate among them
- **Date:** 2026-10-19 (the owner, unhappy with D52's outcome: every bet a LIKE because LOVE and LOCK by Kelly size never beat LIKE).
- **Why the Kelly tiers fail:** a Kelly size measures how far the Bot disagrees with the Book, and a bigger disagreement is more often the Bot missing something (adverse selection). Confidence has to come from evidence that a pick wins, not from the size of the disagreement.
- **Rule (`reasons.mjs`):**
  - **Every bet carries its reasons** (`reasonsFor`, from what the Bot knows at placement alone): the schedule's rest (`restedVsB2b`, `rest2`: the NHL and NBA lines, D34/D51), the timing (`cleanOpen`: bet early at a clean open, D42; `openGap`: the Book's opening price past the sharp book's no-vig seen by its own snapshot, D48/D51), the line's segment (`seg:<market>:<fav|dog|over|under>:<early|late>`: the NFL's late overs among them), a prop's stat and side (`stat:<stat>:<side>`), the projection a full SD past the line (`sd1`), and the Kelly size itself (`kelly2`, `kelly5`: the old bands as one candidate among the rest). Each has a family (rest, timing, segment, stat, projection, size). Not replayable and so not in yet: the news in hand (D49), the residual's own confidence, the NBA's body clock (needs the time zones on the lines path).
  - **A reason is proven** in a sport's market (`walkReasons`, `verdictOf`) where, on the replayed record's bets settled before the moment (flat 1u, each season `DECAY` 0.75 of the next from the newest one with 10 picks), its picks made money, led the rest of the market's picks by `Z_LOVE` 1.645 standard errors on `R_MIN` 60 picks or more, led in `SEASON_SHARE` 60% of the seasons with 10 of its picks (two at least), and beat the close at least as often where both have 30 opens. **Strong:** `Z_LOCK` 3 SEs, 8 points, and the close beaten 5 points more often.
  - **The tiers** (`tierOf`): LIKE any positive edge (D21, unchanged: the Bot bets from any edge); LOVE a proven reason; LOCK two proven reasons of different families, or one strong. Never a LOVE or a LOCK under the owner's 2% Kelly floor (`LOVE_FLOOR`). The rules' caps stay (a suspect price, a barred line, a practice, role or research pick: a LIKE at most). This replaces D52's quality cap on the band; D52's stake on the calibrated chance stays, under the tier's cap.
  - **The tiers' own place** (`placeTiers`, walk-forward by month, as D51's bar earns its own): LOVE shows only where the would-be LOVEs and LOCKs before that month beat the would-be LIKEs by flat return and by beat-the-close (`PLACE_MIN` 40 of them), LOCK only where its would-be bets (20) beat the LOVEs the same way. So LOCK > LOVE > LIKE is earned on the record, never assumed.
  - **Stakes** follow the tier (`TIER_UNITS`): the desk's stake (shrunk Kelly on the calibrated chance) held to 1u a LIKE, 2u a LOVE, 3u a LOCK: a big Kelly with no proven reason stakes as a LIKE.
  - **The Book:** each pick carries `tier` and its proven `reasons` with their records (picks.json; `reasonText`: "NHL rested against a back-to-back (moneyline): 5 of 7 seasons, +4.1% on 300"); the K popover lists them under "Why the Bot is confident"; the row's band and the what-if read the tier at every price (`book-rows.ts tierBand`, `bet-why.ts priceAt`).
  - **MMA is left as it is** (its Kelly bands, D37): `gate-mma.mjs` and `mma-desk.mjs` don't load the module. Not because its bands are proven: its replayed record was under repair this round (D56: mirrored opens put prices on the wrong fighter; prices past the live window), so nothing here keys on MMA's band record; the reason tiers go to MMA once it is judged on its own clean rows.
  - **The file** `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/reasons.json` (each market's reasons with their records and verdicts, the place) is written by `gate.mjs --record` from the replay's walk, never by the desk, which reads it (`run.mjs withTier`).
  - **Not an epoch change (D4):** bands and stakes on top of unchanged chances.
- **Measured (2026-10-19, the replayed record on a rebuilt cache, every bet walk-forward): no reason is proven in any team sport, so no LOVE or LOCK shows there: every team-sport bet stays a LIKE.** D34's and D51's patterns were measured on every side of every game; on the Bot's own placed bets (a positive edge at its own placement, post-D48) they are few and unstable: NBA `restedVsB2b` ML +17.5% on 98 but leading in 1 of 5 seasons; NBA `openGap` ML +5.8% on 345 (3 of 6 seasons); NHL `openGap` ML −2.3% on 166, spreads −0.2% on 332, totals +10.3% on 93; NHL `restedVsB2b` spreads −11.4% on 67; NFL late overs +12.2% on 48 (under 60); NFL props' receiving unders +6.1% on 530 and +5.5% on 409 (1.3 SEs); MLB `kelly2` ML +10.0% on 78 (0.7 SEs); the Kelly size elsewhere loses (NFL props −4.0% on 574, NHL totals −15.4% on 161). At a looser bar (1 SE, half the seasons) a few reasons prove themselves for a while and their would-be LOVEs and LOCKs then lose held out (NFL would-be LOVE −7.9% flat on 220 against LIKE −1.9%; NHL would-be LOCK −46% on 16): the place check holds them, as it should. Before (D52) and after, the recorded replay on the rebased code (n, record, units ROI, beat the close): NFL 3,240 1610-1620-10, −0.3% → −0.6% (the 1u LIKE cap on the pre-snapshot Kelly bands: LOVE −7.4u, LOCK −6.8u, the big LIKEs +8.1u); NBA 3,597 1601-1994-2, −1.5% → −1.2%, beating the fair close (D56) 50% of 345 both; NHL 1,809 794-989-26, −3.4% both, 46% of 704; MLB 1,278 572-697-9, −1.0% → −1.5% (its 89 pre-snapshot LOVEs, +9.5u, now LIKEs at 1u), 35% of 432; +2.3u in all. LIKE beats PASS (flat −3.9% to −5.3%) in every sport. A tier opens by itself at a record once a reason earns it.
- **Gate (D22, `--candidate worktree --champion origin/master --refit`, the four team sports, on 53d42ba1):** SHIP, ties everywhere (no chance or projection moves); recorded with `--score-only worktree --refit --record` on the rebased code. `CAP_RULES` 3 (D53): an open bet placed under cap 2 is priced again at a fresh quote.
- **Code:** `reasons.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs` (the tiers after the bar's place; `flags.tiers`; `reasonsFile`), `gate.mjs` (the module, `reasons.json` in `recordScore`), `gate-history.mjs` (`pin` on the line rows), `run.mjs` (`readReasons`, `withTier`), `picks.mjs` (`levelOf`, `capOf`, the row's `tier` and `reasons`), `book-rows.ts`, `bet-why.ts`, `book-page.component.*`. Tests: `tests/model-reasons.test.mjs`.
- **Loops:** the record writes the file from the replay's walk; the desk and the page read it; nothing here moves a chance, a pick or a soft term; a reason is a key the record judges, never a weight (D51); a band or a cap never turns a bet off (D1).
- **Never:** a LOVE or a LOCK under the 2% Kelly floor; a reason judged on a pick settled after the bet's moment; a tier shown before its own place is earned; the desk writing the file.

### D59. MMA's fair chance calibrated for the favorite-longshot bias: measured, built, held for the owner (off)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the algorithm round's first item: the MMA replay 594 bets −95.2u, LOCK 22 19-3 +20.7u, LOVE 164 43-117 −61.8u, LIKE 408 122-280 −54.1u, while every skipped side flat lost only 2.7%: the picks were worse than random).
- **What the history says (the cleaned archive, D56; `scratchpad/mma`):** the Bot's MMA moneyline trust is near 0 (early 0.1, mid and late 0), so its chance is the Book's fair: Pinnacle's no-vig where DraftKings' snapshot saw it. That no-vig carries the favorite-longshot bias: at the close Pinnacle's 0.1-0.2 lands 0.097 (said 0.162), 0.2-0.3 lands 0.213 (0.254), 0.3-0.4 lands 0.329 (0.351), 0.7-0.8 lands 0.787 (0.746), 0.8-0.9 lands 0.903 (0.838); the logit slope of the result on the fair is 1.15 on the whole history, about 1.0 in 2020-21 and 1.10 to 1.16 from 2022 on. So every DraftKings dog price past Pinnacle's no-vig read as an edge: 507 of the 594 replayed bets were moneylines, 434 of them dogs (hit 24.0% where the Bot said 32.5%), −103u flat.
- **The fix (`gate-mma.mjs` `fitFairCal`, `calFair`, `calibrateRows`; `mma-desk.mjs` `pricedFair`, `fairCalOf`):** the fair the Bot prices a moneyline from is logit(fc) = b0 logit(the fair) + b1 logit(DraftKings' own no-vig), a soft ridge toward the raw fair (b = [1, 0], `FAIR_CAL_LAM` 100 rows' worth), fit walk-forward on the closes over before each row's month in the gate (the record's fit on every close goes to `trust-prior.json` `ml.fairCal` for the desk). The trust is fit at the calibrated fair (the rows' `fc` and the close's `qc`). The raw no-vig stays the Book's benchmark: the beat-the-fair-close (D56), the Book's log loss, the bet's stored `fair` and its CLV. Fit on 2,668 closes: b = [1.054, 0.128] (slope about 1.18).
- **Measured, the replay (`gate.mjs --candidate env:MMA_FAIR_CAL=1 --champion origin/master mma`):**

| MMA replay | Bets | Record | Units | ROI | Beat the fair close (avg return) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Current | 594 | 184-400-10 | −95.2u | −15.0% | 31.6% of 282 (−5.4%) |
| Calibrated, no beat bar | 1,036 | 655-369-12 | +1.7u | +0.2% | 36.9% of 509 (−2.3%) |
| Calibrated, with D60's bar | 1,036 | 655-369-12 | −24.1u | −3.9% | 36.9% of 509 (−2.3%) |

  By band (no bar): LOCK 27 21-5-1 +20.1u, LOVE 237 134-100-3 +7.9u, LIKE 772 500-264-8 −26.3u: LOCK > LOVE > LIKE, LIKE still red. The Bot now mostly bets favorites (−150 and shorter about +2% to +7% flat) and still loses on the dogs it keeps (0.3-0.5 implied −37u flat on 129).
- **Gate: HOLD.** The history's legs: results log loss 0.6173 → 0.6154 (−0.0019, a tie at 95% −0.0039 to +0.0001), against the close a tie (−0.0001), the top picks' CLV −0.0385 → −0.015 (better beyond noise), hit 41% → 78%. Held by the live ledger's close leg alone: 10 graded MMA bets since 10-08, close log loss 0.6197 → 0.6213 (+0.0015, 95% +0.0002 to +0.0030), past D22's `CLOSE_LEG_TOL` (0.0005): a calibration away from the raw close is penalized by construction on a leg measured against the raw close (D22's exception), and those 10 rows are the old Bot's own dog picks. Not the exception (past the tolerance), so **the owner's call**: the code ships off (`FAIR_CAL_ON` false; `MMA_FAIR_CAL=1` turns it on for the gate or the desk), the record not written with it, `MMA_START` unchanged.
- **D56's dropped snapshots:** the repair dropped 1,912 opens, mostly near-even bouts. At the close, bouts whose favorite sits at 0.5-0.6: 607 of 832 have no open left; at 0.6-0.7, 104 of 961; past 0.7, 1 of 875. So the replay's opens are skewed to lopsided bouts (where the bias is largest), and the near-even bouts are bet at the close instead. The calibration is fit on the closes (31 dropped), so it isn't biased by the drop; the open sample is.
- **To ship (the owner):** set `FAIR_CAL_ON` true, bump `MMA_START` (D4: the price moves), `gate.mjs --candidate worktree --champion origin/master mma --record` (the record writes `ml.fairCal`).
- **Code:** `gate-mma.mjs` (`FAIR_CAL_ON`, `FAIR_CAL_LAM`, `fairCalOn`, `fitFairCal`, `calFair`, `calibrateRows`, `historyRows`' `fd`, `finishByLead`'s `fc`/`qc`, `trustBets`, `ledgerRows`' `fairOf`, `scoreMma`'s `ml.fairCal`), `mma-desk.mjs` (`setFairCal`, `fairCalOf`, `pricedFair`, `pickFor`, `boardSidesOf`, `fitMmaTrust`'s live rows). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D59.
- **Never:** the raw no-vig replaced as the benchmark (the beat-the-fair-close, the Book's log loss); a month's calibration fit on its own closes or later ones; the desk fitting the calibration (the record writes it).

### D60. The replay's guard and the beat-the-close bar (Gemini's): more units at worse prices is rejected; a market under 52.5% beating the fair close stakes the practice stake
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the algorithm round's second item).
- **Rule:**
  - **The gate's guard** (`gate-score.mjs` `replayGuard`, `sportVerdict`'s `replay`; `gate.mjs compareScores`): a candidate whose replayed record makes more units than the champion's while its bets beat the fair close less often (by more than `GUARD_TOL`, half a point) or at a lower average expected return at the fair close (`GUARD_EV_TOL`, 0.001) fails its sport, read where both replays have `GUARD_MIN` (30) bets with a fair close. Fewer units isn't this guard's business. Not the owner's exception (D22): a failed guard is never "only the close leg".
  - **The bar** (`desk.mjs` `BEAT_BAR` 0.525): a market earns more than the practice stake only while its bets beat the fair close at least 52.5% of the time. Live (`lineGate`, `propGate`): the line bets and each prop stat's, the last `LINE_GATE_N` (200) graded with a close since the epoch, from `LINE_GATE_MIN` (40): a CLV of 0 or more and the share past the bar, else the practice stake (D1: never off; under 40, full stakes as before). In the replay (`replay-record.mjs` `beatBar`, walk-forward): each bet's market (a line's market, a prop's stat), its earlier bets settled by the bet's moment with a fair-close check, from 40 of them over the last 200: under the bar the bet is held to the practice stake and a LIKE (`beatHeld`). Bets at the close have no check and don't count. MMA's replay reads it too (its live desk has no CLV gate).
  - **Not an epoch change (D4):** stakes on top of unchanged chances.
- **Measured (the replayed records, `gate.mjs --candidate worktree --champion origin/master`, every sport: SHIP, ties everywhere; recorded with `--score-only worktree --record`):**

| Sport | Bets | Record | Current | With the bar | Beat the fair close (unchanged) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | 3,597 | 1601-1994-2 | −28.2u | −21.0u | 49.6% of 345 |
| NHL | 1,809 | 794-989-26 | −31.2u | −29.9u | 46.3% of 704 |
| MLB | 1,278 | 572-697-9 | −12.4u | −15.4u | 34.9% of 432 |
| MMA | 594 | 184-400-10 | −95.2u | −58.4u | 31.6% of 282 |
| NFL | 3,017 | 1467-1543-7 | −40.4u | −40.4u | all at the close: no check |

  No market in any sport reaches 52.5% (the fair close's no-vig shares the favorite-longshot bias, D59: a dog's "beat" is easier than it is real, a favorite's harder). The NFL's record is not re-recorded: its bets are all at the close, so the bar can't touch them (the candidate's replay equals the champion's), and this machine's NFL cache lacks the weather facts the last record had (the record guard held it); its committed file stands.
- **Code:** `gate-score.mjs` (`replayGuard`, `GUARD_MIN`, `GUARD_TOL`, `GUARD_EV_TOL`, `sportVerdict`'s `replay`), `gate.mjs` (`compareScores`), `desk.mjs` (`BEAT_BAR`, `lineGate`, `propGate`), `replay-record.mjs` (`beatKey`, `beatBar`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D60.
- **Loops:** the live gates read the ledger since the epoch each run, the replay's bar its own walk; neither moves a chance or turns a bet off.
- **Never:** a market's bets set to 0 by the bar; a replayed bet held on a check settled after its moment; a version that wins units at worse prices shipped.

### D61. The intraday history: the Book's and the sharp book's lines between the open and the close, and the desk's credits freer till the plan resets
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner's go: spend the 5M plan's leftover credits this cycle, keep 50,000 back; the plan resets to the $30 plan about 2026-11-09).
- **The pull (`intraday.mjs`, local only):** The Odds API's historical game lines, DraftKings and Pinnacle, at 24, 12, 6, 3 and 1 hours before each start (MMA also 168 and 72), the last two seasons a sport (`FROM`); one call a sport at each whole hour a game's offset lands on, every game starting in the next `MAX_AHEAD` hours kept from it. The props the same way, one call an event and hour (`PROP_OFFSETS`: NFL 24/12/6/3, NBA 24/6/3/1, NHL and MLB 6/3/1; the archive has the open and close already). Stored as change-only rows beside the archive: `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/odds-history/intraday/<season>.jsonl.gz` (I rows, the archive's L shape, framed on the archive's home by the names, D56; S rows the snapshots) and `props-<season>.jsonl.gz` (Q rows, the archive's P shape; T rows the snapshots). The raw answers stay in the puller's cache (`INTRADAY_WORK`).
- **The curve (`intraday-curve.mjs`, `intraday.mjs curve`):** the expected return at the fair close (D56) of DraftKings' price taken N hours out, every side and the "gap" sides (DraftKings past Pinnacle's no-vig at the same snapshot, the Bot's trust-0 line bets; a spread or total where the books' lines differ, the side with the better number), with the results.
- **Credits freer (`oddsapi.mjs` `BIG_UNTIL` 2026-11-09, `BIG_MONTHLY` 150,000, `monthlyAt`):** till then the desk's cycle cap is 150,000 (the API's real balance still the lower bound, as D55); the window's spending in the same cycle (`u.big`, recordOn) is left out of the tally after it, so the $30 plan's month starts whole. A lost usage file counts it (under-spends, never over). D55's rule otherwise unchanged.
- **Gate (D22):** nothing priced reads the intraday files or the window (a tie by construction).
- **Spent (2026-10-20):** the lines 611,080 credits (NBA 151,620, NHL 164,610, NFL 39,510, MLB 198,060, MMA 57,280; 21,324 snapshots); the props 1,507,170 (NBA 419,010, NFL 257,830, NHL 248,610, MLB 581,720); 2,118,250 in all, 551,664 left on the API (over the 50,000 reserve; the props pull ran with a 300,000 floor). Committed: 21 MB (lines 4.3 MB, props about 17 MB).
- **What the curve says (the moneylines' gap sides, the expected return at the fair close; n, beat share):**

| Hours out | NBA | NHL | NFL | MLB | MMA |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 24 | +2.2% (45, 69%) | +3.4% (13) | +1.0% (81, 58%) | — | +1.2% (24) |
| 12 | +0.8% (158, 52%) | +2.3% (27) | +3.8% (61, 70%) | −0.5% (38) | +2.1% (23) |
| 6 | +2.1% (156, 59%) | −1.4% (14) | +3.4% (59, 75%) | −0.8% (73) | +3.2% (24) |
| 3 | −0.7% (100, 44%) | −1.2% (12) | +4.1% (63, 79%) | −0.7% (96) | +1.0% (24) |
| 1 | +0.5% (175, 50%) | −1.7% (19) | +4.2% (77, 78%) | −0.1% (111) | +5.3% (23) |

  Every side taken at the Book's price runs about the vig (−4% to −4.5%) at every hour; prices taken earlier beat the close more often (NBA 28% at 24 hours, 11% at 1). The NBA's and NHL's gaps hold their value 6 hours and more out and are gone by 3: bet them when first seen, never wait. MLB's gaps never had value. The NFL's dog gaps "beat" the fair close 70-80% at every hour yet lost on the results (−16% to −40% ROI on 60-80 each): the fair close's no-vig overrates dogs (D59's bias), so a dog's beat of it is not an edge. Spreads and totals: the books' lines differ on about a fifth of games; the side with the better number gains 0.1 to 0.6 points on the close and still loses about the vig. **The props run the other way:** a player line the Book shows past the sharp no-vig is worth more near the start than far out (NFL gaps about −1% to −3% at 24 hours, +0.5% to +1.3% at 3; NBA about −2% at 24 hours to −0.8% at 1; MLB strikeouts and outs −0.8% at 6 hours, +0.9% at 1; the 1-hour reading sits near the close, so part of its beat is mechanical): a prop's early number carries the role and news risk D42 prices. So lines early, props late. **No timing rule changed:** the replay places at the archive's open or the close and can't yet place at these hours; the curve is the evidence for the next round (an intraday placement in the replay, and D59's calibration for the team sports' fair).

### D62. The stacked fatigue reason: a candidate the record judges (unproven)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the algorithm round's sixth item).
- **Rule (`reasons.mjs` `fatigue`, `fatigueOf`, `FATIGUE_TAIL`, `FATIGUE_GAP`; `desk.mjs` `withTravel`; `gate.mjs` and `run.mjs` put the context's travel on the schedule):** a side's index is a back-to-back 1, a thousand miles since its last game 1, a time zone moved east a half, a thousand meters climbed 1. A line pick (moneyline, spread; NBA, NHL, NFL) carries the reason where the other side's index tops its own by the sport's 85th percentile of the games' gaps (NBA 1.6, NHL 1.6, NFL 2.1, measured on the schedules) and the Book's price is within 2 points of the sharp fair (it hasn't moved for it). Family `rest`: never a LOCK's second reason beside the rest ones (no double count); "two independent signals agree" is D58's LOCK rule already.
- **Measured (the record):** NBA 7 picks (−22.9%), NHL 1: under the 60 a reason needs; not proven, no tier moves. The travel facts reach only the seasons games.json keeps, and few of the Bot's bets sit at the sharp fair.
- **Gate (D22):** ties (a band on top of unchanged chances). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D62.

### D63. The betting windows: the replay places a game line at the intraday snapshots, and each segment's best hour is learned walk-forward (none earned yet)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the improvement round's first item; D61's curve: the NBA's and NHL's line gaps worth something 6 hours and more out and gone by 3, the props better late).
- **Rule (`windows.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs intradayLines`):**
  - **The intraday lines in the replay.** `gate.mjs` reads D61's intraday files (`intraday.mjs readIntraday`) as the archive's line rows of kind `'i'` (each book and market as it stood at the snapshot nearest each offset hour, within 45 minutes; `h` the offset), priced as of their own snapshot like the opens (the ratings after the finals over by then, the day-of terms as known then, the trust of the months before, D48), and hands them to the replay alone (`rows.intra`): never the bar, the confidence fit, the trust or the legs. `GATE_NO_INTRA=1` leaves them out.
  - **A key's candidates:** the archive's clean open, each intraday snapshot (clean, inside the sport's window, its facts known by then, its lean held to the lineup risk's surviving share), the close; each at its own price and hours, in time order.
  - **The policy, by segment** (`segOf`: the market and the side class, `ml:fav`, `spread:dog`, `total:over`): `legacy` (the open, else the close: the replay as it was) or "not before" an hour H of 24, 12, 6, 3 or 1 (`POLICY_HOURS`): held till H hours out, then the first snapshot whose edge clears the price.
  - **The walk (`walkWindows`):** each month's policy per segment from the games settled before it alone: every policy's flat result on every game of the segment with intraday snapshots, paired against `legacy` on the same games; a policy replaces `legacy` only where its paired gain is past `WIN_Z` 1.645 SEs over `WIN_MIN` 60 games (D58's proven bar), and its picks placed before the close beat the fair close `WIN_BEAT` 52.5% of the time on `WIN_BEAT_N` 30 checks or more (D60's bar) and at least as often as the old placement's (`GUARD_TOL` half a point) at no lower a mean return there (D60's guard, on the window). No evidence: as before.
  - **Live (`run.mjs betGames`, `heldNow`, `holdFor`):** the record writes each segment's verdict on the whole history to `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/windows.json`; a line pick whose segment's policy is an hour H is held ("waiting for its betting window") while the game is more than H hours away, then bet at the price then; the last chance always bets. `legacy`: as before. Props aren't windowed: the replay prices them at the close (their day-of facts, D48), so there's no earlier evidence for them.
- **Measured (the replay, every snapshot the history has: NBA 7,887 keys, NHL 8,796, MLB 14,820, NFL 1,989; the last two seasons):** no segment earns a window in any sport; every segment's verdict on the whole history is `legacy`, and the replay places every bet where it did (the records tie). On the way: at 1 SE over 40 the walk moved a few segments for a month or two and those bets lost (the NBA's 13 −3.1u; the NHL's 81 beat the fair close 6-9% of the time against 41-47% at the open); at 1.645 SEs over 60 the NFL's total overs earned "not before 12 hours" (+7.5 points a game paired, SE 3.9, on 169 games) and its 81 12-hour bets made units while beating the fair close 3 times in 38: more units at worse prices, which D60's bar on the window now refuses. So D61's "bet the lines when first seen" is what the desk already does (its 26-hour window), and the NFL's earlier gaps (D61: dogs "beating" a fair close that overrates them) don't win on the results. Timing is a segment key the bands could earn on (D58's `seg:<market>:<class>:<early|late>`), and stays one.
- **Gate (D22):** ties (no chance or projection moves). Not an epoch change (D4).
- **Code:** `windows.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs` (`intradayLines`, `SNAP_TOL`, `historyLineBets`' kind 'i', `finish`'s `intra`), `gate.mjs` (the intraday lines into `hist.lines` and the asOf asks, `rows.intra`, `windows.json` in `recordScore`), `replay-record.mjs` (`candsOf`, the windows' walk, `flags.windows`), `run.mjs` (`r.windows`, the hold). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D63.
- **Loops:** the record writes the file from the replay's walk; the desk reads it and never writes it; the window moves only when a pick is placed, never its chance, stake or band.
- **Never:** a window chosen on games settled after the bet's moment; a window that wins units at worse prices against the fair close; a pick lost to the wait (the last chance bets).

### D64. The props' stat bar: D51's bar on the props, by stat and side (on in the NBA and the NFL)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the round's second item, props first: the owner, "find which stats the model beats the Book on and let those earn stakes").
- **What the history says (the gate's walk-forward prop closes, the Bot's log loss / the Book's):** the Bot leads the Book on most stats by a hair: NBA pts .6922/.6922, reb .6872/.6875, ast .6856/.6857, fg3 .6784/.6795, pra .6921/.6926; NFL passAtt .6932/.6945, passCmp .6898/.6906, rushYds .6918/.6926, recYds .6920/.6927, rec .6826/.6831, and trails on passInt (.6892/.6884), passTd and rushRecYds; NHL sog tie, saves ahead, pts tie; MLB hits and total bases ahead, strikeouts and outs behind. A lead in log loss isn't a lead past the vig: the replayed prop bets by stat, flat, NBA pts −2.9% (663 unders), ast −5.4% (317 unders), reb +5.5% (299 unders), fg3 +1.7% (887 unders), pra +4.9%; NFL recYds unders +7.2% (530), rec unders +4.5% (426), passInt −16.3% (164 unders) and −18.4% (60 overs).
- **Rule (`desk.mjs` `PROP_BAR`, `PROP_BAR_SPORTS`, `propKey`, `propBar`, `propBarGate`, `readPropBar`; `replay-record.mjs` `propBarAt`; `gate.mjs propBarOf`):** a prop pick's real stake is earned by its key `prop:<stat>:<over|under>` exactly as a line's by its segment (D51): on at the smallest minimum edge of `EDGE_GRID` whose picks at that return or more are net positive past `BAR_Z` 1 SE over `BAR_MIN` 40, on the priced history's prop picks (every positive-edge close, flat, recency-weighted by season) pooled with the live prop bets since the epoch; else the practice stake, a LIKE at most (D1: never off). In the replay the walk is by month on the picks before it (`walkBar`), with the bar's own place by season (`barPlace`, the props apart from the lines: a season's props at full stakes where full stakes out-earned the bar on the seasons before). Live: `bothGates` with the stat's CLV gate (D60); the record writes `prop-bar.json` (the keys, `applies`, the place). **On where the gate shipped it (`PROP_BAR_SPORTS`, the replay's verdict by sport, as `anchor.mjs anchorOn`):** the NBA and the NFL; held in the NHL (−0.5u on 32 prop bets) and MLB (−0.04u on 80): too few props to judge, and no gain.
- **Measured (the replayed records on this round's rebuilt cache, the same bets either way):** NBA −21.03u → −10.05u (3,597 bets; on at the end: `prop:fg3:under`); NFL −38.94u → −27.60u (3,244; on at the end: `prop:recYds:under`, `prop:rec:under`; the bar applied in every props season, 2023-2026); NHL −29.91u → −30.41u and MLB −15.41u → −15.45u with it on (held). The bets beat the fair close the same (props are bet at the close: no check). The NFL's committed record before this round (−12.04u on 3,240) was made on a props fit (code 4a1aee79) this checkout's code doesn't produce; the like-for-like champion on the rebuilt cache is −38.94u.
- **The weakest stat, held for the next round:** the NFL's interceptions (the Bot's log loss behind the Book's, both sides losing 16-18% flat: its count shape at the 0.5 line); the bar holds them at the practice stake meanwhile.
- **Not an epoch change (D4):** stakes and bands on top of unchanged chances. `CAP_RULES` 4 (D53): an open bet placed under 3 is priced again at a fresh quote.
- **Tests:** `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D64.
- **Never:** a stat turned off by the bar; the desk writing the file; a key judged on a pick settled after the bet's moment.

### D65. MMA's Kelly bands earn their place (D58's place check on MMA)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the round's fourth item: the owner's "LOCK > LOVE > LIKE, each better than the one below on the replayed history"; MMA's record LOCK 7 +7.4u, LOVE 57 19-37-1 −17.2u, LIKE 972 −14.2u: its LOVE below its LIKE).
- **Rule (`replay-record.mjs`, `mma-desk.mjs`):** MMA keeps D37's Kelly bands (D58's reasons aren't MMA's), but each band now earns its place as D58's tiers do (`reasons.mjs placeTiers`, walk-forward by month): LOVE shows only where the would-be LOVEs and LOCKs before that month beat the would-be LIKEs by flat return and by beat-the-fair-close on `PLACE_MIN` 40, LOCK only where its own beat the LOVEs; a band not earned falls to the one below, its stake held to that band's cap (`TIER_UNITS`: a LIKE 1u, a LOVE 2u). The record writes the verdict on the whole history (`replay-record.json flags.mmaPlace`); the desk reads it and holds a new bet's band and stake to it (`conf.cap`, `placeHeld`). `MMA_PLACE=0` off.
- **Measured (the replay):** the would-be bands on the whole history LOCK 27 +27.5% flat (beat the fair close 20% of 10), LOVE 237 +1.5% (31.7% of 123), LIKE 772 −5.1% (39.1% of 376): LOVE out-returns LIKE but beats the fair close less often (D59: the fair close's no-vig overrates the dogs, so a favorite's beat is harder), so neither band earns its place and every MMA bet is a LIKE: 1,036 bets 655-369-12, −24.08u → −16.52u. Honest by D58's rule: a band is earned, never assumed.
- **The team sports' bands on this round's records (D58, unchanged):** one reason proven on the whole history, the NFL's `stat:recYds:under` (+7.2% flat on 530), but no replayed bet carried it proven at its own moment with a Kelly past the 2% LOVE floor (the props' Kelly is small), so no would-be LOVE exists yet and every team-sport bet is still a LIKE; live too, till the place check earns LOVE on the record. The windows (D63), the props' bar (D64) and D62's fatigue are keys the record judges, none proven yet.
- **Not an epoch change (D4).** `CAP_RULES` 4 (D53).
- **Tests:** `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D65.

### D66. The news lag: a star newly out while his teammates' lines haven't moved, a `newsLag` reason earned on its own live record
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the round's third item, built; the measurement below and in "Measured and held, 2026-10-20: the news lag on the props").
- **Rule (`news-lag.mjs`, `reasons.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `timing.mjs propAsk`, `oddsapi.mjs TIERS`, `picks.mjs`, `book-rows.ts starOutText`):**
  - **Who's a star:** by the Book's own lines on the game's board, kept run to run for the game (his prop is pulled once he's out): NBA the top two points lines on his team or any of 17.5 or more (the measurement's star); NHL the top two shots lines; NFL the top passing line (QB1), rushing (RB1) and the top two receiving (WR1, TE1). MLB isn't read. The NHL's goalies keep their own path.
  - **Detection:** a star Out or Doubtful on the run's report (ESPN's, or Sleeper's or a reporter's put in ahead of it, D49) for the first time, kept with the first run's time (`first`) and his teammates' lines and over prices from the run before (the price then), in `.cache/model/news-lag-<sport>.json` (pruned 6 hours after the start; a lost file starts over and tags nothing whose price then it doesn't have). A star already out the first run the desk sees the game is no news.
  - **The tag** (`lagTag`): a teammate's over on a stat the absence feeds (NBA points, rebounds, assists, threes, PRA; NFL rushing and receiving; NHL shots and points) whose projection reads the star out with its vacancy up (`vacF` > 1: the props model's redistribution), within `LAG_FRESH_H` (12) hours of the first sighting, while his line is no higher than then and the over's no-vig chance up less than `MOVE_TOL` (3 points); the NBA's only while the star's own prop is still posted (once the Book pulls it the history's gap is gone). On the bet as `newsLag`: { player, id, status, first (t_news), min (minutes from the news to the pricing), src, then: { line, odds } }; t_bet is the bet's `placedAt`.
  - **The re-ask:** a star newly out and not yet asked for since (`lagPending`, read before the game's ask) makes the game's props ask `newsLag` (`propAsk`, second only to a game's first ask; `TIERS.newsLag` 1, the top share of the paced allowance, above `news` 0.9 and `refresh` 0.6; `PROP_ASKS` still caps a game). Where the board has its own free prices (NBA, NHL) the ask is deferred as a first ask is (D42): the board prices the teammates at once, and the fresh quote is bought only where a row's lean clears its price. The watcher (D55) dispatches the desk on a new Out or Doubtful on ESPN's list or a reporter's post (polled every 5 minutes, a 20-minute cooldown between runs), so the props are priced again in the first run after the news: about 5 to 30 minutes, plus the run's own time to reach the props.
  - **Confidence (D58):** `newsLag` is a reason (family `news`, props, NBA NFL NHL), **live-only**: the replay can't time the news, so the replayed file never speaks for it (`liveTier` drops a file entry for it) and its verdict comes from the live ledger's graded, staked edge bets alone (`liveVerdicts`: flat 1u at the bet's price, beat-the-close from its CLV, every epoch's tagged bets, since the tag is the news's and starts now), judged at D58's bar unchanged: `R_MIN` 60 tagged picks and 60 others in the market, `Z_LOVE` 1.645 SEs over the rest, leading in 60% of the seasons with 10 of its picks (two seasons at least), beating the close as often. **No prior:** D58 has no prior term, so the historical measurement seeds nothing; it starts at zero evidence. A LOVE also needs the 2% Kelly floor and LOVE's own place (`place.love`, D58); never relabeled.
  - **Stakes:** the standard rules: the tier's cap (a LIKE 1u till proven), the props' CLV gate and stat bar (D60, D64), the early fast-news practice stake where the news came from Sleeper or a reporter (D49); gates shrink, never stop.
