  - **Shown:** picks.json carries `lag` { player, first, min } on a tagged pick; the popover's "Why the Bot is confident" lists "Star out: <name> (news <n> min ago)" first (`starOutText`, minutes from the first sighting to now), then the proven reasons. The Algorithm tab lists no reasons (nothing to add there).
  - **The review (Gemini's, `node news-lag.mjs <sport>`, `lagReview`):** each tagged bet's t_news, t_bet, the latency between, the first price-log row after the bet where the Book's line rose or the over's no-vig rose 3 points (when DraftKings moved, its line and price then) and the execution delta (the line or no-vig points the bet beat the move by). Pinnacle's props aren't logged (the desk asks the Book alone for props, D55), so its move is null. A review reads the price log; a bet never does (D54).
- **The sanity check (Gemini's, on the history, before trusting the number):** were the 6-hour teammates' lines already moving (the market knew: "smash spot" games)? Each teammate's 6-hour line against his line at the event's first snapshot (about 24 hours out), the NBA's last two seasons (2,620 box scores read free from ESPN; more games than the first measurement's 894 cases, whose box cache covered fewer), the star's own prop still posted:

| Teammates' points overs at 6 hours | n | Over hit | Book's fair | Flat ROI at the Book's price |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Line unchanged since the first snapshot | 1,700 | 58.1% | 50.0% | +9.4% (the two seasons +13.5% on 858 and +5.2% on 842) |
| Line already up (the market moving) | 40 | 75.0% | 49.1% | +43.7% |
| Line down | 26 | 50.0% | 51.5% | −8.6% |

  96% of the cases had not moved by 6 hours: the edge sits in lines the Book hadn't touched, not in games the market already knew; those lines then moved about 10 no-vig points by the close. Rebounds (unchanged: 50.5%, −4.4% on 1,591), assists (52.3%, −1.4% on 1,115) and threes (52.2%, +1.0% on 1,325) show little: they ride the one tag to be judged with it (a split by stat is the next round's if points carry it).
- **Gate (D22):** live-only (a tie by construction, as D42 and D49): no chance, projection or stake moves by the tag, and the replay never sets it. Not an epoch change (D4).
- **Paid calls:** none in this build (the measurement read the committed intraday history and ESPN's free box scores).
- **Code:** `news-lag.mjs` (`starsOf`, `lagUpdate`, `lagTag`, `lagPending`, `lagPrune`, `readLag`, `starOutText`, `lagReview`, `LAG_STATS`, `STAR_BY`, `LAG_FRESH_H`, `MOVE_TOL`), `reasons.mjs` (`newsLag`, `LIVE_REASONS`, `liveVerdicts`, `seasonOfStart`, `liveTier`'s `live`), `run.mjs` (the ask's `lagNew`, the tag after pricing, `propBet`'s `newsLag`, `withTier`), `timing.mjs propAsk` (`starOut`), `oddsapi.mjs TIERS`, `picks.mjs` (`lag`), `book-rows.ts` (`NewsLag`, `starOutText`), `book-page.component.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D66.
- **Never:** the tag moving a chance, a projection or a stake; a tag on a line that already moved; the replay's file speaking for a live reason; a prior on it; the price log read on a live bet.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-20: the news lag on the props (the round's third item)
- **The question:** when a star is ruled out, do his teammates' props lag? (D61's intraday props, the NBA's 2025-26 season; a star a points line of 17.5 or more at the event's first snapshot; "out" from the box score.)
- **Found (scratchpad newslag.mjs):** 216 such stars sat out (4,426 played). While the Book still posted the star's own prop, his teammates' points overs hit 63.1% at 24 hours (130, the Book's fair 49.6%), 56.4% at 6 hours (894, fair 50.0%: +6.5% flat at the Book's price), 55.1% at 3 hours (695), 57.6% at 1 hour (172); their lines then moved about 10 points toward the over by the close. Once the star's prop was pulled the gap was gone (51-52% at 3 and 1 hours). Rebounds and assists show no lag. At the close the Bot's projection reads the out teammate fine (the teammates' points overs 47.7% against the Bot's 49.4% and the Book's 50.0%: no under-adjustment).
- **Held:** no new chance term (the projection's vacancy is calibrated at the close) and no replayed rule (the replay can't know when the news broke: the box score is hindsight, D48). The trigger already exists: the watcher kicks the desk on Out and Doubtful news (D55), the props are asked again on an out (`propAsk` 'news'), and the projection reads the out teammate, so a teammate's over against the stale line is priced as an edge. Built as D66: a `newsLag` reason on those live bets, so the record can earn it a band on live results.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-20: D59's favorite-longshot calibration for the team sports' lines
- Walk-forward by season on the moneyline closes (logit(result) = b0 logit(Pinnacle's no-vig) + b1 logit(DraftKings'), D59's ridge): the slopes sit at 1 (NHL 1.04/0.04, MLB 0.97/0.01, NBA 0.98/0.01, NFL 1.02/0.03), so there's no bias to take out, and the calibrated fair's positive-return sides do no better than the raw ones (NBA closes +7.3% flat raw on 566 dogs, +1.7% calibrated on 1,617; MLB −8.2% raw, −9.7% calibrated). Not built.

### D67. The deep history: the 5M plan's last credits on more seasons, more hours, every book and the derivative markets (research only)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner: "get every bit of historical data possible as we have credits in odds api this month and won't in future, so save/store it just in case"; 551,390 left, 61,000 kept back for the live desk and the other agent's backfill).
- **The pricing (probed):** a historical call costs 10 x the markets it returns, one region whether it names 2 books or 10 (30 credits either way for the lines), so every call names the ten books one region covers (`oddsapi.mjs WIDE_BOOKS`: DraftKings, Pinnacle, FanDuel, BetMGM, Caesars, theScore/ESPN Bet, Fanatics, BetRivers, Hard Rock, Bally): the other US books' prices come free (a book the API didn't carry then is absent; in the NFL: DraftKings, Pinnacle, FanDuel, Caesars, BetRivers from 2020, BetMGM from October 2020, ESPN Bet and Hard Rock from November 2023, Fanatics and Bally later).
- **The pull (`deep-history.mjs`, local only, `JOBS` in the order bought):** D61's way (one call a sport at each whole hour an offset lands on; one call an event and hour for props and markets), skipping every hour D61 bought; each job's raw compact answers in `DEEP_WORK/<job>.jsonl` as they come (resumable: a key there is never bought twice).
- **Stored:** `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/odds-history/intraday/dense/`: `<season>.jsonl.gz` (D61's I and S rows, framed on the archive's home by the names, D56), `props-<season>.jsonl.gz` (D61's Q and T rows), `markets-<season>.jsonl.gz` (`["M", oid, snapshot, book, market, name, description, point, price]`, each price on its outcome's own name, D56), `unmatched-<year>.jsonl.gz` (the sightings no archived event matches, mostly MMA bouts called off or renamed, on the API's own names: `["U", id, snapshot, start, home, away, book, market, line, a, b]`). Book codes `BOOK_CODES` (dk, pin, fd, mgm, czr, espn, fan, br, hr, bly). `readDeep(sport)` reads them.
- **Gate (D22):** nothing priced reads them: `readIntraday` reads `intraday/` only, never the `dense/` folder, so D63's windows, the replay and the desk are untouched (a tie by construction). Tests: `tests/model-deep-history.test.mjs`.
- **Bought (2026-10-20; 490,680 credits, 60,702 left on the API; every call ten books):**

| Job | Sport, market | Hours before the start | Seasons | Calls | Credits | Committed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| nfl-lines-a | NFL h2h, spreads, totals | 48, 24, 12, 6, 3, 2, 1 | 2020-2023 | 2,847 | 85,410 | 0.7 MB |
| nfl-lines-b | NFL h2h, spreads, totals | 48, 2 (D61 has 24/12/6/3/1) | 2024-2026 | 418 | 12,540 | 0.3 MB |
| nba-lines-a | NBA h2h, spreads, totals | 6 | 2020-2024 | 2,762 | 82,860 | 1.2 MB |
| nhl-lines-a | NHL h2h, spreads, totals | 6 | 2020-2024 | 2,788 | 83,640 | 1.0 MB |
| nfl-props-a | NFL props (props.mjs API_MARKETS, 10) | 3 | 2023 (333 games) | 333 | 27,390 | 0.8 MB |
| nfl-markets-a | NFL team totals, alternate spreads and totals, first-half h2h/spreads/totals | 1 | 2023-2026 (1,019 games) | 1,019 | 56,880 | 7.0 MB |
| mma-lines-a | MMA h2h, totals | 336, 168, 72, 24, 12, 6, 3, 1 | 2020-2024 | 6,359 | 126,760 | 0.9 MB |
| nba-props-a | NBA props (5) | 3 | 2024 (313 of 1,351 games, stopped at the floor) | 313 | 15,200 | 1.1 MB |

  About 13 MB committed in all; the raw compact answers (155 MB) stay in `.cache/model/deep`. Left out for cost: an every-sport 12-hour grid on every season (about 2.6M credits), MLB's older seasons (D61: its gaps never had value), more props hours (about 50,000 credits a season and hour a sport).

### D68. The fast lane: the watcher polls every minute, and news on one game dispatches that game's props alone, minutes from the news to the bet
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the coordinator's "FAST LANE": the watcher every minute, free sources only; a fast desk run on GitHub for news on one game, props only, no refits, about 3 to 5 minutes from the news to the bet; inside D55's credit pacing).
- **Supersedes, in part:** D55's watcher every 5 minutes (now every minute) and its dispatch of the full desk on a new Out or Doubtful or a reporter's post (now the fast lane's when the news names one game); D66's "polled every 5 minutes, a 20-minute cooldown between runs" for its re-ask. The rest of D55 (the full lane's 20-minute cooldown, 40 a day, never while a run is queued or running) and D66 stand.
- **Rule:**
  - **The poll** (`scripts/desk-watch.mjs`, the task "SeasonRanker desk watch" every minute, `scripts/desk-watch.ps1 -Register`, `EveryMinutes` 1, 3 minutes at most a poll, a poll still going when the next is due skips that one): ESPN's scoreboards, injury lists and the D49 reporters' Bluesky posts, as before; Sleeper stays once a UTC day (D49), never polled here. Kept cheap: the scoreboard days inside the lines' window every poll, the days past it (the props' windows, read only for a window opening) every `FAR_EVERY_MIN` (5), their games kept in the watcher's state between; a reporter's posts since his cursor a page of `POSTS_PAGE` (10). About 25 free requests a minute in season (ESPN 8 to 10 scoreboards and up to 4 injury lists, Bluesky 15 accounts, gh 2). The log trimmed to its newest half past 2 MB.
  - **The news on one game** (`advance`, `postGame`): a new Out or Doubtful on ESPN's list carries the player's team's next game inside its window; a reporter's high-impact post (`HIGH`) carries the one game inside its window whose team it names (the nickname or full name, as written) or whose listed player (ESPN's list) it names; naming none or two, it stays the full lane's. Such an item is the fast lane's (`fast`, `game`), pending till the game starts. The line moves, the windows opening and the NFL's inactives stay the full lane's (a line moves on the news too, and the full lane's line move catches that).
  - **The fast run** (`.github/workflows/model-desk-fast.yml`, `run.mjs <sport> --fast=<game id>`): the sport's run for that game's props alone. The props' fit rebuilt from the saved one, never refit (`fitPlan` as if past its budget: a due refit waits for the full lane; one that can't be rebuilt, no props this run, fitcache.mjs); no game line priced or bought (`betGames` over no game; `liveLines` the free event list alone, for the props' ask's event id); only that game's open props taken back on news (`repriceOpen`), the other games' inputs kept as the full run left them; not counted in the runs' cadence (`RUNS`, GAP: the last chance's timing reads the full lane's). Everything else as any run: the grading, the cheap refits (trust, calibration, correction: under a second each), the props' asks by tier under the paced allowance (D55 `budgetOf`, `spendable`, `TIERS`, D66's `newsLag` first), `PROP_ASKS`, the fresh quote only to bet (D30, D42, D53), the board (the other games kept from the last board) and the picks. No AI step (the research, the write-ups, the news signals, the miss review: the full lane's), no morning snapshot; the data's checks alone (`tests/data.test.mjs`, the live data's quality tests), not the full suite.
  - **The two lanes never race:** the fast workflow runs in the full desk's concurrency group (`model-desk`): one desk run at a time across both, so neither reads a ledger the other is writing; its commit rebases and retries as the full desk's; its cache is the full desk's (the same key's prefix), so the Odds API's tally (D55, mirrored in odds-spend.json) and the saved fits carry over both ways. GitHub keeps one waiting run a group, a newer replacing it: the watcher never dispatches the fast lane while a full run waits (that run reads the news), and a full run that replaces a waiting fast one reads the same news.
  - **The rates** (`dispatchDecision`, `fastDecision`): the full lane none while a run of either lane is queued or running, `COOLDOWN_MIN` (20) after the last full run's start (a fast run's start doesn't count), `MAX_PER_DAY` (40), unchanged. The fast lane none while a fast run is queued or running or a full one is queued (a full one running doesn't stop it: the fast run waits behind it), `FAST_COOLDOWN_MIN` (5) a game since its last fast dispatch or run, `FAST_MAX_PER_DAY` (30) a UTC day; past the cap its news goes to the full lane. The soonest game first. A fast run settles only its own game's news (its game in its run's title, `fastGameOf`); a full run settles everything.
- **Measured (2026-10-20):** a local `--dry` fast run of the NHL's TOR @ CBJ: 18 seconds (the context 7s, the props rebuilt from the saved fit 7s, the one game priced, 33 props, in 0.5s), against 15s for the NHL's whole warm dry run: the bettor's work is seconds either way; the time is the workflow's. The full desk's run of 16:20 UTC on GitHub: the job's setup, checkout, Node and cache 32 seconds; the bettor 2.5 minutes for every sport (the NFL's props rebuilt from its saved fit 43s, the NBA's and NHL's 3 to 4s, MLB's context 63s); the full test suite 110 seconds; the commit 33; the deploy 6 minutes (its build 5). The fast run, estimated from those: the setup 32s, the bettor 20s (NBA, NHL) to 60s (NFL), the cache, install and the data's checks (25s locally) about 40s, the commit about 30s: about 2 to 2.5 minutes a job.
- **The latency, news to bet:** the watcher sees the news within a minute of ESPN's list or the post (30 seconds on average); the dispatch starts a job in seconds (6s on the 16:20 run); the bet is priced and placed (its `placedAt`) about 1 to 1.5 minutes into the job, committed at about 2 to 2.5: about 2 to 3 minutes from the news to the bet, 3 to 3.5 to master, 9 to 10 to the site (the deploy). Behind a full run already going, plus what's left of it (a typical one about 6 minutes, one refitting the NFL up to 35). The Book's move it races: D66's teammates' lines unmoved at 6 hours in 96% of the stars' outs, D49's Bluesky 0.8 hours ahead of ESPN.
- **Credits:** none new. The fast run's asks are the props' asks any run would make on that news (D55's `news` tier, D66's `newsLag`), from the same paced allowance; NBA, NHL and MLB price off their free boards first (D42's deferred ask). 30 fast runs a day at the NFL's 10 credits an ask is 300 at most, inside the props' share; `PROP_ASKS` caps a game.
- **Gate (D22):** live-only timing (a tie by construction, as D42 and D55): no chance, projection or stake moves. Not an epoch change (D4): the bets are the same rules' at an earlier run.
- **Code:** `run.mjs` (`FAST`, `fitPlan`'s spent, `liveLines`, `betGames`, `repriceOpen`, `betProps`), `.github/workflows/model-desk-fast.yml`, `scripts/desk-watch.mjs` (`POLL_MIN`, `FAR_EVERY_MIN`, `POSTS_PAGE`, `FAST_WORKFLOW`, `FAST_COOLDOWN_MIN`, `FAST_MAX_PER_DAY`, `fastDecision`, `fastGameOf`, `postGame`, `gamesOf`'s boards, `advance`'s fast items, `settle`'s fast runs), `scripts/desk-watch.ps1`. Tests: `tests/model-desk-watch.test.mjs` D68, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D68.
- **Loops:** none fits on it.
- **Never:** a refit, a game line or a lines call in the fast lane; the two lanes side by side or in different concurrency groups; a fast dispatch while a fast run is queued or running or a full one is queued; a fast run settling another game's news; the poll on Sleeper; a fast run counted in the cadence; the watcher running the bettor or spending a credit.

### D69. Every season with prop odds is priced: the replay's players' games reach back as far as the archive's props
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner's coverage gap: the replay priced the NBA's props in 2026 alone though the archive's props start 2023-05; MLB's in 2025-26 alone; the NHL's in 2026-27).
- **Why:** the props' replay reads the players' games of `history` (games.json), which keeps the last two seasons; an archived prop whose game has no box score has no projection and is never priced. The NFL's players' games (nflverse, 2016 on) already reached every props season, so its seasons were all priced (closes in the history / priced: 2023 11,443 / 10,915, 2024 13,089 / 12,745, 2025 12,039 / 11,560, 2026 4,339 / 4,162): its few 2024 bets (71) are the model's own walk-forward (the anchor and the trust, fit on 2023 alone, hold 2024's chances near the Book: a mean gap to the Book of 0.7 points against 1.4 in 2025, 592 rows past 2 points against 3,487), not missing data.
- **Rule (`gate.mjs propsHistory`, `PROPS_EXTEND`, `rebuildCache`; `context.mjs mlbOlder`):** the props' replay (the gate's layers a and b and the replayed record) reads the players' games of games.json's seasons and of every older final in the history's events from the first season the archive has props for: the NBA's and the NHL's box scores from ESPN (`playerlogs.mjs boxRows`, the same cache), MLB's starters and lineups matched to StatsAPI's schedule with their game logs (`mlbOlder`; the teams' codes from games.json's) and the batters' logs by season. The older games' expected points are the ratings' replay over the extended games (`expExt`); their day-of context is none (the ratings alone, as the lines' older seasons). The props' fit stays the desk's (games.json's rows): the gate prices the older seasons under the saved fit. `gate.mjs --rebuild-cache` fetches them once (free: the NBA's 3,942 finals in 96 s, the NHL's 4,196 in 118 s, MLB's 4,909 matched in 26 s). `GATE_PROPS_EXTEND=0` leaves them out (the "before" below). The gate's log prints each season's closes in the history against the closes priced.
- **Measured (the replay on this round's rebuilt cache, before → after):**

| Sport | Season | Prop closes priced | Prop bets (units) | Whole record |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | 2023 | 0 → 2,025 | 0 → 168 (+2.9u) | |
| NBA | 2024 | 0 → 77,682 | 0 → 1,689 (+24.7u) | |
| NBA | 2025 | 0 → 70,409 | 0 → 743 (+5.9u) | |
| NBA | 2026 | 73,367 → 75,322 | 2,431 (−9.4u) → 655 (−3.6u) | 3,597 bets −10.1u → 4,421 +29.3u |
| NHL | 2023 / 2024 / 2025 | 0 → 1,245 / 41,584 / 39,130 | 0 → 14 / 262 / 157 (−1.3u / −6.5u / +11.7u) | |
| NHL | 2026 / 2027 | 44,199 / 5,291 → 45,290 / 5,291 | 32 (+5.3u) → 132 (+7.6u) / 0 | 1,809 −26.8u → 2,342 −20.6u |
| MLB | 2023 / 2024 | 0 → 69,573 / 87,052 | 0 → 91 (−2.7u) / 0 | |
| MLB | 2025 / 2026 | 76,723 / 65,109 → 77,345 / 65,109 | 64 / 16 → 0 / 16 | 1,277 −8.5u → 1,304 −11.9u |
| NFL | 2023-2026 | unchanged (already full) | 352 / 71 / 1,827 / 249 | 3,024 −34.3u |

  The NBA's 2026 props bet fewer (655 against 2,431): the anchor and the trust are fit walk-forward on the seasons before, and with 2024-25 before it 2026's chances sit nearer the Book. MLB's props rarely clear the vig in any season (the anchor's pull: strikeouts 0.93, outs 0.98).
- **Gate (D22):** the extension is the gate's own loader (this checkout's, for both versions): both replays read the same games, a tie by construction; the record moves because the replay sees more seasons. Not an epoch change (D4): the desk's own projections are untouched (it reads games.json's two seasons); what it reads from the record moves as at every record, now fit on every props season: the anchor's pull (the NBA's points 0.885 to 0.895, PRA 0.880 to 0.943, rebounds 0.884 to 0.795; on 225,438 closes, was 73,367), the lineup risk, the props' stat bar (the NBA's prop:fg3:under no longer on, the stat bar stakes the rest), the confidence layer.
- **Code:** `gate.mjs` (`PROPS_EXTEND`, `propsHistory`, the props layer's `propGames` and `expExt`, the by-season log, `rebuildCache`), `context.mjs` (`mlbOlder`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D69.
- **Never:** a props season left unpriced while the archive has its props and a free source has its box scores; the props' fit refit on the older seasons by the gate (the desk's fit stands).

### D70. The closing-line bands (Gemini's design): a reason earns LOVE or LOCK by its shrunk return at the fair close, its beat share and results that don't lose; the band lab stages every rule before one ships
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner: confidence bands honest and earnable, LOCK > LOVE > LIKE over history; and a staging sandbox so band designs can be tried on the whole history before one ships).
- **Rule (`clv-bands.mjs`: `SHIPPED`, `walkClv`, `judge`, `tierClv`, `clvFileOf`, `liveMerged`; `replay-record.mjs` the tiers; `run.mjs livePicksOf`; `reasons.mjs liveTier`):**
  - **Evidence:** a reason (D58's: the segment, the stat and side, the timing, the rest, the Kelly size; D66's news lag) in a sport's market is judged on the bets that carry it, walk-forward: a bet reads only the bets settled before its own placement. Each bet with a closing check (`clv`: its price against the fair close's no-vig chance at its line, D56) adds its expected return at the fair close and whether that was positive. The mean is shrunk by empirical Bayes toward 0, w = n / (n + k), k 100 (`clv-eb`, k fit by the method of moments month by month toward the market's own mean: no different).
  - **The bars:** LOVE where n (bets with a check) ≥ 50, the shrunk mean ≥ +2% and the beat share ≥ 51.5%; LOCK where n ≥ 150, ≥ +4%, ≥ 53.5%, or two reasons of different families past LOVE's (D58's). **And the results guard:** the reason's graded bets (50 or more) aren't losing flat (ROI ≥ 0): the fair close's no-vig overrates some sides (D59, D61: the NFL's and MMA's dogs "beat" a close that overrates them and lose), so a closing-line lead alone doesn't earn. The owner's 2% Kelly floor under both (D21, D58). The rules' caps stay (a suspect price, a barred line or stat: a LIKE at most); the stakes are `TIER_UNITS` (1u, 2u, 3u), with D60's beat-the-close bar on top, as before.
  - **No closing check, no earning:** a market bet at the close (the props; the NFL's lines: the archive's NFL opens are 48 hours out, outside the lines' 26-hour window) has no closing check in the replay, so its reasons can't earn there; they earn live as the desk bets them earlier (below). The `clv-hybrid` variant (such a reason earning on D58's results bar instead) was tried and held: its NBA LOVEs (75) made less flat than its LIKEs (+0.8% against +1.0%).
  - **Live, between records:** `reasons.json` keeps each reason's closing-line tallies (`stats`) and the rule; each desk run adds the live ledger's graded, staked edge bets since `MODEL_EPOCH` (each one's reasons read again from the bet as the desk placed it, its `clv.ev`, its flat result: `run.mjs livePicksOf`) and judges every reason on the sum (`liveMerged`), so a reason earns on live closing lines in weeks, not at the next record. MMA keeps D65 (its Kelly bands' place), untouched.
  - **The news lag (D66) by stat:** a tagged over carries `newsLag` and `newsLag:<stat>` (the news family: one reason toward a LOCK, never two), both live-only, so the points overs, which carried the history's measurement, earn on their own. **No historical prior:** the measurement (the NBA's teammates' points overs at 6 hours with the line unchanged, 1,700 bets, 58.1% against a 50% fair, +9.4%) is real money at a real 6-hour price, but its selection is the box score's "didn't play", which a bettor at 6 hours couldn't know: 96% of those lines were unmoved at 6 hours, which says most of the outs weren't public yet (late scratches), so the +9.4% is partly hindsight and can't stand in for a bettor's record. Live, at the measured line move (about 10 no-vig points by the close), a tagged over's closing-line return is of the order of +15%: at k 100 a reason at that rate clears LOVE on its 50th bet (shrunk about +5%) and LOCK near its 150th; the NBA's stars sit out most nights, so weeks.
- **The band lab (`band-lab.mjs`, `band-variants.json`):** `REPLAY_DUMP=<dir> gate.mjs --score-only worktree <sport>` writes `<sport>-lab.json` (every replayed bet before the bands with its reasons, holds, price, fair, closing check and result; the passes in brief; the summary's arguments); `node band-lab.mjs <sport|all> [--variant <name|all>] [--reasons] [--write] [--dump <dir>]` (the dump `.cache/band-lab` by default) applies a rule in seconds (no refit) and prints the LOCK / LOVE / LIKE table (bets, record, units, ROI, flat ROI, beat the fair close, the Book's own expectation) and the monotonic check (each band's flat return and beat share against the band below); `--reasons` lists each reason's closing-line evidence; `--write` puts every variant's record into `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/replay-record.staged.json`, which the Algorithm tab (admins only) shows under its Live / Staged switch on By Sport and By Confidence, the rule picked by name.
- **Measured (every variant on this round's replays; every sport, every variant: all LIKE except as noted):** `d58` (the rule as it was), `clv` (Gemini's numbers), `clv-eb`, `clv-loose` (k 50; n 30, +1%, 51%; LOCK n 100, +3%, 52.5%), `clv-strict` (k 200 toward the market's mean; n 80, +2.5%, 52.5%; LOCK n 200, +5%, 55%; no two-reason LOCK), `clv-results` (shipped), `clv-hybrid` (NBA 75 LOVEs 39-36 +2.3u, flat +0.8% against LIKE's +1.0%: the monotonic check fails; NHL 1), `clv-place` (D58's place on top). The records (all LIKE): NFL 3,024 1470-1547-7 −34.3u (no closing check: all at the close); NBA 4,421 2076-2343-2 +29.3u, beat the fair close 49.6% of 345; NHL 2,342 1039-1277-26 −20.6u, 46.3% of 704; MLB 1,304 588-707-9 −11.9u, 34.8% of 431; MMA 1,038 656-370-12 −16.0u, 36.9% of 510. The closest reasons: the NBA's early moneyline dogs (314 checks, +2.0% raw, +1.6% shrunk, beat 51.0%, flat +7.1%) and `openGap` moneylines (339, +1.4%, 50.1%); the NHL's early spread dogs (262, +0.6%, beat 57.3%, flat +2.6%) and rested-against-a-back-to-back moneylines (31 checks, +4.4%, beat 84%, flat −4.1%); MMA's early favorites flat +6.7% on 426 at −1.1% at the fair close. The live ledger's bets since 10-08 lose the fair close by about the vig (props −6%, beat 2% to 8%). So the bands are honest and, today, earned nowhere: LIKE until a reason's closing line earns more, as D52 and D58 found by other roads; the monotonic check has no band above LIKE to read yet.
- **Gate (D22):** ties (no chance, projection or placement moves; the bands are the same on the history). Not an epoch change (D4). `CAP_RULES` unchanged (no open bet's band moves).
- **Code:** `clv-bands.mjs`, `band-lab.mjs`, `band-variants.json`, `replay-record.mjs` (the tiers, `REPLAY_BANDS=d58`, the lab's dump), `gate.mjs` (the module), `reasons.mjs` (`isLive`, `newsLag:<stat>`, `liveTier`), `run.mjs` (`livePicksOf`), `model-desk.component.*`, `replay-history.ts` (`StagedReplayFile`). Tests: `tests/model-clv-bands.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D70.
- **Never:** a reason judged on a bet settled after the bet's moment; a band from a closing-line lead whose results lose; a historical prior for a live-only reason whose selection is hindsight; the staged file read by the desk or the record.

### Measured and held, 2026-10-20: blowout props, MLB pitch caps and props lagging the game lines (the round's items 2-4)
- **How:** scratchpad `r4/research` (q2-nba.mjs, q2-nfl.mjs, q3-mlb.mjs, q4-lag.mjs): flat 1u at DraftKings' price, the selection from what was known at the snapshot alone, the box score only to grade; each season apart; the fair close Pinnacle's no-vig where it had the prop, else DraftKings'.
- **Blowout props (a favorite by 11.5 or more in the NBA, 13.5 in the NFL):** the favorite's two top scorers' points unders hit about 53% against a 50% fair (the effect is real) and still make only the vig back: at 6 hours −3.3% (2025, 389) and +0.8% (2026, 475); at the close +5.2% / −1.4% / −2.4% (2024-26, 380-500 each, SE about 4.5%); their PRA unders +7.0% / −2.5% / +0.9%; the bench's overs −4% to −9% everywhere; the beat of the fair close 3% to 15% (EV about −5%, the baseline's): the price doesn't lag it. The NFL's: 63-95 bets an hour, noise (±13%).
- **MLB pitch caps:** the starters' strikeout unders in a season's first three starts −1.2% at the open (2,039), −3.9% at the close (2,613); September −5.6% at the close; the first start back from the IL (StatsAPI's activations) +8.0% ± 6.8% at the open, its seasons flipping sign (−9.2%, +17.8%, +16.8%, +3.4%), −1.3% at the close; the baseline −4.4% at the close, by month March-April −1.4% to June −6.2%; the outs unders worse everywhere (the first start's −11.9%: the Book already shades the early cap). The third start alone at the open (+4.6% ± 3.4%, 3 of 4 seasons) is one cell of many looked at.
- **Props lagging a game line's move (an NBA total or spread moving 1.5 or more between snapshots):** the props don't follow (a total's move: 27% moved with it by the next snapshot, 25% against, 48% flat; a spread's move runs against more often: the injury news D66 reads); taking the lagging side at the next snapshot −8.2% (2025, 2,710) and −8.0% (2026, 3,605), beating the fair close 5-6% of the time; the NFL's moves of 3 or more: 4 pairs in the history.
- **Held:** no rule for any of the three (no edge past the vig that holds by season and at the fair close).

### D71. The NFL's lines bet earlier: D63's windows read the deep history's dense NFL lines (2020 on), and the walk keeps the close (no hour earned)
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner's approval to test: every NFL line in the replay was bet at the close (the archive's opens are 48 hours out, outside the 26-hour lines window), so none had a fair-close check and no NFL reason could earn a D70 band).
- **Rule (`windows.mjs` `DENSE_SPORTS`, `DENSE_OFFSETS`, `DENSE_BOOKS`; `gate.mjs`):** for a version whose `windows.mjs` exports `DENSE_SPORTS` (the NFL), the gate reads D67's `intraday/dense/` lines (`deep-history.mjs readDeep`) beside D61's files as more kind-'i' candidates (`intradayLines`, the same nearest-snapshot reading, within 45 minutes) at 24, 12, 6, 3, 2 and 1 hours (inside `TIMING.nfl.lines` 26; the 48-hour snapshots stay research), DraftKings and Pinnacle only; an event's hour D61's files already have is D61's (`seenIntra`). Everything else is D63's: the same policies (the 2-hour snapshot a candidate under "not before 3h", no policy of its own), the same walk by market and side class (1.645 SEs over 60 games, D60's 52.5% on 30 checks and the guard), the same live hold (`windows.json`, `run.mjs heldNow`). `GATE_NO_DENSE=1` leaves them out. A version without the export (the champion before D71) reads D61's files alone, so the gate compares like with like.
- **Research hooks (env only, never a record):** `REPLAY_DUMP` also writes `<sport>-windows.json` (the walk's outcomes: each game's flat result and fair-close check under every policy); `REPLAY_WINDOW_FORCE=<policy>` places every line segment by one policy so the band lab can read the reasons' closing checks had the Bot bet that early.
- **Measured (the NFL, 5,286 line keys with snapshots, 2020-2026, was 1,989; every policy's pick against the close's on the same games):**

| Segment | Bets at the close | Best hour, paired | Beat the fair close there |
|---|---|---|---|
| ml:fav | 42, −8.0u | 6h +0.2% (z 0.04) | 45% of 100 |
| ml:dog | 316, −49.4u | 6h +0.4% (z 0.13) | 65% of 353 (EV +1.9%) |
| spread:fav | 84, −4.7u | 12h +6.9% (z 1.47) | 36% of 106 |
| spread:dog | 3, +1.0u | 24h (7 games) | 3 checks |
| total:over | 231, +8.1u | 24h +1.6% (z 0.53) | 7% of 127 |
| total:under | 54, −1.9u | 3h +1.4% (z 0.29) | 6% of 36 |

  No segment clears the walk's bar in any month: every verdict is `legacy` (the close), the replay places every NFL line where it did, and the record ties (3,244 bets 1592-1642-10, −27.6u, the Book's own expectation −73.3u). Forced: every line at 24 hours −43.7u (3,904 bets, beat the fair close 44.3% of 837), at 6 hours −28.6u (3,752, 44.4% of 732): neither beats the close. The early moneyline dogs are D61's and D70's story again: they beat a fair close that overrates them 60-65% of the time at +1.6% to +2.0% and lose 13-17% flat.
- **The bands (the band lab on the forced dumps, the shipped `clv-results`):** no NFL reason earns LOVE or LOCK at either hour. The closest: `seg:ml:dog:early` (433 checks at 24h, +1.6% raw, +1.3% shrunk, beat 60.5%, flat −16.9%; 350 at 6h, +2.0%, +1.5% shrunk, beat 65.1%, flat −12.8%), `openGap` moneylines (523, +1.2% shrunk, beat 60.4%, flat −14.5%) and `fatigue` moneylines (54, +0.8% shrunk, beat 68.5%, flat −30.8%): short of +2% shrunk, and the results guard (flat ROI ≥ 0) refuses them anyway. `clv-loose` would make 8 LOCKs at 6 hours, 1-7 −6.3u. So the NFL earns no band on the history, and earns live only as the desk's own earlier bets add closing checks (D70's live merge).
- **Gate (D22):** SHIP, ties everywhere (no chance, projection, placement or stake moves). Not an epoch change (D4: nothing priced or placed moved). The NFL re-recorded (its `windows.json` now on the 2020-2026 evidence).
- **Code:** `windows.mjs`, `gate.mjs` (the dense read, `windows` among the version's modules), `replay-record.mjs` (the windows' dump, `REPLAY_WINDOW_FORCE`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D71.
- **Never:** a dense hour outside the lines window; the forced window in a record; the dense lines in the bar, the fits or the legs (D63's: the replay's placement alone).

### D72. The trusted-edge bands (the owner's idea): a bet's edge split into the model's pieces, each piece trusted only on its walk-forward record; measured, staged, none shipped
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner: "instead of finding a second 'algo' for confidence, maybe we should take the pieces of the formula that are most weighted (most accurate) and if the bet hits those to a certain degree AND is a positive enough kelly score it gets a confidence"; and the bands a pyramid, most LIKE, fewer LOVE, fewest LOCK, accuracy rising toward the top).
- **Attribution (research only, `REPLAY_ATTR=1` on the replay; off, nothing changes: the replayed bets are identical, checked bet by bet on all four sports):** `ratings.mjs` puts each prediction's context terms on it by group (`am` the margin's, `at` the total's: rest, travel, starters, weather, matchups, officials, park, from `context.mjs` TERMS' groups at the fit's weights the prediction read); `backtest.mjs repriced` turns each group into its part of the chance (the chance less the one with that group out of the margin or total); `gate.mjs propParts` splits a prop projection into log factors (form: his recent games' weight against his season rate; role: trend, ramp and share; vacated; opponent; funnel; targets; pace; script; context; fit: the scale and the middle pull, as `writeup.mjs driversOf` names them) and `gate-history.mjs propAttr` each into its part of the over's chance; `finish` scales the parts by what the anchor and the trust kept (pCur - f over raw - f), the rest of the projection's gap is `base` (the team ratings, or his own rate, against the Book) and the residual blend's `cal` (p - pCur; on a total the named terms' shift too), so the parts add to the bet's edge exactly. `replay-record.mjs` writes `<sport>-attr.json` (every close row's parts, fair, result, prices) beside the lab's dump. Not covered: the history's extended games carry no context (their lines' edge is all `base`), so only games.json's seasons split by term; MMA has no pieces.
- **Reliability (`trusted.mjs walkTrust`):** per sport and class (lines, props), month by month from the close rows settled before the month only: the result's excess over the fair close (y - f) regressed on every piece at once (ridge toward 0), each piece's slope and SE; held out, each piece's log-loss gain where it moves the chance (the joint chance at the weights fit before the row's month, with the piece against without it); and its share of wins as the main driver. **Proven** when 500 rows carry it, its slope's lower bound (1.645 SE) clears 0 and its held-out gain is over 0; its weight its slope held to [0, 1], unproven 0. On the whole history (the final month): props proven in every sport (NFL base, cal, fit, opponent, form, vacated, context, script; NBA fit, base, vacated, form, opponent, pace, cal; NHL base, fit, script, context, opponent, form; MLB base, fit, opponent, context); lines almost nothing (NFL weather, NBA rest, MLB officials, each with wide SEs; every other line term's slope inside noise): the lines' context parts are small after the trust's shrink.
- **Trusted edge and bands (`trustedEdge`, `trustBands`; band lab mode `trusted`):** the sum of a bet's parts times each one's weight. Bars `abs` (LOVE and LOCK by the trusted edge), `pct` (the top shares of every bet settled before the bet's month: the pyramid set walk-forward), score `te`, `teKelly` or `share` (the trusted share of the whole edge, "hits those pieces to a certain degree"), a Kelly floor under LOVE and LOCK, the caps as every rule's (a suspect price, a barred line or stat: a LIKE at most; `caps: false` a test that lets the band rise, its stake unchanged).
- **Measured (`band-lab.mjs all --seasons`, flat ROI, LOCK / LOVE / LIKE):** nothing is both a pyramid and monotonic beyond noise in any sport. NFL `trust-pct` 10 -24.2% / 23 +14.1% / 3,211 -1.7%; NBA `trust-share` 256 -4.4% / 308 +6.2% / 3,857 +1.0%; NHL `trust-share` 6 +46% / 47 +16.3% / 2,289 -2.8% (monotonic, but 53 bets in the top two, 2026's LOVE under its LIKE, z 1.2 for LOVE over LIKE); NHL `trust-love-only` 159 LOVEs +5.0% against LIKE's -2.8% (z 0.9, 2024's LOVE -10.7% under LIKE's -0.9%); MLB 13 or fewer above LIKE. With the stat bar's cap lifted (`trust-pct-nocap`) the pyramid appears (NFL 4.5% / 17.4% / 78.1%, NBA 10.5% / 20.4% / 69.1%) and LOVE beats LIKE (NFL +1.8% vs -2.2%, NBA +3.4% vs +1.3%, z under 0.9), but LOCK, the largest trusted edges, is the worst band in both (-5.6%, z -0.8 and -1.65 against LOVE): D52's finding again, the biggest edges are the tails of the noise. No fair-close check reads the top bands (their bets are props at the close). **More picks:** none to add; every close row with a positive Kelly score is already a replayed bet (9 of 244,133 NBA rows, 2 of 41,297 NFL), so a looser LIKE entry needs earlier placements, not a band rule.
- **Held:** no sport ships; the live rule stays D70's `clv-results`. Every trusted variant is staged (`band-lab.mjs --write`) beside the earlier ones, each named on the Algorithm tab's Live / Staged switch by its `label`; By Confidence shows each band's share of the bets beside its record.
- **Gate (D22):** ties (no chance, projection, placement or stake moves; `REPLAY_ATTR` off by default). Not an epoch change (D4).
- **Code:** `trusted.mjs`, `band-lab.mjs` (mode `trusted`, `--seasons`, the share column, the labels), `band-variants.json` (labels), `ratings.mjs`, `backtest.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs`, `gate.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs` (the attribution, env-only), `model-desk.component.*`, `replay-history.ts` (`bandSharesOf`, `labels`), `model-desk.scss`. Tests: `tests/model-trusted.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D72.
- **Never:** a piece trusted on rows settled after the bet's month; a percentile read off later bets; `REPLAY_ATTR` in a record or on the desk; a trusted band shipped without beating the band below by season.

### D73. The two-band trial (the owner's): LIKE and LOVE, LOCK off; a prop's LOVE from its trusted share, the thresholds picked walk-forward by month; LOVE on in the NHL, the trusted-share LIKE stakes in the NBA
- **Date:** 2026-10-20. **A trial**, labelled so on the Book ("LOVE (trial): driven by proven pieces: ...") and here, until a more proven band model replaces it.
- **Owner:** "I'm fine going two-band until we find a more proven model, or just lowering thresholds, whatever makes sense", and "is there any tweaking we could do to get a small winner in the sports?"
- **Rule (`trusted.mjs` `TRIAL`, `TRIAL_OPTS`, `chooseLove`, `chooseMid`, `trialWalk`, `trialBand`, `liveParts`, `liveTrial`, `trialFileOf`, `readTrusted`):**
  - **Two bands.** In a trial sport every bet is a LIKE or a LOVE; LOCK is off (the biggest edges were the worst band in every sport, D52, D72). Lines stay LIKE (their pieces are almost never proven, D72, and the live desk can't split them).
  - **LOVE:** a prop whose proven pieces (D72's walk on the props' close rows settled before the month) carry at least `share` of its edge, at a Kelly score in `[0.01, kellyMax)`. Each month `share` (0.3 to 0.8) and `kellyMax` (2% or none) are picked from the bets settled before the month alone: the pair whose would-be LOVEs led the rest by the most standard errors in flat return, with 30 LOVEs or more, their own return positive and the lead not negative; none such, no LOVE that month. A LOVE stakes 2u (`loveUnits`).
  - **The LIKE props' stake (the NBA only, `mid`):** a plain prop LIKE stakes 1u where its trusted share is past the month's step, else the practice stake 0.5u; the step (0 the stake as it was, 0.2/0.4/0.6, or every one at 0.5u) picked each month by the prior props' units.
  - **The 2% LOVE floor (D21, D58) lowered for the trial's LOVEs alone,** to its Kelly range (`picks.mjs tierKellyOf`, the UI's `tierClears`): at the 2% floor the walk's LOVEs lose out of sample (NBA 67 LOVEs −14.1% flat against LIKE +1.3%; NHL none), so the only LOVE the evidence supports sits at 1-2% Kelly. This is the owner's "just lowering thresholds" of the same day; the owner can put the floor back (the trial then has no LOVE).
  - **Caps stay on top:** a suspect price, a barred stat (D64), a CLV gate (D60), a practice, role or research pick: a LIKE at the stake that rule set (D1: shrink, never stop).
  - **On (`TRIAL`) piece by piece, where it beat the rule as it was out of sample:** the NHL's LOVE (its LOVEs beat its LIKEs in ROI and the sport's units rose); the NBA's LIKE stakes (+7.1u). The NBA's LOVE is off: on the round's first replay its walk-forward LOVEs led (323, +1.6% against +1.0%), on the re-recorded replay (the cache's newer NBA closes: 4,533 bets) they lost (260, −0.4% flat against LIKE +2.2%). The NFL and MLB: the walk never earned a LOVE (no rule led on their prior bets), and the LIKE stake step gave the NFL +0.6u on 3,244 bets (noise) and MLB −0.2u: off. The NHL's LIKE stake step lost 6.4u: off.
  - **The live split** (`trusted.mjs projectionParts`, `betParts`; `run.mjs trialPartsOf`) reads the bet's own projection (the fields `writeup.mjs driversOf` reads) at its stat's fit, so `props.mjs`, a fit file (`fitcache.mjs FIT_FILES`), is untouched: an edit there forces a fresh props search, which this round showed moves a record far (the NHL's 2,342 bets to 6,252 on a fresh search) and is a model change of its own. Live, his role's trend and share steps and the squad step fall in `vacated` (unproven `role` gets only his ramp back); neither is a proven piece in the NHL.
  - **The record and the desk:** `gate.mjs` computes the props' pieces in a trial sport (`propParts`, no longer env-only there; the replayed bets are the same with or without them, D72); `replay-record.mjs` bands and stakes the replay by the walk and refuses (the record guard) a trial sport's replay without the pieces; `--record` writes `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/trusted.json` (the prop pieces' weights at the end, the walk's next rule). The desk (`run.mjs withTrial` after `withTier`, the pieces below) reads the file, never fits it. D72's "never REPLAY_ATTR in a record or on the desk" stands for the research bands; this trial is its one exception, by the owner's decision.
  - **The Algorithm tab:** By Confidence shows LOVE and LIKE (a LOCK row only where the shown record has LOCK bets: MMA's D65 bands).
  - **Not an epoch change (D4):** bands and stakes on unchanged chances. `CAP_RULES` 5 (D53): an open bet placed under 4 is priced again under the two bands.
- **Measured (the replayed records, like for like: the same cache and fits, the trial off (`REPLAY_TRIAL=0`) against on; flat ROI ± its standard error; out of sample the walk's own monthly picks, in sample the walk's final rule applied to every bet, which flatters it):**

| Sport | Version | LOVE | LIKE | All |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NHL | Current (D70) | — | 2,342 1039-1277-26, −20.62u, ROI −1.7%, flat −2.2% ± 2.3 | −20.62u |
| NHL | Trial, out of sample (recorded) | 22 11-11, +1.38u, ROI +3.1%, flat +3.1% ± 22.2 | 2,320, −21.31u, −1.8% | **−19.93u** |
| NHL | Trial, in sample | 54 31-23, +22.50u, +20.8% ± 14.3 | 2,288, −31.87u, −2.8% | −9.37u |
| NBA | Current (D70) | — | 4,533 2051-2480-2, +44.13u, ROI +1.8%, flat +2.0% ± 2.0 | +44.13u |
| NBA | Trial, out of sample (recorded) | off | 4,533, +51.22u, +1.9% | **+51.22u** |
| NBA | Trial, in sample | off | 4,533, +51.59u, +1.9% | +51.59u |
| NBA | LOVE on (held), out of sample | 260 136-124, −2.08u, −0.4% ± 6.1 | 4,273, +50.68u, +2.2% | +48.60u |
| NFL | Current = trial (off) | — | 3,244 1592-1642-10, −27.60u, −1.7% | −27.60u |
| MLB | Current = trial (off) | — | 1,304 588-707-9, −11.92u, −1.7% | −11.92u |

  By season (out of sample, flat): NHL LOVE 2025 4 bets −53.3% (LIKE +1.7%), 2026 18 +15.6% (LIKE +6.8%); no LOVE before 2025 (under 30 prior LOVEs). NBA units, current → trial: 2020 −12.9 → −12.9, 2021 −1.1 → −1.1, 2022 +0.9 → +0.9, 2023 +26.5 → +26.5, 2024 +28.6 → +28.6, 2025 +7.8 → +13.1, 2026 −5.7 → −4.0. The standard error of LOVE's lead over LIKE: NHL +5.4 ± 22.3 points (z 0.2); the NBA's held LOVE −2.6 ± 6.4. **Directionally right in the NHL, nowhere near proven:** the label says trial. Beating the fair close: unchanged (the trial's props are bet at the close: no check). Small winners: the NBA is one (+51.2u, +1.9%); the NHL's best honest number is −19.9u, the NFL's −27.0u (the stake step, noise, not shipped), MLB's −11.9u (as it is): no honest stake rule makes them positive. D60's bar picked walk-forward from 50% to 54% moved a sport ±1u (noise): unchanged. D64's bar lifted for trusted bets (a barred prop allowed a LOVE) lost walk-forward: unchanged. The walk-forward schemes tried, in order, each honest, listed so the owner sees the search: by month with a 1-SE lead on 50 LOVEs and a wide grid (NBA LOVE −0.3%, NHL −24.5% on 27, NFL −18.3% on 86); by month on the trusted share alone (no LOVE earned); by season (NBA −1.0%); by month on 30 LOVEs with a 2% Kelly ceiling (shipped). At the owner's 2% Kelly floor every scheme's LOVE lost.
- **The owner's call (2026-10-20):** keep the lowered LOVE Kelly floor for the trial (confirmed after the D74 record); it stays as above until the trial ends.
- **Tests:** `tests/model-trial.test.mjs` (D73), `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D73.
- **Never:** a LOCK in a trial sport; a month's rule read off bets settled after the month began; the 2% floor lowered for anything but the trial's LOVEs; a LOVE on a line or a held bet; the desk writing `trusted.json`.

### D74. The best price: the Book and the Bot take and show the best US sportsbook's price, Pinnacle still the fair
- **Date:** 2026-10-20 (the owner's decision, via the coordinator: "the Book and the Bot take and show the BEST PRICE across US sportsbooks instead of DraftKings only. Only say which book in a hover title").
- **Amends:** D21's "`BOOK = 'draftkings'` only; the owner rejected best-price shopping" (the owner's own reversal: DraftKings stays the home book, whose line ESPN's free board shows and the desk prices at, and the fair's fallback; the price bet is the best US book's at that line); D26 (the books are named in one place, the price's hover, "Best price: FanDuel"; an unshopped price's hover names none).
- **Measured first:** findings.json (D67's dense archive): 1-8 cents better, better than DraftKings on 27-48% of picks, +1-2% of return. Live (2026-10-20, two paid probes, 8 credits): the NBA's 13 games, 78 sides: better than DraftKings on 53 (68%), median 4 cents (FanDuel 13, BetMGM 9, ESPN Bet 8, Bally 7, Caesars 7); one game's props, 114 sides: better on 77 (FanDuel 23, BetRivers 21, BetMGM 13). The live calls already named every US book (`WIDE_BOOKS`, one region's cost), so the prices cost no credits.
- **Rule (`best-price.mjs`):**
  - **The books:** the nine US books one region names (`US_BOOKS`: DraftKings, FanDuel, BetMGM, Caesars, ESPN Bet, Fanatics, BetRivers, Hard Rock, Bally). Pinnacle is the fair price and never a book to bet at; MMA's offshore totals anchors (fights-totals.mjs `TOTAL_ANCHORS`) never either. MMA's call names FanDuel, BetRivers and BetMGM beside them (`TOTAL_BOOKS`, 10: one region).
  - **At the Book's own line** (`bestPair`): each book's pair at DraftKings' line only (a book on another line is left out, never moved); a pair counts only when sane (both sides, its vig not negative: `SANE_SUM`; its no-vig within `FAIR_TOL` 4 points of the books' median at that line: a stale or in-play line dropped); each side the highest price, DraftKings first at a tie; never worse than DraftKings' (`sideBest`).
  - **The fair unchanged:** Pinnacle's no-vig where it posts the line, else DraftKings' own two prices (a pair of two books' best has less vig than any one book's). The residual's, the confidence layer's and the correction's price inputs read DraftKings' own pair too (`dk` on a side).
  - **Live:** `oddsapi.mjs linesFromEvent` (`best` per market), `run.mjs liveLines` (`lines.best`), `desk.mjs price` (a side's odds the best, its book `book`, DraftKings' kept as `dk`); `props.mjs propsFromBodies`, `withApiPrices`, `priceProps` (`bestOdds`, `bestBook`); `mma-desk.mjs linesOf`, `pricedFair`, `pickFor`; `fights-totals.mjs totalLinesOf`, `totalPickFor`. Kelly, the edge, the stake, the bands and the CLV all read the bet's price, so they read the best one. The bet's `book` is the best book's name (`nameOf`). ESPN's free board (DraftKings alone) still decides when a paid quote is bought (D55) and is bet on none (D30); a game's first props ask names the other books for the markets ESPN's board already lists, so they still add no market of their own.
  - **The board:** a side's `o` is its best price and `pb` its book's code (board.mjs; props `pb: [over's, under's]`; MMA's sides too, with `dko` DraftKings' price where another book's is shown, so the next run's fallback fair reads DraftKings'); no `pb` where the price is ESPN's board. The settler checks a user's price against the board seen (settle-lib.mjs boardObservations), which carries the best price, so it accepts it unchanged.
  - **The site:** the Book's price buttons and the ticket's price say "Best price: <book>" on hover where the board shopped it (book-names.ts), nothing else on the page changes; the Bot Picks' browser re-pricing (suggestions.ts, wallet-math.ts `withBestPrices`) takes the board's shopped price at the same line unless ESPN's DraftKings price is strictly better (then that, its book DraftKings), and never presents ESPN's price as a best one. A user's bet stores `book` (a code, at most 8 characters, firestore.rules, tests/rules/wallet.test.mjs).
  - **The replay** (`shopRows`, gate.mjs and gate-mma.mjs before the replay; `replay-record.mjs sideOf`): a row is placed at the best US price only where D67's dense archive has another US book at the row's own snapshot (within `SHOP_TOL` 10 minutes, each book as it stood then); everywhere else at DraftKings' own, and the file says so (`flags.shop`: shopped, better, DraftKings' alone; `PRICE_NOTE`; the Algorithm tab's caveat). `GATE_NO_SHOP=1` leaves it out.
  - `MODEL_EPOCH` and MMA's `MMA_START` bumped (a pricing change: every bet's price, Kelly and CLV move).
- **Measured (the replayed records, `gate.mjs --candidate worktree --champion 30a7af85 <sport> --refit --record`, 2026-10-21):**

| Sport | Bets at the best price (better than the Book's own) | Committed before (D73, the Book's prices) | Same run, the Book's prices (pre-D74 code, props refit) | After (D74) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | 532 of 3,538 (397) | 3,244 bets, -27.6u (-1.7%) | 3,229, -17.9u (-1.1%) | 3,538, -25.6u (-1.4%) |
| NBA | 119 of 4,785 (98) | 4,533, +51.2u (+1.9%) | 4,711, +47.5u (+1.7%) | 4,785, +47.7u (+1.6%) |
| NHL | 252 of 6,414 (201) | 2,342, -19.9u (-1.6%) | 6,252, +61.4u (+1.2%) | 6,414, +63.4u (+1.3%) |
| MLB | 0 of 1,294 (no dense archive) | 1,304, -11.9u (-1.7%) | 1,294, -2.8u (-0.4%) | 1,294, -2.8u (-0.4%) |
| MMA | 368 of 1,304 (313) | 1,038, -16.0u (-2.8%) | 1,038, -16.0u (-2.8%) | 1,304, -5.8u (-0.8%) |

  The middle column is the fair comparison: the champion in the same `--refit` run (the saved props fit was stale for both versions' code, so every team sport's record was refit; the props' settings searched again move the NHL from 2,342 to 6,252 bets on their own, not the price). The best price adds bets (a side that clears the vig at a better price): NHL +2.0u, NBA +0.2u, MMA +10.2u, MLB none (no dense archive), and the NFL -7.7u (309 more marginal close bets, mostly lines, that lose: its ROI -1.1% to -1.4%). The props are never shopped in the replay (the dense props snapshots are 3 hours out, never at a placement), so the history's best-price share is 3-28% of bets and the live gain (68% of sides better) is mostly unmeasured there.

- **Gate (D22):** SHIP in every sport, ties everywhere (no chance, projection or fit moves; only the placement's price): `gate.mjs --candidate worktree --champion 30a7af85 <sport> --refit --record` (MMA without --refit).
- **Code:** `best-price.mjs`, `oddsapi.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `desk.mjs`, `props.mjs`, `board.mjs`, `mma-desk.mjs`, `fights-totals.mjs`, `gate.mjs`, `gate-mma.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs`, `leagues.mjs` (`MODEL_EPOCH`); the site's `book-names.ts`, `suggestions.ts`, `book-rows.ts`, `book-page.component.*`, `replay-history.ts`, `account/wallet/*`; `firestore.rules`. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D74 (and D26's names map), `tests/book-best-price.test.mjs`, `tests/wallet.test.mjs`, `tests/rules/wallet.test.mjs`.
- **Never:** Pinnacle or an offshore book as the price; a best price at another line than the Book's; a best pair's prices as the fair; a price worse than DraftKings' called the best; ESPN's DraftKings price shown as a best one; a replayed bet at another book's price from a snapshot more than `SHOP_TOL` from its own; a book named anywhere on the page but the price's hover.

### D75. The props' fit is walk-forward: each season's props priced under settings searched on the seasons before it alone
- **Date:** 2026-10-21 (the owner's honesty check on D74's record, via the coordinator: "is each month's prop priced only with settings chosen from data before that month?").
- **Found:** no. The props' replay (`gate.mjs scoreVersion`) priced every season under one set of settings (`project.mjs fitStat`: each stat's K, form weight, scale, dispersion, calibration and context terms, searched on the rows' 30-70% window and checked on the last 30%). Without `--refit` those were the desk's saved fit, searched on games.json's two newest seasons and applied back to 2023 (look-ahead); with `--refit` (D74's run, the saved fit stale for the epoch) they were searched on every row the replay reads, D69's older seasons included, then used to price those same seasons (in-sample, and against D69's own "Never: the props' fit refit on the older seasons by the gate"). The players' bases were always learned game by game; the settings were not.
- **Why the NHL's bets nearly tripled in D74 (2,342 to 6,252):** not more props priced (the closes priced were the same, 1,245 / 41,584 / 39,130 / 45,290 / 5,291 by season), a fit tuned on the seasons it then priced: the whole-history search's settings disagree with the Book more often and in the direction that won in those seasons, so many more props cleared the vig (2026's season alone 3,037 bets +67.0u). Not legitimate: out of sample (below) the same seasons give 2,648 bets.
- **Rule (`gate.mjs` `PROPS_WF`, `PROPS_WF_BACK`, `wfSeasons`, the props layer's walk):** for each season S from the first with the history's props (or the ledger's current season), the settings are searched by `fitProps` on the rows of the seasons before S alone (no soft-term prior from the live state), then the season's rows are replayed under them (the reuse path, no search) and only S's player games are kept for layer a, the priced history and the ledger's matches. The players' games reach one season before the first props season (`PROPS_WF_BACK`, amends D69's `propsHistory`), so the first is priced on a fit of its own past; the first row season is a base only, never priced. Fits are cached by season (`.cache/model/fits/wf/<S>/<sport>.json`, this code's `fitCode` and that row count; `--refit` searches again; `GATE_WF_PREFIT=<seasons>` fits and exits, to run seasons in parallel). The record's fingerprint carries the walk's fits (`inputs.fit.wf`, the seasons and their rows), not the desk's saved fit. `GATE_PROPS_WF=0` is the old in-sample way, for comparing only. Still in sample, small: the ratings' few hyperparameters (games.json's fit) behind the older seasons' expected points, and a stat's league mean (`envFor`) over the rows through S.
- **Cost:** the NFL 40-65 minutes a season (2023-2026), the NBA 16-64, the NHL 8-38, MLB 3-13, once per code change; a record with the cache is minutes.
- **Measured (the replayed records, `gate.mjs --score-only worktree <sport> --record`, 2026-10-21; D74's committed record against this one; bets, units, ROI):**

| Sport | D74 (in-sample props fit) | Walk-forward | Props alone, D74 → walk-forward | LOVE (NHL's trial), D74 → walk-forward |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | 3,538, -25.6u (-1.4%) | 3,577, -49.6u (-2.5%) | 2,489 +9.7u → 2,528 -14.3u | none |
| NBA | 4,785, +47.7u (+1.6%) | 6,968, +21.3u (+0.5%) | 3,063 +33.6u → 5,246 +7.2u | none |
| NHL | 6,414, +63.4u (+1.3%) | 2,648, -14.2u (-1.0%) | 4,475 +93.6u → 567 +5.6u | 760 +29.7u → 22 +2.0u |
| MLB | 1,294, -2.8u (-0.4%) | 1,691, -15.8u (-1.6%) | 97 +6.6u → 492 -5.9u | none |
| MMA | 1,304, -5.8u (-0.8%) | unchanged (no props) | | |

  The game lines barely move (the props feed the lines only through the shared layers). D74's props gains were the in-sample fit's; the honest props record is about break-even (NBA +7.2u, NHL +5.6u on 5,246 and 567 bets) or losing (NFL, MLB).
- **Gate (D22):** a harness fix (the gate's own loader, both versions alike), not a model change; recorded with `--score-only` (one version: a champion run would replay the same walk). `MODEL_EPOCH` unchanged (the live desk's fit is untouched: it searches its own two seasons every day, which is walk-forward for its own bets).
- **Code:** `gate.mjs` (`PROPS_WF`, `PROPS_WF_BACK`, `wfSeasons`, `propsHistory`, the props layer, `inputsOf`). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D75 (and D69's season back).
- **Never:** a season's props priced under settings searched on rows from that season or later; one fit's settings applied back to earlier seasons in a record; a `--refit` that searches the replay's whole history and prices it.

### D76. The shopped-price guard: a bet that clears only at another book's price needs the month's minimum return there, else the practice stake
- **Date:** 2026-10-21 (the owner approved, via the coordinator: "a bet that only has value at a shopped price needs a minimum edge, or must also clear at DraftKings; choose it walk-forward per sport, apply it where it helps out of sample, NFL at least").
- **Rule (`best-price.mjs` `shopOnly`, `SHOP_GRID`, `SHOP_MIN_N`, `shopT`, `walkShopGuard`, `shopGuardOf`, `shopTOf`, `SHOP_GUARD`):** a side is shop-only where its expected return at DraftKings' own price is 0 or less and above 0 at the shopped one. Each month's minimum return T is picked from `SHOP_GRID` (0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 6%, or Infinity: clear at DraftKings too) on the shop-only bets settled before the month began, flat: the T whose bets under it lost the most (none lost, or under `SHOP_MIN_N` 30 bets: 0, no guard). A shop-only bet under T is a LIKE at the practice stake (`PRACTICE_UNITS`), held there through the tiers as a barred bet: a shrink, never off (gates shrink, never stop). Replay: `replay-record.mjs` (`shopOnlyBets`, `flags.shopGuard`: the shop-only bets, those under T, their flat units, the units before and after, `next` the T for what comes next). Live: `run.mjs` reads `next` from the sport's replay-record.json (`r.shopT`) and a game line under it takes the practice stake with the guard's why. Props are never shopped in the replay (D74), so the guard has no evidence on them and reads only the lines.
- **Measured (the walk-forward records above, the guard off against on, the walk's own months out of sample):**

| Sport | Shop-only bets | Under the month's T | Their flat units | Units, off → on | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL | 305 | 243 | +2.1u | -49.6u → -49.6u (every one at the practice stake already: the lines' bar) | on (the owner's "NFL at least"); no gain measured |
| NHL | 192 | 112 | -5.6u | -14.2u → -13.7u (+0.5u) | on |
| NBA | 85 | 36 | +12.1u | no change (practice already) | off: the guarded bets won |
| MMA | 270 | 28 | +5.9u | no change | off: the guarded bets won |
| MLB | 0 (no dense archive) | | | | off |

  D74's "about 300 NFL bets that lose (-7.7u)" was the in-sample record's; under the walk-forward fit the NFL's 305 shop-only lines win +2.1u flat and sit at the practice stake already, so the guard changes nothing there today. Its live T (1%) applies where a line segment has earned full stakes (the NFL's late overs).
- **Gate (D22):** the NHL +0.5u; elsewhere a tie. Recorded with D75's records.
- **Code:** `best-price.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `gate.mjs` and `gate-mma.mjs` (`GATE_SHOP_COMPARE=<dir>`, measuring only: the replay with the guard off and on). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D76.
- **Never:** a shop-only bet turned off or tracked for the guard's sake; a month's T read off bets settled after it began; the guard on in a sport where its walk's guarded bets won, without the owner's say.

### D77. The news lag read from prices alone (Gemini's procedure): measured on every season, no edge, so D66 stays live-only and the desk takes no price trigger
- **Date:** 2026-10-21 (the round's first item; the owner: infer when the market learned a star was out from the price archive alone, never from box scores).
- **How (scratchpad `r7/lagprice.mjs`):** each event's DraftKings boards at every snapshot the archive has (the open, D61's intraday 24/12/6/3/1 hours from the puller's raw snapshots, D67's dense 3-hour props for 2024, the close). A star is his team's top two points lines or any 17.5+ (NBA), top two shots lines (NHL), QB1, RB1 and the top two receivers by line (NFL), read at the first snapshot with his team's lines; his team from his games before this one. **The signal:** his own prop posted at one snapshot and gone at the next while the board is up (at least 4 lines on the stat, 60% of the snapshot before, two teammates still posted). **The bet:** at that first snapshot, every teammate's over on a stat D66 tags whose line hasn't moved since the snapshot before (the line no higher, the over's no-vig up under 3 points), at DraftKings' price then. The box score only grades (a teammate who didn't play: void). The fair close is Pinnacle's no-vig at the bet's line, else DraftKings', else the close's line converted (flagged; the same-line subset reads the same). Also split: the team's spread moved against it by 1 point or more at the same snapshot (the line signal).
- **Measured (the unmoved teammates' overs, flat 1u at DraftKings' price; the fair close's expected return and beat share):**

| Sport | Season | Signals | Bets | Hit (Book's fair) | Flat ROI | At the fair close | Beat it |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | 2024 (dense 3h only) | | 836 | 53.1% (49.9%) | -0.1% ± 3.3 | -6.2% | 6% |
| NBA | 2025 | | 2,278 | 50.5% (49.7%) | -4.8% ± 2.0 | -5.0% | 18% |
| NBA | 2026 | | 2,402 | 49.0% (49.9%) | -8.1% ± 1.9 | -5.2% | 17% |
| NBA | all | 957 | 5,516 | 50.2% | -5.5% ± 1.3 | -5.2% | 16% |
| NHL | 2025 / 2026 / 2027 | 253 | 1,023 / 754 / 30 | 48.0 / 51.7 / 50.0% | -9.8 / -0.7 / -2.6% | -5.8 / -5.4 / -6.6% | 3% |
| NFL | 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 | 104 | 85 / 527 / 72 / 51 | 40.0 / 51.4 / 34.7 / 51.0% | -27.5 / -4.1 / -35.8 / -4.7% | -6.2 / -6.5 / -5.3 / -5.3% | 5% |

  The NBA's points overs (the stat D66's history measured): 867, -3.4% ± 3.2, -4.7% at the fair close. Before the close only (the 24, 6, 3 and 1-hour snapshots): 2025 1,841 -4.1%, 2026 2,020 -7.3%. With the spread moved against the team: 2,054, -7.3%. **Why:** when the Book pulls the star's prop it re-prices his teammates in the same snapshot: 8,263 of the NBA's 13,779 teammate lines had already moved at the first snapshot without him (-9.4% flat on them), and the ones left unmoved lose about the vig at the close. D66's own finding agrees (once the star's prop is pulled the gap is gone); its +9.4% was the box score's hindsight (late scratches the market hadn't seen), which a price-only bettor can't select.
- **Held:** no historical evidence for `newsLag` (nothing to earn: every cell runs at or under the vig at the fair close) and no price trigger on the live desk (the rule was "if it holds"). D66 stands as it is: live-only, no prior, the NBA's tag only while the star's own prop is still posted.
- **Paid calls:** none (the archive, the puller's raw snapshots in `.cache/model/intraday`, ESPN's and nflverse's free box scores).
- **Gate (D22):** nothing priced moved. Not an epoch change. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D77.
- **Never:** a news-lag case selected by who didn't play; a price trigger on the desk without a walk-forward edge at the fair close.

### D78. The props' four pieces (early exit, blowout minutes, fatigue, play type with on/off): measured walk-forward on D75's replay, none lowers log loss beyond noise, none ships; the play-by-play aggregates built for the owner
- **Date:** 2026-10-21 (the round's items 2-5).
- **How (scratchpad `r7/`: `feat-nba.mjs`, `feat-nhl.mjs`, `feat-nfl.mjs`, `feat-mlb.mjs`, `layer.mjs`, `run-layer.mjs`, `mix-nba.mjs`):** D75's walk-forward replay's prop closes (`REPLAY_DUMP`: every close the record prices, with the Bot's chance and the Book's fair; NBA 225,438, NHL 132,540, NFL 38,798, MLB 30,360 starters' rows) joined to features read only from games before the row's day (the close's inactives and the NFL's injury report known by then, D48). For each stat and season S, a logistic layer fit on the seasons before S alone: the base (a + b logit(Bot) + c logit(Book): a refit of the blend) and the base plus one piece's features (ridge 50; 10 to 1000 checked), scored on S. A piece's delta is its log loss less the base's on the same rows, its SE clustered by game. **The bar, set before the NFL and NHL were read:** lower log loss at z 2.5 or more (about 30 stat-and-piece cells a sport) and in every held-out season.
- **The pieces:** **early exit** (a hurdle's drivers: his share of games under half his usual minutes, time on ice, snaps or (MLB) 12 outs, shrunk; his minutes' spread; back from an absence; a back-to-back; the spread's size; the NFL's Questionable and Doubtful), **blowout minutes** (P(|margin| >= 15) from the close's spread, NBA sd 12.5; 17 and 13.5 in the NFL; by starter or bench, favorite or dog; and, NBA, the explicit two-state mixture: the starters' and the bench's minutes scaled in the blowout state, the mean held, the shape's change added to the Bot's chance, the two scales picked on the seasons before), **fatigue** (minutes or time on ice over the last 7 and 14 days against his usual, two bins; 3-in-4, 4-in-5; time zones moved and which way; the NFL's short and long rest; MLB's pitch counts and rest), **play type** (NBA: his rim, mid and three shares from ESPN's play-by-play against the defense's concessions by zone to date, its free-throw and assisted rates allowed; NFL: his deep, middle and red-zone shares and depth from nflverse's play-by-play against the defense's deep, middle and depth allowed, EPA a target and a carry allowed, the backs' targets allowed) and **on/off** (each regular out tonight: his stat in his team's games that regular missed this season against all of his, shrunk, the logs summed).
- **Measured (the delta against the base, log loss x 10^4, minus is better; held out NBA 2025-26, NHL 2025-27, NFL 2024-26 (passing 2025-26), MLB 2024-26):**

| Sport | Stat | Early exit | Blowout | Fatigue | Play type | On/off | All |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | pts | -0.1 ± 0.5 | -0.0 ± 0.3 | +0.7 ± 0.6 | +0.3 ± 0.2 | -0.1 ± 0.0 | +0.8 ± 0.9 |
| NBA | reb | +0.6 ± 1.0 | +0.3 ± 0.3 | +1.9 ± 1.5 | +0.4 ± 0.4 | -0.1 ± 0.4 | +3.0 ± 1.9 |
| NBA | ast | +0.8 ± 1.1 | -0.1 ± 0.4 | +1.0 ± 2.1 | -1.3 ± 0.9 | +0.4 ± 0.4 | +0.7 ± 2.6 |
| NBA | fg3 | -0.4 ± 0.7 | -0.3 ± 0.3 | -0.1 ± 0.6 | -1.5 ± 1.1 | -0.1 ± 0.2 | -3.1 ± 1.5 |
| NBA | pra | -0.0 ± 0.5 | +0.1 ± 0.2 | +1.2 ± 0.9 | -0.1 ± 0.3 | +0.0 ± 0.1 | +0.9 ± 1.1 |
| NHL | sog | -0.1 ± 0.6 | -0.0 ± 0.1 | +0.7 ± 0.9 | | +0.1 ± 0.3 | +1.0 ± 1.0 |
| NHL | pts | -0.5 ± 1.4 | +0.0 ± 0.1 | +2.3 ± 1.1 | | +0.1 ± 0.2 | +1.8 ± 1.7 |
| NHL | saves | +1.8 ± 2.6 | -0.1 ± 0.1 | +8.7 ± 5.2 | | +0.1 ± 0.0 | +9.6 ± 5.4 |
| NFL | recYds | | | +8.3 ± 3.6 | +7.3 ± 2.0 | | +14.7 ± 5.0 |
| NFL | rec | | | | +2.6 ± 2.3 | | +7.0 ± 6.8 |
| NFL | rushRecYds | | -0.1 ± 0.1 | +8.7 ± 9.0 | -5.2 ± 3.6 | | |
| NFL | passYds | +5.8 ± 5.8 | +0.2 ± 0.2 | +2.8 ± 13.5 | +12.2 ± 7.5 | -1.1 ± 0.8 | +20.5 ± 17.0 |
| NFL | passInt | +0.8 ± 6.3 | -0.0 ± 0.1 | -0.2 ± 7.4 | -25.1 ± 9.8 | +5.6 ± 4.6 | -18.0 ± 14.3 |
| MLB | k | +0.2 ± 0.6 | | +2.5 ± 2.1 | | | +2.8 ± 2.2 |
| MLB | outs | +0.1 ± 0.7 | | -0.4 ± 1.1 | | | -0.4 ± 1.3 |

  (Blank NFL cells and the NFL's other stats: |z| under 1.5.) The NBA's two-state mixture by the spread's size (held out, the mixture's log loss less the Bot's, 0-4 / 4-8 / 8-12 / 12+ points): pts -0.3 / +1.4 / +0.4 / +0.1; reb -0.7 / -2.2 / +1.0 / +1.3; ast -1.8 / -0.7 / -2.7 / +0.9; fg3 -2.2 / -4.4 / +1.6 / +0.5; pra +0.1 / 0.0 / +3.9 / +1.2: what it gains sits in the close games, not the blowouts (a wider shape, not the bench state), and points and PRA get worse. The features read right; the residuals by each one's quintile are flat (the Bot and the Book price them already: the model has its blowout, back-to-back, usage, vacated, opponent and depth terms).
- **The one cell past the bar, held:** the NFL's interceptions with the defense's EPA allowed (-25.1 ± 9.8, z 2.6, both 2025's 536 rows and 2026's 180 better; at ridge 200 -9.9 ± 3.5). The third season it can be checked on, 2024 (fit on 2023's 479 rows), shows nothing (-1 against the base, and the base's own refit on one season loses 0.009 to the Bot). One cell of about 120, on 716 rows, not repeated: not shipped. The interceptions stay at the practice stake by D64's bar meanwhile; a candidate for the next round once 2026 adds rows.
- **The play-by-play aggregates (the owner's note: clean datasets the stats site might use; not on the site: the owner's decision):** `apps/nfl/scripts/pbp/` (`build.mjs`, a README listing every field, `data/<season>.json` 2016-2026, 3.5 MB: per player-season target and air-yards shares, aDOT, deep and middle target rates, red-zone and end-zone targets, YAC over expected, EPA and success by role, CPOE, run-gap shares; per team-season offense and defense EPA, pass rate over expected, red-zone TD rate, depth allowed) and `apps/nba/scripts/pbp/` (`fetch.mjs`, `build.mjs`, a README, `data/<season>.json` 2021-2026, 1.4 MB: per player-season shot zones and FG% by zone, assisted %, free-throw rate, clutch shooting, on/off per 48 from the lineups read; per team-season offense and defense by zone). The raw play-by-play stays in `.cache` (the NBA's about 90 MB, gitignored). Slot alignment isn't in the free data (the README says so; the middle-target rate is a proxy). The bettor reads none of the committed files.
- **Gate (D22):** nothing priced moved (no piece shipped). Not an epoch change. No re-record: the pricing code is unchanged, so `--record` would replay the same algorithm (the record guard holds input-only moves). Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D78.
- **Never:** a props piece shipped on the seasons it was picked on alone; a feature read from the game itself (its minutes, its box) for a row priced before it; the raw play-by-play committed.

### D79. The consistency round: no team, player or player type beats the close out of sample; the Bot's own consistent losers (a stat and side, a line market and side) stake the practice stake
- **Date:** 2026-10-22 (the owner's "consistent winners and losers regardless of odds" round).
- **How it was measured (scratchpad `cons/`, research only):** every DraftKings close since 2020 (lines) and since 2023 (props, the gate's priced closes joined to the archive's DraftKings over/under prices, 696,820 rows), each row's result against the close's no-vig and its flat return at DraftKings' own price. Persistence walk-forward only: season to next season and first half to second half (team or player means, correlation), the between-key spread of true skill (method of moments), and the trailing 20/40/82 games (teams) or 20/50/150 rows (players) shrunk toward 0, tested on the later seasons (lines 2023 on; props after each sport's first season) with day-clustered SEs and BH q-values across every test; selection rules (side, threshold) set on the earlier seasons alone. Splits: home/away, favorite/dog, the NFL's coach-and-quarterback era (resets the window).
- **Teams (lines):** nothing persists. Season-to-season correlation of a team's result against the close is within noise of 0 in every sport and market (NFL ATS r 0.00 on 160 pairs, NBA ATS −0.05, NHL puck line −0.01, MLB run line 0.11 p 0.19; scattered p < 0.05 of about 70 tests, signs flipping between the season and half-season reads); the true between-team spread against the close is 0 in the NFL and NHL. The top fifth of team-seasons against the spread went on to −7.1% (NBA), −5.5% (NHL), −1.5% (MLB), +2.5% (NFL) the next season; the bottom fifth −4.7%, −3.9%, −3.9%, +2.2%. No rolling test survives BH (best q 0.46: MLB home overs, whose sign flipped from the tuning seasons).
- **Players (props):** nothing beats the vig. Season-to-season r near 0 in every stat but the NBA's rebounds (r 0.15 on 428, p 0.001; against the Bot's chance too) and threes (0.11, p 0.03). Following the trailing leaders (top tenth of the shrunk signal) loses −2% to −10% held out in every stat. As a shift on the Bot's chance (fit on the tuning seasons) it lowers held-out log loss only in NBA rebounds (−0.6 ×10⁻⁴, 2 SE) and worsens it elsewhere: watching, not built.
- **Player types (side picked on the tuning seasons, held-out ROI at DraftKings' price):** a line 25% over the player's last five (role up), 20% under, his first 15 props, a new team (15 rows after a change), late season, heavy trailing minutes late, home/away, five straight overs or unders, the stat's low and high lines, the Bot's big disagreements: none beats break-even beyond noise. Unders lose less than overs everywhere (the over shade): the best are NBA PRA unders after a team change +7.5% on 427 (tune n 108; z 1.7) and NFL unders after a role-up line +1.0% on 3,153. Price or player: the residual against the Bot equals the one against the Book in every segment, so where a line lags a role change it lags the Bot's projection the same, and not by enough to clear the vig. (A first cut read a game's own minutes from its box score for its other props and showed PRA overs +10%; fixed to the games before, it's gone.)
- **The Bot's own losers:** by player (his earlier replayed bets' record) none: NBA players with a bad record went +4.7% on 281 next; by team none beyond noise. By stat and side, yes: the bad keys kept losing in the later half of the NBA's replay (−12.7% flat on 553 against +5.6%, z −3.8, BH q < 0.01): NBA rebound overs −21.1% flat on 175 (2023-24), then −9.1% on 311 (2024-25) at an average 0.82u, the D64 bar standing aside there by its own place.
- **Rule (`desk.mjs` `OWN_BAR`, `OWN_BAR_SPORTS`, `OWN_K`, `OWN_THR`, `OWN_MIN`, `ownKey`, `ownVerdict`, `ownBarOf`, `ownBarGate`, `readOwnBar`; `replay-record.mjs` `ownBar`; `gate.mjs` `recordScore` writes `own-bar.json`; `run.mjs` the lines' and props' gates):** a bet's key is `prop:<stat>:<side>` or `<market>:<side>`; its stake is held to the practice stake and a LIKE where the Bot's own bets on that key settled before the moment number `OWN_MIN` (10) or more and their flat return summed over n + `OWN_K` (20) is `OWN_THR` (−10%) or worse. The bet is still placed and graded (D1), so the key can earn its stake back. The parameters were fixed before the replay was run with them. The replay walks it after every other stake rule; the record writes each key's replayed record before the epoch; the live desk pools it with its graded bets since the epoch (tracked rows, voids and practice picks aside). On in the NBA, NHL, NFL and MLB; not MMA (unmeasured: no dump of its replay this round).
- **Measured (the gate's replayed records, the bar off (`env:OWN_BAR=0`, the same code as master otherwise) against on; bets and record unchanged):** NBA +34.0u → +59.1u (6,484 bets), NHL −26.9u → −21.9u (2,562), MLB −15.8u → −9.9u (1,691), NFL −49.6u → −47.7u (3,577): +35.0u in all. Held going in (own-bar.json, flat on the replay before the epoch): NBA rebound overs −69.9u on 545, threes overs −25.0u on 136, assist overs, away spreads; NHL total unders −46.4u on 355, points unders −16.6u on 140, shots overs; MLB away moneylines −51.7u on 345, strikeout overs −24.4u on 142; NFL away moneylines −53.2u on 304, interception overs, receiving overs. The like-for-like NHL record (this cache) is −26.9u before; the committed −13.7u on 2,648 was another cache's inputs. On the round's dump the two looser variants gave +40.8u and +30.5u: the sign holds either way.
- **Gate (D22):** `--candidate worktree --champion env:OWN_BAR=0`, every team sport: SHIP, ties everywhere (no chance, projection or pick moves; the replay guard passes: the same bets, the same beat share). Run against the bar-off worktree, not origin/master, because the champion's checkout hashes LF files against this worktree's CRLF ones and so re-searched every props fit (hours a sport). Not an epoch change (D4): stakes on top of unchanged chances.
- **Tests:** `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D79.
- **Never:** a key turned off by its own record; a key judged on a bet settled after the bet's moment; a player's, team's or type's own record used to size a stake (measured: no persistence); the desk writing `own-bar.json`.

## Loops

Each loop below runs by itself. For each one: what it changes, how often, what it reads, and what it must never touch.

| Loop | Changes | How often | Inputs | Never |
|---|---|---|---|---|
