| Trust fit (`desk.mjs` `fitTrust`, `fitBuckets`; `run.mjs` `refitTrust`) | `state.trust` and line buckets: stake size, the CHECK bar's cleared chance, research pricing | every run | bets and tracked rows since `MODEL_EPOCH` (practice included), results and close `q`, the backtest | read pre-epoch rows; decide whether a prop plays (D18) |
| Live calibration (`calib.mjs`; `run.mjs` `refitCalib`) | `state.calib`: shifts on the model chance (guards, CHECKs, research pricing, the trusted fallback) | every run, at most `CAL_STEP` a UTC day | every graded prop's raw chance (`modelRaw`) across epochs, other sports pooled | fit on its own shifted chance; stack under the correction; jump to 0 |
| Learned correction (`correct.mjs`; `run.mjs` `refitCorrection`), off since the gate's verdict (D18, `CORR_PRICING`) | each prop's priced chance `p`, and the `topN` ranking, when on | every run | every graded prop (bets, tracked, practice), raw chance, no-vig, result, close `q` (CLV v2 carried to the bet's line) | read the calibrated chance; let the trust shrink on top |
| CHECK learning (`check-learn.mjs`) | role thresholds (soft weights, 0.25 a run), cause bet rules, `ROLE_BET` gates | every run | every graded CHECK, closed ones too for the gates | act under 40 decided; apply a loosening before its interval clears |
| CHECK patterns (`check-patterns.mjs`) | resolves open CHECKs by rule (cost 0) | every run, free, `--dry` too | researched CHECKs, `check-seeds.json` | count its own rule resolutions; go past `MAX_RATE`; skip the gates or caps (D11) |
| CHECK research (`research.mjs`) | verdicts, injury report inputs (`researchInputs`), research bets | hourly with `CHECK_RESEARCH=1`, under `CAP_WINDOWS` | open CHECKs, the injury report, rosters, web search | store a failed search (D7); raise a cap; run on `--dry` (saved findings replay only) |
| Research level and gate (`researchLevelOf`, `researchGateOf`) | stake level of research bets | every run | research bets since the epoch, every sport, CLV v2 `prob` | turn research off (D1, D19) |
| Miss review (`miss-review.mjs`) | the Self-check panel's recommendations and bug flags | daily from 12:00 UTC, batch | each sport's biggest misses since its last review | write a weight or `state.json` (D10) |
| Post-mortems and stat re-checks (`postmortem.mjs`; `run.mjs` 4b) | grades, a broken premise's `weight`, re-grades after stat corrections | every run (re-check a day and a half after the start) | finals' box scores, the league's own box (NHL) | grade a DNP as a loss |
| CLV capture (`clv.mjs`; `run.mjs` `captureClose`) | each bet's `clv` (v2: `prob`, `q`, `beat`) | every run | ESPN closes once final, the board's ladder | count a moved line it can't carry as 0 |
| Weekly self-check (`selfcheck.mjs`; `model-desk-weekly.yml`) | `selfcheck.json`, a report only | weekly | ledgers, CHECKs, `state.json` history in git, the gate's `history-leaks.json` | change the bettor |
| Morning snapshot (`odds-history.mjs morning`; the desk workflow) | `odds-history/morning/<day>.jsonl.gz` | once a day a sport, from 10 AM Eastern | The Odds API (the `snapshot` budget, 300 a day), games.json | price or place a bet (D30); spend past its cap or the hold |
| Residual model (`residual.mjs`; `residual-train.mjs`, `model-desk-weekly.yml`; on since 2026-10-19, D32) | `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/residual.json`: the monthly snapshots (the Book's error on the Bot's lean and its own shade) and each kind's blend weight, the soft blend of every priced prop's and game line's chance with the corrected one | weekly (the self-check's workflow), behind the hold-out guard; the blend at most `BLEND_STEP` a refit | the priced history (every open and close), the anchored raw, the result; its current price with the blend off (`RESID_OFF` in its process) | read the ledger's blended prices; fit on top of its own blend; ship a kind worse than the Book on its newest months; be fit by the desk or a gate record |
| The anchor's pull (`anchor.mjs` `fitAnchor`; `gate.mjs --record`) | `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/anchor.json`: each stat's pull toward the book's implied mean, 0 the bot's own number (D33) | each gate record (a shipped version), never a desk run | the priced history's closes, walk-forward by season | fit on the ledger's rows; a floor or a prior toward the book; a pull on a stat where the bot's number beats the book's out of sample |
| MMA trust from the history (`mma-desk.mjs` `fitMmaTrust`; the rows from `gate.mjs --record`, D36; a fit a lead band, the early band untapered, D37) | `state.trust` for MMA's moneylines and round totals by lead band: the stake, the practice cap, the round totals' live switch | every MMA run | the live MMA bets since `MMA_START` (whole) and `apps/mma/scripts/model/history-rows.json` (the priced history as the shipped model priced it walk-forward, recency-weighted, capped at `PRIOR_ROWS`) | write the rows or the prior; read the ledger's rows into the history; fit the history's chances |

| The replayed record (`replay-record.mjs`; `gate.mjs --record`, D47) | `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/replay-record.json`: the Bot's history before the epoch (today's algorithm over the priced history at its own placement, bands and stakes); `picks.mjs recordBlocks` merges it with the live ledger since the epoch into picks.json's record and recent blocks every run | the file each gate record (a shipped version), never a desk run; the merge every run | the gate's walk-forward rows (lines and props, open and close), the lineup risk fit on the seasons before each row's, the live ledger's bets on games from the epoch on | be written by the desk; price a row from a snapshot after its placement; count a game in both parts; feed the trust, the calibration, the residual or the wallet's duel |
| The news-speed input (`fastnews.mjs`; `news-signals.mjs postSignals`; D49) | the coming games' report copies (Sleeper's statuses, the reporters' claims), `news-fast.json` (the claims), `news-speed.json` (each source's first sight) | Sleeper's dump once a UTC day; the posts each desk run (the news step, after the bettor); the report copies every run | Sleeper's players dump, the curated Bluesky accounts' posts, ESPN's report and rosters | apply a status after the run or outside the game's window; soften ESPN's; price free text; pull the dump twice a day; bet early on it above the practice stake before `fastEarned` |

| The reason tiers (`reasons.mjs`; the file from `gate.mjs --record`, D58) | each bet's band (LIKE; LOVE on a proven reason, LOCK on two or a strong one, over the 2% Kelly floor) and its stake cap; the reasons the Book's popover lists | the file each gate record; the read every run | `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/reasons.json` (the replayed record's bets walk-forward: each market's reasons, flat, recency-weighted; the tiers' own place) | be written by the desk; judge a reason on a pick settled after the bet; show a tier before its place is earned; a LOVE under the floor; move a chance |
| The beat-the-close bar (`desk.mjs` `lineGate`, `propGate`; `replay-record.mjs` `beatBar`, D60) | a market's stake: the practice stake while its bets beat the fair close under 52.5% | every run (live), the record (replay) | the ledger since the epoch; the replay's own bets before each | set a stake to 0 or move a chance |
| The two-band trial (`trusted.mjs`; the file from `gate.mjs --record`, D73) | a trial sport's bands (LIKE, LOVE) and its props' stakes | the file each gate record; the read every run | `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/trusted.json` (the props' pieces' weights and the walk's next rule, from the replayed record walk-forward by month) | be written by the desk; pick a month's rule on bets settled after it began; a LOCK; move a chance |
| The intraday history (`intraday.mjs`, D61) | `odds-history/intraday/` | once, by hand on the big plan | The Odds API's historical snapshots | run in the desk; price a bet |

### Recency in the fits (audited 2026-10-18)

- **Team ratings:** decay geometrically. Each offseason multiplies a rating by `revert`: NFL, NBA and MLB at 0.5, NHL at 0.98. An NHL rating carries almost whole into the next season.
- **A player's base:**
  - This season's games against a prior, with weight `priorW = K / (games + K)`. K runs 2 to 32 by stat.
  - In the prior, last season counts 1 and every older season 0.5, lumped together.
  - The last 5 games' form weight runs from 0 to 0.4.
  - The defense tables count this season 1, last season 0.5, older seasons 0.
- **The fitted settings themselves** (each stat's K, form weight, scale, dispersion, calibration and context terms) are fit flat over their window. The NFL's window is roughly 2019 to 2023 and is held out on 2023 to 2026; the other sports' window is two seasons. The recency half-life `hl` (`HL` 3/2/1 seasons) is tried on every stat, but only NFL passing yards kept it (3 seasons).
- **Since 2026-10-18 (the owner: recent seasons count more, rule eras respected), every stat's fit (`project.mjs` `fitStat`) tries:**
  - A half-life by season, `HL` 8, 4, 3, 2 or 1, picked inside the fit window. Held out, the best is tried first. If it fails, the fit steps softer to the longer half-lives that also beat none inside, `HL_TRIES` (3) in all. The gate is the same as before (`DRAFT_GAIN`, no larger bias).
  - A rule-era weight (`ERAS`: NFL 2024-09-01, the kickoff; MLB 2023-03-30, the pitch clock and shift ban), at `ERA_W` 0.5 or 0.25 on the games before the break. It is judged where the fit and held-out windows have 200 or more games on each side: fit on the games before the newest half of those since the break, scored on that half, kept with `DRAFT_GAIN` a game or more (`floors.era`).
  - Either kept, the lean and r are fit again from the newest game over both windows by those weights (`floors.hl.live`). None kept, the settings are as fit.
- **Measured 2026-10-18 (the NFL's dry-run refit):** no stat kept a half-life. Passing yards' 3 failed its bias test, and the rest failed on gain. The era weight 0.25 was kept on passing yards (+0.0003 a game), attempts (+0.0003), completions (+0.0007), carries (+0.0096) and rush+rec yards (+0.0004). Their live lean went 0.978 to 0.974, 0.994 to 0.983, 0.996 to 0.982, 0.979 to 0.986 (carries' r 4 to 6) and 0.988 to 1.002. Passing TDs, interceptions, rushing yards, receiving yards and receptions stayed flat. MLB's two-season window is all after its break, and the NBA and NHL have none, so none of them has an era to weigh. No NHL, MLB or NBA stat took a half-life either.
- **The gate (D22, `--refit`, against origin/master):** NFL props layer a, deciding seasons, 3.6264 to 3.6251 (95% -0.0021 to -0.0006, better); older seasons tied. NHL, MLB and NBA layer a tied (NHL 1.4963 both, NBA 2.4747 both). SHIP.
- **No fit uses 25-year-old seasons.** The NFL's history starts in 2016 and the others' at the last season.
- **Gaps:**
  - Only the lean and r are refit live by recency. K, the form weight, the context terms and the calibration are still fit flat over the fit window.
  - The prior lumps older seasons at 0.5.
  - The MMA fit, the calibration and the correction weight all their rows flat. The last two only have rows since 2026-10-08.

- **O3** (D32, the residual model against the gate's close leg): the owner turned it on ("whatever gets us closer to winning more"), on the results, CLV and hit evidence over the close leg. D22 carries the exception as a documented, pinned rule; the residual model is on. See D22 and D32.

### D81. One price a side: the board holds a fresh paid best price over a later ESPN read, the page holds the same rule, and Bot Picks judges every row at that price
- **Date:** 2026-10-22.
- **Owner:** "Bot Picks, the game board, the slip and the popover must ALWAYS show the same price for the same side/line. If the price moves so the pick no longer has an edge, the recommendation is removed from Bot Picks. The Bot's own bet stays in Open Bets and the Algorithm tab." The report: Bot Picks' #2, Ryan Nugent-Hopkins Under 1.5 Shots, the Bot's bet at +115 (Bally Bet, the paid quote's best US price, D74, 22:43 UTC); the game's board at -120 (DraftKings on ESPN's free board, `sug.src` 'espn'), a PASS there.
- **Cause:** a paid props ask comes only when the Bot is about to bet (D42's deferred ask, D55); every run between, the NHL's and NBA's props are priced off ESPN's free board, DraftKings alone, and the board's row was rebuilt from that read with nothing of the paid quote kept. So the next run's board showed DraftKings' -120 while picks.json kept the bet's +115, and the page showed both: the row at the bet's band and Kelly, the board at its PASS. The game lines had the same hole between paid lines calls.
- **Rule:**
  - **The board** (`board.mjs heldPaid`, `boardMarkets`/`boardProp`'s `paid` and `now`, run.mjs `prevBoardGame`/`prevBoardProp`): a shopped side carries its paid quote's time (`pt`) and DraftKings' own price in it (`dko`, a prop's `[over's, under's]`; none where DraftKings was the best). A side this run priced at ESPN's free price alone takes the last board's paid price at the same line where the quote is still fresh (`PAID_FRESH_MS`, an hour), DraftKings' price hasn't moved since (`PAID_MOVED`, half a point of the side's chance against the quote's `dko`) and the paid price is strictly better; its Kelly and band worked at it. Else ESPN's, no book. Never a later DraftKings read silently over a fresh better paid price; never a stale one held.
  - **The expiry:** an hour is D55's shortest stale quote (`LINES_FRESH.refresh`: the other books and Pinnacle move without DraftKings), the D30 reading: a price older than that isn't one the Bot knows now. The move is D55's `LINES_MOVED.chance` on one side (the API's rounding, 0.1 to 0.3 points, isn't a move). Bets are unchanged: D30 still places a bet only at this run's own quote; the held price is the board's, a suggestion's and a user's price only.
  - **The page** (`paid-price.ts`, the same constants, pinned equal): the board as loaded goes through `freshBoard` once (lines.service `model`): a side past its quote's hour falls to its `dko`, no book, its Kelly and band worked again there (`bandAt`, game-lines' call's rule). ESPN's live game lines take the board's shopped price only while DraftKings on ESPN hasn't moved from the quote's `dko` (wallet-math `bestPriceOf`). Bot Picks' quote (suggestions.ts `quoteOf`) reads the price the game board shows: a game line ESPN's at the same line with the board's best over it, a prop the board's as loaded. A board without `pt` (an older one, MMA's paid-only board) as it is.
  - **Bot Picks** (suggestions.ts `rejudged`, book-page `withSuggestions`): every Bot's bet row is judged again at its side's current price as a suggestion is (`whatIfOf` over the bet, `priceAt` there: its tier and the desk's caps held): no edge there (a PASS), off Bot Picks (still in Open Bets and the Algorithm tab); an edge, its band and Kelly the current price's, the bet's own kept (`locked`: the K popover's why is the bet's, its what-if opens at the current price). No price known (the game off the board, its line moved), the bet as it stands. The game board's Bot side reads the same judged rows (book-page `boardPicks`), so its band and Kelly are Bot Picks'. The slip takes the row's current price (`rowSelection`, as before).
- **Measured:** the reported case pinned (`tests/book-paid-price.test.mjs`): the bet at +115 Bally Bet, the board's ESPN read -120: with the quote fresh (20 minutes) the board, the game board and Bot Picks all show +115 at Bally Bet and the row stays; at 61 minutes all show -120, a PASS, and the row and any suggestion are gone; DraftKings moved since the quote, -120 at any age.
- **Gate (D22):** a tie by construction: no chance, stake, placement price or bet moves (D30 unchanged), only the board's shown price between paid quotes. `MODEL_EPOCH` not bumped (no bet changes).
- **Code:** `board.mjs` (`PAID_FRESH_MS`, `PAID_MOVED`, `dkMoved`, `paidFresh`, `heldPaid`, `pt`/`dko`), `run.mjs` (`prevBoardGame`, `prevBoardProp`); the site's `book/paid-price.ts`, `suggestions.ts` (`quoteOf`, `rejudged`), `book-rows.ts` (`locked`), `book-page.component.ts`, `account/wallet/lines.service.ts`, `wallet-math.ts` (`bestPriceOf`). Tests: `tests/book-paid-price.test.mjs`, `tests/book-suggestions.test.mjs`.
- **Never:** a fresh better paid price replaced by a later DraftKings-only read; a paid price shown past `PAID_FRESH_MS` or after DraftKings moved; the bettor's and the page's rules apart; a Bot's bet on Bot Picks at a price where it has no edge; Bot Picks and the game board at different prices for one side and line; a bet placed at a held price (D30).

### D80. A final is graded within minutes: the watcher dispatches the fast lane on a game gone final with open Bot bets, and the Algorithm tab shows its bets pending till then
- **Date:** 2026-10-22.
- **Owner:** "there is a delay from when a game ends and it leaves In Play, and it's not showing up in Recently Closed." Then: the pending results count in the tab's stats at once, marked, swapped for the desk's grade when it lands, the official one winning.
- **Cause:** a bet is graded only by a desk run, and nothing started one on a final: the full lane waits for news, a window or GitHub's timer (D55, D68). Measured over the 7 days to 2026-10-22, 69 games with graded Bot bets, the ledger's first `gradedAt` against ESPN's last play's wallclock: median 19 minutes, mean 27, p75 NHL 36 / NFL 34, worst 131 (plus the deploy after). The page meanwhile dropped the bet from In Play the minute ESPN said final and showed it nowhere till then.
- **Rule:**
  - **The watcher** (`scripts/desk-watch.mjs` `finalsOf`, `openOn`, `advance`'s `seen.finals`, `settle`): each poll reads ESPN's scoreboard for the Eastern days of now and `FINAL_LOOK_H` (8) ago; a game newly completed (never one called off) is checked once against its sport's ledger as committed (raw GitHub, read only then); with open Bot bets on it (a tracked row is no bet) it's pending for the fast lane on that game, `FINAL_KEEP_H` (6) at most. A fast run on a game that's over prices nothing (its props are asked only before the start, no game line ever, D68): it's the grading alone with the run's cheap refits (trust, calibration; the props' fit never refit), no paid quote, no AI step. Any run of the sport settles every final of it (each run grades all of a sport's open bets). The rates are D68's unchanged: 5 minutes a game, 30 fast dispatches a UTC day (past it, the full lane's under its 20 minutes and 40 a day), none while a desk run waits; a final waits for them.
  - **The page** (`replay-history.ts withPending`, `finalAtOf`; `desk-math.ts splitOpenOf`; the model desk component): a bet whose game ESPN shows final leaves In Play and is at the top of Recently closed, its result off the final and the box score by `desk-math.ts provisional` (the wallet's "pending settlement"), marked pending; a push to be (maybe a void) or a prop whose count isn't in the box score yet is pending with no result. A won or lost one counts at once in By Sport, By Confidence and the totals ("incl. N pending" under the record), dated for the time range by the game's end. While any is pending the page reads that sport's ledger again each minute (no-cache), and the desk's grade replaces it: a graded bet is never touched, so each bet counts once and the official result wins (a stat correction, a void).
- **Expected:** final to graded about the poll (1 minute) plus a fast run's start to its grading (fast runs measured median 4.3 minutes whole), the deploy after; longer only when a desk run is running or a rate holds it. To be measured again from the ledger after a week.
- **Gate (D22):** a tie by construction: no chance, stake, placement or grade changes; only when the grading runs and what the page shows before it. `MODEL_EPOCH` not bumped.
- **Code:** `scripts/desk-watch.mjs`; the site's `book/replay-history.ts`, `book/desk-math.ts`, `book/desk-model.ts`, `book/model-desk.component.{ts,html}`, `styles/components/model-desk.scss`. Pinned: `tests/model-desk-watch.test.mjs` (D80), `tests/book-replay-history.test.mjs` (withPending, finalAtOf, the panel transition).
- **Never:** a bet in neither In Play nor Recently closed; a pending result counted beside its graded one, or over it; a final dispatch past D68's rates; a paid quote for a final's grading.

### D82. Young players and the Bot's own lean: the NBA's young players lean on their own season (the prior's weight cut by stat and experience, walk-forward); the NFL's props against the Bot's own lean take the practice stake
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner's case of 2026-10-22: the NHL's Matthew Schaefer, a second-year defenseman, Under 2.5 shots at +122, 1u, lost on 7 shots. His projection: rate 3.12 on 9 games, recent 4.15, last season's 82 games 57% of the rate (priorW 0.572), the form weight 0, anchored toward the Book's 2.91 at pull 0.6965 to 2.96; its own pOver 55%, so the Bot leaned over and bet the under on about a point of shopped price edge).
- **How (D75's walk-forward replay, every sport's props; scratchpad `yg/`):** each player row tagged with his seasons played (`experience.mjs tagExperience`: his first season the earliest of the rows' own (after their first season), the stats site's careers (`first-seasons.json`; the NBA by ESPN id, MLB by StatsAPI id, the NHL by a unique name) and the NFL's nflverse rookie season; rookie, second year, third year, veteran or unknown). The knobs, a young player's prior weight K times km and his form weight w plus wa (`project.mjs youngKnob`), replayed on a grid (km 1, 0.5, 0.25, and 0.1, 0.05 in the NBA; wa 0, 0.2, 0.4), one replay a point (a player's projection reads only his own knobs). For each stat, experience and season S, the point with the lowest log loss on that cell's player games of the seasons before S (300 or more, else the model as it is; `young-fit.mjs`). The anchor's pull for a young player (times 0, 0.25 ... 1.25) walked the same way on the closes (`gate-history.mjs` `YOUNG_PULL`, measuring only). **The bar, set before reading:** the projections' log loss (layer a) better at z 2.5 or more overall and in each complete held-out season, the priced chance not worse, then D22 and D60.
- **Measured, the projections' log loss (layer a, every eligible player game, x 10^4, minus better, SE by game; held out NBA 2024-26 (2027's 1,170 rows +0.1 ± 2.6), NHL 2024-27, NFL 2024-26, MLB 2024-26):**

| Sport | Rookies | 2nd year | 3rd year | Veterans | All | Each season |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | -72.4 ± 10.2 | -10.8 ± 3.2 | -5.6 ± 2.7 | 0 | **-7.8 ± 1.0 (z -8.1)** | -5.0, -7.4, -11.2 |
| NHL | -18.1 ± 6.1 | +7.0 ± 5.4 | +0.3 ± 2.7 | 0 | 0.0 ± 0.3 | +0.1, -0.2, -0.1, +1.4 |
| NFL | -3.3 ± 4.6 | +81.1 ± 31.6 | -2.5 ± 6.1 | 0 | +10.1 ± 4.3 (worse) | +18.8, +5.9, -3.4 |
| MLB | +0.9 ± 1.1 | +1.1 ± 1.5 | +3.9 ± 1.2 | 0 | +0.7 ± 0.3 (worse) | |

  The walk picks a lower prior weight (km 0.5 to 0.25), never more form weight: a young player's own season to date predicts better than his past, his last five no better than it. The priced chance at the closes moves little (the anchor pulls toward the Book): NBA -0.0 ± 0.0, NHL 0.0, NFL +0.1, MLB 0.0. The young pull (the anchor by experience) picks more pull, not less (NHL 1.25 on most cells), and is noise: NHL -0.1 ± 0.1, NBA +0.2 ± 0.1 worse: held.
- **The against-own-lean bets (the replay's props, the walk's month's d from LEAN_GRID 0, 2, 5, 10 points under 50% on the bets settled before the month, D76's procedure, `lean.mjs`):** the props the Bot bets where its own chance on the side is under 50% lose against the ones with the lean everywhere (flat: NBA -3.0% on 1,055 against +2.7% on 4,214; NHL -6.3% on 336 against +7.6% on 233; NFL -2.4% on 759 against -0.1% on 1,769; MLB -6.1% on 153 against +0.8% on 339), but the walk's guard pays only in the NFL (records below: NFL +8.6u, NBA -3.1u, NHL -7.9u: its trial LOVEs' winners, MLB -2.8u). None goes against the Book's fair in the replay (props aren't shopped there, D74): the Schaefer kind, a side under the fair at the shopped price, is live only, and the 50% test catches it (his under 45%).
- **The records (the replayed record, every bet; the lines identical in each column, so the moves are the props'; LOVE the NHL's trial only):**

| Sport | Current | With young-player weighting | Plus the lean rule |
|---|---|---|---|
| NBA | 6,483, 3146-3335-2, +60.1u (+1.4%) | 6,333, 3084-3247-2, +81.5u (+1.8%) | +78.4u (-3.1u): off |
| NHL | 2,564, 1137-1386-41, -18.3u (-1.3%); LOVE 9-10 -0.1u | 2,589, 1145-1403-41, -14.1u (-1.0%); LOVE 20-13 +16.6u | -22.0u; LOVE 6-5 +2.4u: off |
| NFL | 3,577, 1701-1863-13, -47.7u (-2.5%) | 3,571, -44.7u (-2.3%) | -35.7u (+9.0u); on its own (shipped): -39.1u (+8.6u) |
| MLB | 1,691, 775-907-9, -9.9u (-1.0%) | 1,685, -18.1u (-1.9%) | -20.9u: off |

  The props are priced at the close in the replay, so D60's beat-the-close has no bets to read (no market at 52.5% anywhere, as before).
- **Shipped:** the NBA's experience weighting (`apps/nba/src/StaticData/model/young.json`, the walk's table: each season's own picks, '*' for a season it hasn't reached; set on the projections after the fit's search, never during it: `project.mjs setYoung`, `gate.mjs`, `run.mjs`), and the against-own-lean guard in the NFL (`lean.mjs LEAN_SPORTS`, `replay-record.mjs flags.leanGuard`, the live desk's d its `next`; a guarded prop a LIKE at the practice stake: gates shrink, never stop). **Held:** the weighting in the NHL (no gain), NFL and MLB (worse); the young pull; the lean guard in the NBA, NHL and MLB.
- **Schaefer under the new rules:** the NHL's second-year shots cell keeps the model as it is (the walk found no gain there), so his projection stands at 3.08, anchored 2.96. Had it halved his prior's weight his raw mean would have been about 3.18 (anchored 2.99), quartered about 3.30 (3.03), with form weight 0.4 about 3.76 (3.15): every version leans over, the anchor eats most of it. The lean guard would have staked him at 0.5u, but in the NHL it costs its LOVE winners, so it stays off there.
- **Gate (D22):** `gate.mjs --champion env:YOUNG_KW={},REPLAY_LEAN=0 --candidate worktree nba nfl nhl mlb`: SHIP (NBA beats: layer a's props -0.0010 [-0.0013, -0.0008], the props' close log loss a tie, against the Book at the closes -0.0005; NFL, NHL, MLB tie). `MODEL_EPOCH` bumped (the NBA's chances move). Recorded with `--score-only worktree --record nba nfl nhl mlb` (MMA unchanged).
- **Code:** `experience.mjs`, `young-fit.mjs`, `lean.mjs`, `project.mjs` (`youngKnob`, `setYoung`), `anchor.mjs` (`anchorEntry`'s young multiplier, none shipped), `gate.mjs`, `gate-history.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `props.mjs` (tonight's row's `yrs`), `leagues.mjs`. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D82.
- **Loops:** `young.json` is refreshed by re-running the grid (`young-fit.mjs`'s usage) when a season completes; the live desk never refits it. The lean guard's d walks with the record.
- **Never:** a season's experience knobs picked on that season or later; the table set during the fit's search; a young-weighting cell or a lean guard on in a sport where its walk's numbers lost, without the owner's say.

### D83. MMA weight misses: a weigh-in miss is a soft term of the fights model (missW), the history's table from Wikipedia, the live misses read off the weigh-in results; in at the weight its halves earn (0 today)
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner: "MMA weight misses", and whether the distance from a fighter's camp can be read).
- **The table (`weight-misses.mjs`, `apps/mma/scripts/weight-misses.json`):** every UFC event page on Wikipedia (782, the List of UFC events, fetched by Special:Export 40 a request; cached in `.cache/model/wiki-ufc`), its weigh-in notes ("X weighed in at 147.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit", "X and Y missed weight") matched to the history's bouts by the event's date (a day either side) and the names: the fighter named before the note's verb, never after "went to", "opponent" or "fined"; a surname alone only where the word before it isn't another first name. 262 pages carry notes (392 notes), 18 of them cards the history lacks; **216 bouts matched, 2012-2026** (2015 15, 2019 16, 2020 18, 2021 24, 2022 19, 2023 26, 2024 30, 2025 23, 2026 15); about 1 in 20 of the notes' misses is lost to a name spelt otherwise (Victoria/Viktoriia Dudakova).
- **Measured (the walk-forward fit by half-year, 2014 on, each half fit on the bouts before it):** the missers won 42.6% where the model said 50.5% (183 bouts with one side missing, SE 3.5 points; 2014-19 51.4% vs 50.4%, 2020-22 35.9% vs 48.4%, 2023-26 44.3% vs 52.8%), and 41.8% where the close's no-vig said 47.2% (122 with a close, flat −8.8% at the fair): the market prices about half of it. The held-out log loss: 0.63613 → 0.63610 (−0.00004 ± 0.00005 a row, a tie); on the miss bouts 0.6649 → 0.6629. A pounds-over version (missLbs, capped at 5) was no better (+0.00002).
- **Rule (`fights-model.mjs` `MISS_TERM`, `WEIGHT_MISS_FILE`, `readMisses`, `missW` in `TERMS`; `FIGHT_MISS=0` leaves it out):** missW is his miss less the other's (1, 0, −1), soft like every term (D8): the full fit's weight shrunk by its halves' evidence. **Today it fits at 0** (full −0.042, z −2.09; halves −0.016 / −0.060: the older half's z is under 1, the misses being few before 2019), so it comes in as the misses earn it; the walk's halves put it at −0.005 to −0.026 from 2023. **No look-ahead (D48):** a miss is known at the weigh-ins, the morning before (about 30 hours out); the gate's history rows and ledger rows priced more than `MISS_KNOWN_H` (24) hours before the start read the bout's chance without it (`gate-mma.mjs` `walkForward`'s `pre`, `historyRows`, `chanceOf`).
- **Live (`weigh-ins.mjs` `markMisses`; `mma-desk.mjs` `boutChance` reads `bout.missed`):** inside 48 hours of a card the desk reads MMA Fighting's Bluesky posts (the public AppView, no key; D49's reader) for "weigh-in results", fetches the linked article (free) and takes its starred weights ("Nicolle Caliari (120.5)*") and "missed weight" notes, matched to the card's bouts by name; each miss goes into the bout's chance and onto the table (not on a dry run), so the history grows. ESPN's MMA news feed doesn't carry routine misses. Checked on UFC 333 (2026-10-24): Caliari's miss read off the article of 10-23 06:34 UTC.
- **Gate (D22 + D60, `gate.mjs --candidate worktree --champion env:FIGHT_MISS=0 mma`):** SHIP, ties everywhere (bouts' log loss deciding 0.6241 → 0.6240 [−0.0004, +0.0001]; the history's results and close legs and top picks unchanged at four places: the moneyline trust is 0, so the priced chance is the Book's); the replay guard passes. Recorded with `--score-only worktree --record mma`: 1,304 bets 811-477-16 −5.81u → 1,303 811-476-16 −5.30u.
- **Not an epoch change (D4):** `MMA_START` unchanged: the live price doesn't move at today's weight (0), as a soft term's weight drift never bumps it; when the halves earn it a weight, the price moves by the term's own rule.
- **The existing terms the owner asked about (the full fit today, soft weights in standardized units):** the layoff (log days out) −0.119 (z −6.1, kept: a longer layoff loses); home (his country, a crowd in) 0, homeEmpty 0, travel (thousand km) 0 (the halves disagree in sign: in at 0); altitude (`altHome`, his own country at altitude) and the time zones (`shift`) were tested 2026-10-17 and left out (held out no better), still computed on every row. **Camp distance:** not built. ESPN's bios name a gym for 2,165 of 3,772 fighters (808 gyms) but give no place, only today's gym (fighters move camps, and the big gyms have branches), so a bout's camp at its date isn't known from a free source; his country and the travel to the venue are the terms that stand for it.
- **Code:** `weight-misses.mjs`, `weigh-ins.mjs`, `fights-model.mjs`, `gate-mma.mjs`, `mma-desk.mjs`. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D83.
- **Never:** a miss read into a price seen before the weigh-ins; the term forced on or off by hand (its weight is the soft fit's).

### D84. The NBA's win patterns (the owner's "expected wins against the ones that overperformed"): a segment of what the Bot knew earns LOVE walk-forward; directionally right 2023-24 and 2024-25, wrong in 2025-26, not beyond noise: staged, none shipped
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner: "you've been crushing NBA; take the season to date, NBA only, and see if there are any patterns between wins we expected and wins that overperformed, for confidence band ideas").
- **The bets read:** the replayed record on today's code (`REPLAY_DUMP REPLAY_ATTR=1 gate.mjs --score-only worktree nba`, D75's walk-forward props fits searched again for this code): 6,331 bets, 3085-3244-2, +83.7u (props 5,127: 2023 170, 2024 2,882, 2025 1,641, 2026 434; lines 1,204, 2020-2026); and the live ledger since 2026-10-20 (2026-27): 45 graded bets at a stake (43 props 23-20, +3.3% flat; 2 lines 2-0) and 225 tracked rows: too few to read alone, so the season to date is the replay's seasons plus these. **Expected** is a bet at a high Bot chance (55% or more), **over-performed** a win at a Bot chance under 50% (the mirror: an expected loss under 50%, an under-performer a loss at 55% or more); "beat the fair close by a lot" has no rows here: the props are priced at the close in the replay (no closing check, D70) and the lines' 402 checks are their own market.
- **Calibration (props, flat at the price):** through 2024-25 the Bot's chance holds: under 50% won 45.6% (it said 46.2%, the Book 43.0%), 50-55% 52.2% (52.4), 55-60% 57.7% (57.4), 60%+ 66.2% (61.6; flat +9.0% on 340): the expected wins come in, the top bucket more than said. 2025-26: 49.2% / 50.0% / 67.0% / 63.2% (189 / 116 / 91 / 38). The lines' chance is the Book's (the trust near 0): no pattern there (no bucket test under BH q 0.9). Live 2026-27: the Bot's under-50% props 5-13 (−37%), its 55%+ 7-2; threes unders 10-2; BetRivers' price 9-1 (noise-size).
- **The features (every replayed prop bucket against the rest, discovery 2023-2024 with BH across the 72 tests, held out 2025-2026):** rest a day (he played two days before) +5.6% against −6.1% (z 3.2, q 0.02), held +5.0% against 0.0%; four days or more −14.8% against +2.5% (q 0.10), held −3.5% against +3.4% (his first games back); minutes rising (his last five 3+ past his last twenty) +9.0% against −1.0% (q 0.19), held +8.5% against +0.9% (2025-26 alone −0.7% on 117); rebound overs (D79 holds them already) −22.0%, held −8.9%; February-March −5.0%, held 0.0%; the projection's fit piece driving it (D72) +8.8%, held +4.5% against +7.6%; home or away, the seasons played (D82's buckets), the minutes band, vacated minutes, the opponent piece, the line's size, the price, the edge's size, Kelly: none beyond noise either way. Between the over-performing wins and the low-chance losses the rest-a-day share is +6.6 points, the fit piece +4.8, rebound unders +6.1, rising minutes +3.7; four-plus days' rest −4.0.
- **The band lab (`patterns.mjs` `patternIds`, `patternWalk`, `patternBand`, `contextOf`; `band-lab.mjs` mode `pattern`; `band-variants.json` `pattern*`):** a bet's segments (its chance, edge, price, stat and side, the driving piece, his minutes and their trend, rest, home or away, seasons played, month, timing), each earned in its class (props, lines) month by month on the bets settled before the month alone: its bets made money and led the rest by 1.645 SEs on 60 (`pattern-strict` 2.33 on 100), leading in 60% of the seasons with 10 of them; LOVE one earned segment, LOCK two of different families or one past 3 SEs; the owner's 2% Kelly floor (D21, D58) under both; the caps as every rule's; LOVE 2u, LOCK 3u. Out of sample (the walk's own picks; flat ROI, LOCK / LOVE / LIKE):

| Variant | LOCK | LOVE | LIKE | 2023-24 | 2024-25 | 2025-26 | Units (current +83.7u) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pattern` | 527 +6.2% | 612 +4.2% | 5,192 +1.2% | 2.7 / 5.6 / 1.5 | 11.5 / 3.0 / −1.1 | −5.9 / −27.6 / 8.7 | +154.1u |
| `pattern-strict` | 254 +7.6% | 534 +6.8% | 5,543 +1.2% | — / 6.6 / 1.5 | 9.5 / 21.7 / −1.3 | −5.5 / −29.4 / 9.0 | +132.6u |
| `pattern-love` | — | 1,139 +5.1% | 5,192 +1.2% | 4.7 / 1.5 | 9.1 / −1.1 | −12.8 / 8.7 | +121.9u |
| `pattern-place` (D58's place on top) | — | 788 +5.5% | 5,543 +1.4% | 5.1 / 1.9 | 9.1 / −1.1 | −12.8 / 8.7 | +98.5u |
| `pattern-k1` (1% floor, a test) | — | 2,058 +5.1% | 4,273 +0.4% | 6.0 / −0.3 | 5.8 / −2.2 | −6.0 / 10.1 | +176.5u |
| `pattern-k0` (any edge, a test) | — | 3,572 +4.0% | 2,759 −0.8% | 3.2 / 0.5 | 4.9 / −7.8 | 5.9 / 7.2 | +231.8u |

  The earned segments, after earning: rest a day 2,652 bets +4.2% (3.6%, 4.2%, 8.7% by season), rising minutes 1,097 +7.4% (6.5%, 11.3%, −0.7%), the fit piece 575 +6.3%, rebound unders 306 +12.4%, veterans 538 +4.7% (2023-24 only), 60%+ chance 66 +2.5%. **Verdict:** the pyramid and LOCK > LOVE > LIKE hold on the whole history for `pattern` and `pattern-strict`, but not by season (LOCK under LOVE in 2023-24 and 2024-25's strict, and every band above LIKE loses in 2025-26, the newest, on 60 or fewer bets), and the lead is inside noise: LOVE and LOCK together +5.1% on 1,139 against LIKE's +1.2% on 5,192 (z about 1.2), with seven variants tried. The units rise mostly by staking 2u and 3u on a subset that wins 4-6%, which D58 never takes as proof. **Held: nothing ships**; the NBA keeps D70's bands and D73's trial (LOVE off). Every variant staged (`band-lab.mjs nba --variant all --write`, `apps/nba/src/StaticData/model/replay-record.staged.json`) for the Algorithm tab's Live / Staged switch. The rest-a-day and rising-minutes segments are the ones to watch: they earn by themselves in the walk the day they lead out of sample.
- **Not an epoch change (D4):** nothing on the desk moves; the lab and the staged file are research. Gate: none needed (no chance, pick or stake moves).
- **Code:** `patterns.mjs`, `band-lab.mjs`, `band-variants.json`. Tests: `tests/model-patterns.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D84.
- **Never:** a segment earned on bets settled after the bet's month; a pattern band under the owner's 2% Kelly floor outside a labelled test; a pattern band shipped without beating the band below by season.

### D86. The history audit: every sport's replayed history re-recorded with today's code weekly, nightly and on every model commit, a dated row each, and the gate's recency veto
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner: "when we do a self audit, we should be running history all over again (+ updating algorithm) with how that bot would have bet"; then "run history on EVERY change: an algo change I request, the daily small adjustments, the weekly bigger ones; history always up to date with the latest algo so we can ensure we're improving").
- **Rule (`scripts/history-audit.mjs`, `scripts/history-audit.ps1`):** in its own full clone (`C:\dev\qbr-audit`, its own `npm ci`; never a worktree of the main checkout, never its working tree), reset to origin/master each run, every sport is re-recorded with `gate.mjs --score-only worktree --record <sport>`, one sport a run so one sport's refusal never stops another's; then `selfcheck.mjs`, `tests/data.test.mjs`, and the record files (`apps/*/src/StaticData/model`, `apps/*/scripts/model`) committed ("Weekly|Nightly|Code audit: history re-recorded with today's Bot (<date>)") and pushed (`pull --rebase --autostash -X theirs`: the records are the audit's own files). Three scheduled tasks on the owner's always-on PC (`-Register`; while logged on, the owner's own git sign-in):
  - **weekly** (Mondays 3:30 AM Central, before the 8 AM self-check workflow): `gate.mjs --rebuild-cache <sport>` first (the context facts, the players' games, the injury listings, the weather; a pass again, up to three, while a source failed), then the re-record.
  - **nightly** (4:00 AM): the re-record on the cache as it is: the day's graded bets refit the trust, the bands, the own bar; skipped while another run holds the lock or after a clean run in the last 6 hours (Monday's weekly).
  - **code** (the `SeasonRanker audit check` task, every 15 minutes): a commit on origin/master touching `libs/ranker/scripts/model` (not its `.md` files) since the last audited commit, the newest 30 minutes old (`DEBOUNCE_MIN`, so a burst records once), re-records; the audit's own commits and `Model desk:` commits never trigger (no loops).
- **The walk-forward fits (D75):** not searched again by default. Each season's fit is cached under `.cache/model/fits/wf/<season>` by the fit's code (`fitcache.mjs fitCode`) and its rows, and the search is deterministic, so a fit is searched again exactly when the code or the rows it fits on changed; `--refit` (`-Refit`) forces every season's search (hours: the NBA's alone about 4.5) and buys an identical fit otherwise. The desk's saved fit isn't what the walk-forward replay prices with (the guard reads the walk's code, `inputsOf`), so the weekly's `--rebuild-cache` searches it only when its code changed.
- **The cache:** the clone's own `.cache/model` and `.cache/nflverse`, seeded from the main checkout's and topped up each run with the files it lacks (props fits only where the main's are this code's and the clone's aren't: another checkout's experiment never replaces the clone's). The main cache is only ever read.
- **The record guard (D47) stays as it is:** a sport it refuses (exit 3: incomplete inputs, or a move past 3u with the model unchanged) or that fails keeps its committed files, is logged (`<main>/.cache/history-audit.log`; the gate's output in the clone's `.cache/history-audit/<day>`) and gets one "watching" line on the Findings (`History audit: ...`, ref D86), cleared when it records again. The audit never passes `--accept-inputs`.
- **"Are we improving" (`gate.mjs historyRowOf`, `addHistoryRow`; `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/record-history.json`):** every `--record`, the audit's or an agent's, appends a dated row (`RECORD_TRIGGER`: weekly, nightly, code or manual; the commit, the epoch, the record, units, ROI, beat-the-fair-close share and mean return, each band's bets and units, the history's log loss against the Book at the close for lines and props), at most `HISTORY_MAX` 400 rows. The scorecard keeps one entry per epoch and commit (a re-record replaces it), so the trend is its own file. The Algorithm tab's foot draws each sport's units across the re-records (`record-trend.ts`, the chip's sport or all), the newest units and the change since the one before.
- **The recency veto (D22's, `gate-score.mjs recencyVeto`, `RECENT_MIN`):** a candidate ships only if, on every projection layer (games, props, MMA's bouts and totals), it is not worse beyond noise than the champion on the most recent complete season (current − 1) alone, nor on the current season alone once it holds 200 props rows or 40 game rows (paired, unweighted, clustered by day). A veto fails the sport whatever the recency-weighted seasons say, and is never D22's close-leg exception. It adds to D22; nothing in D22 is undone.
- **The self-check workflow (`model-desk-weekly.yml`) stays:** free and quick, and it refits the residual model from the desk's own Actions cache, which the PC doesn't have. The audit runs `selfcheck.mjs` too, so its push carries a self-check on the new records; the 8 AM workflow writes it again after the residual refit.
- **Not an epoch change (D4):** nothing priced moves; the audit re-records what the code already fits and never changes a rule (an algorithm change goes only through the gate, D22). Never: the live bettor (`run.mjs`) run by the audit; a paid API call; the main checkout's working tree or cache written.
- **Amended by D88 (2026-10-23):** the weekly now also self-tunes after the re-record (a knob from D88's fixed menu, at most one a sport a week, written as data to tuned.json and re-recorded); the nightly and code runs still only re-record.
- **Code:** `scripts/history-audit.mjs`, `scripts/history-audit.ps1`, `gate.mjs` (`historyRowOf`, `addHistoryRow`, `compareScores`), `gate-score.mjs` (`recencyVeto`, `sportVerdict`), `libs/ranker/src/engine/book/record-trend.ts`. Tests: `tests/history-audit.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D86.

### D87. The slate's practice picks are picked again every run and tagged on their tracked rows at no stake; the Algorithm tab's Practice and PASS rows show the tracked rows as if bet
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner: "I have 0-0 practice and pass bets... are we just not doing that anymore? I want to make sure we are practicing timing, betting strategies, etc.").
- **Why:** since D57 the desks skipped the practice selection altogether (`forced = PRACTICE_STAKED ? ... : new Map()`), so the practice candidates were never tagged, and the tab's Practice and PASS rows counted staked bets only: 0-0, though every tracked row was still graded and learned from (D1's TRACK; e.g. 225 NBA tracked rows graded since 10-20).
- **Rule:** the slate's practice candidates are picked every run whatever `PRACTICE_STAKED` (`run.mjs practicePicks`, `mma-desk.mjs mmaPracticePicks`, the replay's practice loop). While it's off (D57, unchanged) each stays the tracked row it is (intent track, units 0, its `trackUnits` as before), tagged after its stake is settled with `topN` and `practiceTracked: true` (`desk.mjs practiceTag`). `desk.mjs isPracticeTop` excludes a tagged tracked row (and its learnView), so the record, the practice line, the CLV gates, the caps, the bands and every fit read it exactly as the untagged row. The replay keeps its practice candidates apart from its bets (`tracked`): the record, bands, bars and strip unchanged; its `practice` tally (and `daily.bands.practice`) is them as if bet at 0.5u, its passes in `daily.bands.pass` at a flat unit (the `pass` tally's rule).
- **The tab (`replay-history.ts botHistoryOf`):** Practice = the replay's practice tally plus the live tracked practice candidates as if 0.5u; PASS = the replay's pass tally plus the other live tracked rows as if 1u flat; the live part after the replay's through (D81/D83's rule), graded rows only, by the time range and the sport. Both labelled "(tracked)", never in LIKE/LOVE/LOCK, the record or the Confident total. A staked bet published as a PASS (old ones) keeps its own row after them.
- **Recorded:** `gate.mjs --score-only worktree --record` (the bets unchanged, so no champion run): the replays' practice tallies now hold the tracked candidates as if 0.5u (NFL 2,283 picks 1148-1133-2 -59.6u; NBA 3,236 1713-1522-1 -29.5u; NHL 6,417 3352-2972-93 -67.4u; MLB 10,466 5593-4865-8 -214.7u; MMA 1,459 890-542-27 -32.9u: about the vig, as D57 found), the passes less them at 1u flat. Inputs complete in every sport. Only the replay files' practice and pass fields (n, practice, pass, byYear/bySeason practice, practiceDays, daily practice and pass) were taken from the run: the bets' side, and every desk file the record writes (anchor, bars, own-bar, reasons, trusted, windows, confidence), stay as shipped, so nothing the live desk reads moves (the NBA's and NHL's walk-forward props fits were searched again on today's larger history and moved their records by about 2u; that belongs to the next model record, not this one).
- **Code:** `desk.mjs` (`isPracticeTop`, `isPracticeTracked`, `practiceTag`), `run.mjs`, `mma-desk.mjs`, `replay-record.mjs` (`summarize`'s tracked, `dailyOf`'s extra), `replay-history.ts`, `model-desk.component.*`, `desk-model.ts`. Tests: `tests/model-practice-tracked.test.mjs`, `tests/book-replay-history.test.mjs` (the tracked rows as if bet), `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D57, D87.
- **Never:** a tagged tracked row staked, in the record or a total, or changing a gate, cap or fit; the Practice or PASS rows mixed into the bands.

### D88. The weekly self-tune: a fixed menu of knobs tried through the replay, at most one change a sport a week shipped as data (tuned.json), and the walk-forward props fit kept stable
- **Date:** 2026-10-23 (the owner: "the audit should adjust the algorithm based on the data, not only re-record"; and on the NFL's record swinging −9.7u from a re-searched props fit: keep the previous fit unless a new one wins by a margin).
- **The menu (`tuned.mjs KNOBS`, fixed in advance; the default is the shipped constant, pinned):**

| Knob | Decision | Default | Grid | Bounds | Sports | Kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `beatBar` | D60 beat-the-close bar | 52.5% | 50, 51.25, 52.5, 53.75, 55% | 50-56% | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `beatMin` | D60 bar's minimum checked bets | 40 | 30, 40, 60 | 30-100 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `barZ` | D51/D64 history bar's SEs | 1 | 0.5, 0.75, 1, 1.25, 1.5 | 0.5-2 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `barMin` | D51/D64 bar's minimum picks | 40 | 30, 40, 60 | 20-100 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `ownThr` | D79 own losers' shrunk return | −10% | −15, −12.5, −10, −7.5, −5% | −20 to −3% | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `ownMin` | D79 own losers' minimum bets | 10 | 5, 10, 15, 20 | 5-40 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `windowZ` | D63 betting windows' SEs | 1.645 | 1.282, 1.645, 1.96 | 1-2.576 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `windowMin` | D63 windows' minimum games | 60 | 40, 60, 90 | 30-150 | NFL NBA NHL MLB | staking |
| `trialKelly` | D73 trial LOVE's Kelly floor | 0.01 | 0.005, 0.01, 0.015, 0.02 | 0.005-0.03 | NBA NHL | staking |
| `loveUnits` | D73 trial LOVE's stake cap | 2u | 1.5, 2, 2.5 | 1-3 | NHL | staking |
| `youngShrink` | D82 young players' weights | 1 | 0.5, 0.75, 1 | 0-1 | NBA | pricing |

  MMA has none: its live desk reads no beat-the-close bar (D60: the replay alone), so a tuned bar would move the record and never a bet. Not on the menu: the anchor's pull and the trust's shrink (each is already fit walk-forward on the closes, D33/D44: a knob over a fit would tune the fit's own output), the timing hours and the props fit's own search (the stability rule below instead).
- **How a candidate is tried (`self-tune.mjs`):** one knob at a time from the current settings. A staking knob is re-staked on the one replay (`gate.mjs` `TUNE_GRID`: after the record's replay, `replayRecord` runs again in the same process under each candidate, `tuned.mjs setOverride`; about 8 seconds a candidate), so the chances, D22's layers a and b, are the champion's by construction and the replayed record decides. A pricing knob gets a full scoring in its own process (`TUNE_OVERRIDE`) and D22's verdict against the champion (`compareScores`: the projections, the priced rows' log loss, close log loss and top picks' CLV, D60's guard, D86's recency veto).
- **When it ships (all of them):** (1) more units than the current settings over the whole replay; (2) the selection-bias guard: chosen on the months before the held-out window alone (the most units there among every candidate, and past `CHOOSE_Z` 1 SE by day), then (3) a win on the held-out window (`HOLDOUT_DAYS` 56: the last 8 weeks of play, an off-season gap counted a week at most, `holdoutCut`), with only the top choice tried there (no second look); (4) not worse on the last complete season, nor beyond noise (1.96 SE) on the current season once it holds `RECENT_BETS` 100 bets (D86's veto on the record); (5) D60's guard: the bets' beat-the-fair-close share and return not down while the units are up, plain (`replayGuard`) and stake-weighted (`stakeGuard`: a re-staking keeps the same bets, so the plain guard can't see stakes moved to worse prices); (6) a pricing knob also D22's SHIP. Never: two changes in a sport within `WEEK_DAYS` (6: one a week), a knob changed within `COOLDOWN_DAYS` (28) tried again, a value off its grid or out of its bounds (the reader ignores one: `tuned.mjs knob`).
- **A change is data:** `apps/<sport>/src/StaticData/model/tuned.json` (`knobs`, a dated `changes` log with each change's evidence, `last`: the week's top candidates and why each held). The desk reads it (`desk.mjs beatOpts`, `barOpts`, `ownOpts`; `experience.mjs readYoung`), the replay too (`replay-record.mjs T()`), and the files the record writes for the desk (windows.json, trusted.json) carry the rest. The record's code fingerprint carries the knobs (`gate.mjs modelCode`'s `tuned`), so a shipped knob is the model's own change to the record guard (D47). `MODEL_EPOCH` is bumped only for a pricing knob (D4): a staking knob moves stakes on unchanged chances (D60's rule). Reversible: delete the knob from the file.
- **In the audit (`scripts/history-audit.mjs`, D86):** the weekly runs it after the re-record on the sports that recorded (`--no-tune` skips it, `--tune` runs it in another mode); a sport it changed is re-recorded (`RECORD_TRIGGER` tune; every sport for a pricing knob, the epoch moved); a re-record the guard refuses puts the sport's knobs back. A Findings line a change ("Self-tune: NFL D60 beatBar 52.5% → 53.75%: +2.1u ..., newest season +0.4u") and one a held sport (its best candidate and why it held, replaced weekly); the commit's body lists them; `record-history.json` gets the tune re-record's row.
- **The props fit's stability (`gate.mjs` `FIT_STABLE`, `FIT_STABLE_K` 1, `stableFits`, `stableVerdict`; D75's walk):** a season's fit searched again on a different row count with the same code (a rebuilt cache, more games) keeps the previous fit's settings stat by stat unless the new search's lower the held-out log loss (the stat's last 30% of the rows before the season, eligible player games, paired, clustered by game) by more than 1 standard error. The kept and taken stats are written beside the fit (`stable`). A code change searches fresh (the gate judges it, D22); `GATE_FIT_STABLE=0` off, measuring only. Measuring only: `GATE_WF_DROP_DAYS` (a snapshot n days back), `GATE_WF_ANYROWS`, `GATE_WF_NEWFROM`.
- **Measured (the stability, 2026-10-23, the NFL and MLB):** each sport's newest fits searched again on a snapshot 21 days back (`GATE_WF_DROP_DAYS=21`; the NFL's 2025 and 2026 fits on 169,779 and 189,973 rows, 0.25% fewer, the last playoff games; MLB's 2026 on 205,290 against 212,028), then the replay under the snapshot's fit (A), today's fresh search (B) and today's under the rule (S), each against B on D22's gate. The settings aren't stable: on 0.25% fewer rows every one of the NFL's 10 stats moved in both seasons (passCmp's K 16 to 8, rushYds' form, volume and trend terms, recYds' role prior, soft terms in or out), every one of MLB's 4.

| Replayed | Units | Props 2025 / 2026 | Today's settings kept | D22 against the fresh search |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFL A, the snapshot's fit | −46.19u | 1,857 bets −1.15u / 292 bets +7.01u | | |
| NFL B, today's fresh search | −48.78u | 1,848 −3.92u / 315 +7.19u | | (B against A fails D86's veto: 2026's props worse beyond noise, +0.0058 a row) |
| NFL S, the rule at 1 SE | −49.59u | 1,855 −3.63u / 292 +6.09u | 7 of 10 (2025), 8 of 10 (2026) | SHIP, better beyond noise: the props projections −0.0019 a row, 2026 −0.0058 |
| MLB A / B | −11.46u / −11.45u | 167 −10.43u, 34 −3.01u / 167 −10.43u, 44 −3.00u | | |
| MLB S, the rule at 1 SE | −10.38u | 167 −10.43u / 38 −1.93u | 3 of 4 (took k) | SHIP, a tie everywhere |

  The margin: at 2 SE the rule kept all four of MLB's stats and lost to the fresh search beyond noise (2026's props +0.001 a row, the recency veto), so it ships at 1 SE (`FIT_STABLE_K` 1; 0 SE took more and was no better: NFL −0.0019, the same units). Picked on these two pairs, so it is watched: each re-search writes its kept and taken stats beside the fit. Less variance where it is measured: the NFL's newest-season projections moved +0.0058 a row from A to B and none from A to S. The units moved about as much either way (NFL −2.6u without the rule, −3.4u with it; MLB 0 and +1.1u), inside the noise; the owner's −9.7u came from fits searched again after the weekly cache rebuild, whose earlier fits are gone, so it can't be replayed.
- **The first run (2026-10-23):** (the audit's run once now, written here after it).
- **Code:** `tuned.mjs`, `self-tune.mjs`, `desk.mjs` (`beatOpts`, `barOpts`, `ownOpts`, `walkBar`'s opts, `ownVerdict`'s), `replay-record.mjs`, `experience.mjs` (`shrinkYoung`), `gate.mjs` (`TUNE_GRID` hook, `stableFits`, `modelCode`), `gate-mma.mjs`, `fitcache.mjs` (`stable`), `scripts/history-audit.mjs` (`tuneAgain`, `tuneLines`). Tests: `tests/self-tune.test.mjs`, `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D88.
- **Never:** a code edit by the self-tune (its only writes: tuned.json, the Findings, `MODEL_EPOCH` for a pricing knob); a candidate chosen on the held-out weeks; a knob outside its bounds; the live bettor run or a paid call by it. It adds to D22, D60, D75 and D86; nothing in them is undone.

### D89. Injury news re-prices and kicks the watcher only on a player who matters: minutes, time on ice or snaps this season (last season's while his team hasn't played yet)
- **Date:** 2026-10-24 (the owner, 2026-10-23: MEM @ GS's bets were priced again three times on "MEM out list (+Isaiah Stewart)" / "MEM questionable list (-Isaiah Stewart)", a player out since the preseason with no games this season; the news on such a player wastes paid quotes and invites flips).
- **Rule (`timing.mjs` `whoMatters`, `matters`, `MATTERS`, `MATTER_GAMES`, `MATTER_QB_ATT`, `STAR`, `STAR_GAMES`, `STAR_GOALIE_STARTS`; `inputsOf`'s `om` and `qm` parts; `inputChanges`; `run.mjs inactivesNow`, `repriceOpen`; `scripts/desk-watch.mjs advance`):**
  - **Who matters**, from the props' player rows (`playerlogs.mjs`), by the average of his own last `MATTER_GAMES` (4) games of the season (the rows' latest): **NBA** 10 minutes a game or more; **NHL** 10 minutes on ice (a goalie by the same bar: his starts are 60); **NFL** 25% of his side's snaps (offense: the most snaps any of his team's players took that game; defense: `rows.defense` the same way), or 10 passes a game (the starting quarterback), or, where a game's snap counts lag (0 snaps read), `WORKED`'s touches. His team's season young (no game of it in the rows yet: the opener, the preseason): his last season's last games read the same way instead, so a regular counts on opening night. A player with no games this season on a team that has played, none in either season, or under the bar doesn't matter. The NFL's unknown (no row in either season: the offensive line, the kickers, a rookie) matters, since its rows hold only the skill players and the defenders; the NBA's and NHL's box scores hold everyone who played, so their unknown doesn't. The roles the desk tracks are inside the bars (a starting QB, a goalie who's played, D66's stars by their minutes); MLB has no injury-list parts (its starters and lineups are their own, unchanged). No rows (a failed read): everyone matters, as before.
  - **The re-price:** `inputsOf` keeps two more parts a side, `om` and `qm`, the out and questionable lists of the players who matter alone, beside the whole lists (`out`, `q`, unchanged). A bet stamped with them is priced again on those, not on the whole lists (`inputChanges` skips `out`/`q` for a side whose `om` the bet and now both have); a bet from before (no `om` stamped) is judged on the whole lists as it was priced, so the change sets off no wave of re-prices. The words are the same ("MEM out list (+A Star)").
  - **The watcher:** the desk commits `apps/<sport>/scripts/model/matters.json` (`whoMatters`' `{ sport, season, played, now, last, star, known }`, written only when it changed; never under `--dry`), the watcher reads it from the repo's raw copy on every poll that reads ESPN's injury list, and a new Out or Doubtful on a player who doesn't matter pends nothing (his status still learned, so it isn't news later). One that won't load: everyone matters. A reporter's post (HIGH) is unchanged.
  - **Kept:** the news log (D54) still logs every listed player's status change; the props still read every out player (the lists themselves and the context's fits are unchanged); only what prices an open bet again and what kicks a run are narrowed.
  - **A star coming back (the coordinator, 2026-10-24):** a player with no game this season (his team having played) still matters if last season he was a high-impact regular, by his whole last season against a higher bar (`STAR`), so a star out all of October is news on his return while a fringe one stays ignored: **NBA** 25 minutes a game, **NHL** 17 minutes on ice (a goalie: `STAR_GOALIE_STARTS` 40 starts, a game of 40 minutes or more), **NFL** 60% of his side's snaps (the starting quarterback's games, 10 passes or more, count whole), over `STAR_GAMES` of last season's games at least (NBA and NHL 20, NFL 6). Stewart: 70 games at 20.8 minutes in 2025-26, under it.
- **The trade-off, named:** a regular under the star bar back from an injury that has cost him every game of this season so far counts again from his first game back (his minutes then), not on the report before it.
- **Gate (D22):** none moves a chance, a projection or a stake; it narrows when an open bet is asked again (D30's fresh quotes, D53's re-price, D55's watcher, D68's fast lane read the same inputs). Not an epoch change (D4). **Paid calls:** none.
- **Code:** `timing.mjs`, `run.mjs`, `scripts/desk-watch.mjs`. Tests: `tests/model-decisions.test.mjs` D89 (Stewart-like no re-price and no kick, a starter both, the young-season read, a returning star counts and a Stewart-type (20.8 minutes) doesn't, the NHL's goalie starts, the NFL's and NHL's bars, a bet from before on the whole lists).
- **Never:** a list change on a player who doesn't matter pricing an open bet again or kicking a run; the news log dropping him; the whole lists dropped (the bets from before read them). It adds to D30, D53, D55, D66 and D68; nothing in them is undone.
